## 任务 13：识别评测

**目标：** 把一批 wav 分批喂给官方识别程序（先按段长从短到长排序、每批 32 段），记下每批的耗时、内存与别的程序的 CPU 占用，再按参考文本算 CER；还有“VAD + 识别”一体程序的端到端版本、标点率、各批汇总。真正起进程的是调用方给的 `run`，所以这里用假进程测。把它接到真实程序上的命令行脚本 `asr.mjs`（`prescreen`、`full`、`vad` 三个阶段）还要用到后面汇总模块里的几个函数（选决赛圈、估计预筛用时），所以放在任务 18 里写。

**文件：**

- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/asr-eval.test.mjs` — `asr-eval.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/asr-eval.mjs` — 识别评测：分批调用官方识别程序、算 CER 与速度

**接口：**

- 依赖：`cer.mjs`、`cpu.mjs`、`sherpa.mjs`
- 提供：`decodeFiles({ run, exe, candId, modelsRoot, threads, files, outDir, chunk = {}, snapshot = cpuSnapshot })`；`decodeWithVad({ run, exe, candId, modelsRoot, threads, vadModel, wav, outDir, tag, snapshot = cpuSnapshot })`；`summarizeBatches(batches)`；`scorePairs(pairs)`；`punctuationRate(texts)`
- 测试：`asr-eval.test.mjs` 9 个

- [ ] **步骤 1：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/asr-eval.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `asr-eval.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { decodeFiles, decodeWithVad, punctuationRate, scorePairs, summarizeBatches } from './asr-eval.mjs';
import { asrArgs } from './sherpa.mjs';
import { readWavInfo, writeWavMono } from './wav.mjs';

const tmp = () => fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'record-s4-asreval-'));

function makeWavs(dir, seconds) {
  return seconds.map((s, i) => {
    const f = path.join(dir, `${String(i).padStart(4, '0')}.wav`);
    writeWavMono(f, new Float32Array(Math.round(s * 16000)), 16000);
    return f;
  });
}

// 假的识别程序：每个 wav 一行 JSON（文本取自 texts[文件名]），stderr 按 rtf 编出解码用时
function fakeOffline({ texts = {}, rtf = 0.1, load = 1.5, exitCode = 0, timedOut = false, dropLast = false } = {}) {
  const calls = [];
  const run = async (req) => {
    calls.push(req);
    const files = req.args.filter((a) => a.endsWith('.wav'));
    let audio = 0;
    const lines = files.map((f) => {
      const i = readWavInfo(f);
      audio += i.frames / i.sampleRate;
      return JSON.stringify({ lang: '', emotion: '', text: texts[path.basename(f)] ?? '' });
    });
    if (dropLast) lines.pop();
    fs.writeFileSync(req.stdoutFile, lines.join('\n') + '\n');
    fs.writeFileSync(req.stderrFile, `recognizer created in ${load} s\nStarted\nElapsed seconds: ${audio * rtf} s\nReal time factor (RTF): ${audio * rtf} / ${audio} = ${rtf}\n`);
    return { exitCode, wallMs: 1000, cpuMs: 800, peakWorkingSetMb: 500 + calls.length, peakPrivateMb: 400 + calls.length, timedOut };
  };
  return { run, calls };
}

const common = { exe: 'E:/t/sherpa-onnx-offline.exe', candId: 'paraformer', modelsRoot: 'E:/m', threads: 8 };

test('逐文件识别：按批调用，结果按输入顺序拼回，每批记下耗时、内存与别人的 CPU', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const wavs = makeWavs(dir, [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]);
  const texts = Object.fromEntries(wavs.map((w, i) => [path.basename(w), `第${i}句`]));
  const { run, calls } = fakeOffline({ texts });
  const r = await decodeFiles({ run, ...common, files: wavs, outDir: path.join(dir, 'out'), chunk: { maxCount: 2 } });
  assert.deepEqual(r.texts, ['第0句', '第1句', '第2句', '第3句', '第4句']);
  assert.equal(calls.length, 3);
  assert.deepEqual(calls.map((c) => c.args.filter((a) => a.endsWith('.wav')).length), [2, 2, 1]);
  const base = asrArgs('paraformer', 'E:/m', { threads: 8 });
  assert.deepEqual(calls[0].args.slice(0, base.length), base);
  assert.equal(calls[1].stdoutFile, path.join(dir, 'out', 'batch-1.out.txt'));
  assert.deepEqual(r.batches.map((b) => b.files), [2, 2, 1]);
  assert.deepEqual(r.batches.map((b) => b.audioSec), [3, 7, 5]);
  assert.equal(r.batches[0].loadSec, 1.5);
  assert.equal(r.batches[0].wallMs, 1000);
  assert.equal(r.batches[2].peakWorkingSetMb, 503);
  assert.ok(r.batches.every((b) => b.othersLoad >= 0 && b.othersLoad <= 1));
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('别人的 CPU 占用：整机忙碌增量扣掉被测程序自己的 CPU，再除以总时间增量', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const wavs = makeWavs(dir, [1]);
  const snaps = [{ busyMs: 1000, totalMs: 10000 }, { busyMs: 3000, totalMs: 20000 }, { busyMs: 3000, totalMs: 20000 }, { busyMs: 4000, totalMs: 30000 }];
  const snapshot = () => snaps.shift();
  const { run } = fakeOffline(); // cpuMs 固定 800
  const r = await decodeFiles({ run, ...common, files: wavs, outDir: path.join(dir, 'o'), snapshot });
  assert.equal(r.batches[0].othersLoad, (2000 - 800) / 10000);
  const vadRun = async (req) => {
    fs.writeFileSync(req.stdoutFile, '');
    fs.writeFileSync(req.stderrFile, 'Elapsed seconds: 1.0 s\nReal time factor (RTF): 1.0 / 10.000 = 0.100\n');
    return { exitCode: 0, wallMs: 1, cpuMs: 200, peakWorkingSetMb: 1, peakPrivateMb: 1, timedOut: false };
  };
  const v = await decodeWithVad({ run: vadRun, exe: 'e', candId: 'sensevoice', modelsRoot: 'E:/m', threads: 1, vadModel: 'v', wav: 'a.wav', outDir: path.join(dir, 'o2'), tag: 't', snapshot });
  assert.equal(v.batch.othersLoad, (1000 - 200) / 10000);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('逐文件识别：退出码不是 0、超时、输出行数对不上都明确报错，不悄悄略过', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const wavs = makeWavs(dir, [1, 1]);
  const go = (opts) => decodeFiles({ run: fakeOffline(opts).run, ...common, files: wavs, outDir: path.join(dir, 'o'), chunk: {} });
  await assert.rejects(go({ exitCode: 3 }), /paraformer 第 1\/1 批：退出码 3，见 .*batch-0\.err\.txt/);
  await assert.rejects(go({ timedOut: true }), /paraformer 第 1\/1 批：超时被杀掉/);
  await assert.rejects(go({ dropLast: true }), /结果行数不对：期望 2，得到 1/);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('逐文件识别：没有文件时不调用程序', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const { run, calls } = fakeOffline();
  const r = await decodeFiles({ run, ...common, files: [], outDir: path.join(dir, 'o') });
  assert.deepEqual(r, { texts: [], batches: [] });
  assert.equal(calls.length, 0);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('命令行长度：程序路径与各参数都算在内，容量卡到差一个字符时少放一个文件', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const wavs = makeWavs(dir, new Array(30).fill(0.1));
  const base = asrArgs(common.candId, common.modelsRoot, { threads: common.threads });
  const l0 = common.exe.length + 3 + base.reduce((n, a) => n + a.length + 3, 0); // 除了文件以外的长度
  const cost = wavs[0].length + 3; // 每个文件等长
  const sizes = async (maxChars) => {
    const { run, calls } = fakeOffline();
    await decodeFiles({ run, ...common, files: wavs, outDir: path.join(dir, 'o'), chunk: { maxChars } });
    assert.equal(calls.flatMap((c) => c.args.filter((a) => a.endsWith('.wav'))).length, 30); // 一个不丢
    return calls.map((c) => c.args.filter((a) => a.endsWith('.wav')).length);
  };
  assert.deepEqual((await sizes(l0 + 5 * cost)).slice(0, 2), [5, 5]); // 刚好放得下 5 个
  assert.deepEqual((await sizes(l0 + 5 * cost - 1)).slice(0, 2), [4, 4]); // 差一个字符就只放 4 个
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('汇总：RTF 用用时之和除以时长之和、加载用时取平均（缺的不算）、内存取最大、别人的占用按耗时加权', () => {
  const b = (o) => ({ files: 10, loadSec: 2, elapsedSec: 5, audioSec: 50, wallMs: 1000, cpuMs: 4000, peakWorkingSetMb: 300, peakPrivateMb: 250, othersLoad: 0.1, ...o });
  const s = summarizeBatches([b({}), b({ loadSec: null, elapsedSec: 15, audioSec: 50, wallMs: 3000, peakWorkingSetMb: 900, peakPrivateMb: 700, othersLoad: 0.3 }), b({ loadSec: 4, files: 5, audioSec: 100, othersLoad: 0.2 })]);
  assert.equal(s.batches, 3);
  assert.equal(s.files, 25);
  assert.equal(s.audioSec, 200);
  assert.equal(s.elapsedSec, 25);
  assert.equal(s.rtf, 25 / 200); // 各批 RTF 的平均是 0.1667，不是这个
  assert.equal(s.loadSecMean, 3);
  assert.equal(s.wallSec, 5);
  assert.equal(s.cpuSec, 12);
  assert.equal(s.peakWorkingSetMb, 900);
  assert.equal(s.peakPrivateMb, 700);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(s.othersLoadMean - (0.1 * 1000 + 0.3 * 3000 + 0.2 * 1000) / 5000) < 1e-12);
  assert.equal(s.othersLoadMax, 0.3);
  assert.equal(summarizeBatches([b({ loadSec: null })]).loadSecMean, null);
  assert.throws(() => summarizeBatches([]), /没有任何批次/);
  assert.equal(summarizeBatches([b({ audioSec: 0 })]).rtf, Infinity);
});

test('打分：CER 合计，识别结果里的阿拉伯数字与空结果单独计数', () => {
  const r = scorePairs([
    ['你好世界', '你好世界'],
    ['二零二四', '2024年'],
    ['再见', ''],
  ]);
  assert.equal(r.cer.refLen, 10);
  assert.equal(r.cer.errors, 3); // 年（插入）一个，再见（删除）两个
  assert.equal(r.cer.rate, 0.3);
  assert.equal(r.digitsInHyp, 4);
  assert.equal(r.emptyHyps, 1);
  assert.equal(scorePairs([['甲', '１２']]).digitsInHyp, 2); // 全角数字也算
  assert.equal(scorePairs([['甲', ' ']]).emptyHyps, 1);
});

test('VAD 端到端：取出各段与文本，记下这一次的耗时与内存', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const calls = [];
  const run = async (req) => {
    calls.push(req);
    fs.writeFileSync(req.stdoutFile, '7.430 -- 12.844: 你好\n13.350 -- 20.620: 世界\n');
    fs.writeFileSync(req.stderrFile, 'Recognizer created!\nElapsed seconds: 15.271 s\nReal time factor (RTF): 15.271 / 1551.820 = 0.010\n');
    return { exitCode: 0, wallMs: 16330, cpuMs: 120000, peakWorkingSetMb: 518, peakPrivateMb: 533, timedOut: false };
  };
  const r = await decodeWithVad({ run, exe: 'E:/t/vad.exe', candId: 'sensevoice', modelsRoot: 'E:/m', threads: 8, vadModel: 'E:/v/silero_vad.onnx', wav: 'E:/d/a.wav', outDir: path.join(dir, 'o'), tag: 'R1_SPK1' });
  assert.deepEqual(r.segments, [{ start: 7.43, end: 12.844, text: '你好' }, { start: 13.35, end: 20.62, text: '世界' }]);
  assert.equal(r.batch.loadSec, null);
  assert.equal(r.batch.audioSec, 1551.82);
  assert.equal(r.batch.elapsedSec, 15.271);
  assert.equal(r.batch.peakWorkingSetMb, 518);
  assert.equal(calls[0].args.at(-1), 'E:/d/a.wav');
  assert.ok(calls[0].args.includes('--silero-vad-model=E:/v/silero_vad.onnx'));
  assert.equal(calls[0].stdoutFile, path.join(dir, 'o', 'R1_SPK1.out.txt'));
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('标点比例：标点个数除以非空白字符数（全角、半角标点都算）；没有字符时是 0', () => {
  assert.equal(punctuationRate(['你好，世界。']), 2 / 6);
  assert.equal(punctuationRate(['你好', '，']), 1 / 3); // 多条合在一起算
  assert.equal(punctuationRate(['a b, c']), 1 / 4); // 空白不计入分母
  assert.equal(punctuationRate(['你好 世界']), 0);
  assert.equal(punctuationRate(['！？']), 1);
  assert.equal(punctuationRate(['３，4.']), 2 / 4); // 全角与半角标点都算
  assert.equal(punctuationRate([]), 0);
  assert.equal(punctuationRate(['  ', '']), 0);
  assert.equal(punctuationRate(['1+1=2']), 0); // 加号与等号是符号，不是标点
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 3：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/asr-eval.mjs`**

```js
// 识别评测：把一批 wav 分批喂给官方的识别程序，记下每批的耗时、内存与别的程序的 CPU 占用，再按参考文本算 CER。
// 真正起进程的是调用方给的 run（生产里是 measureAsync 的包装），所以这里可以用假进程测。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { cerTotal } from './cer.mjs';
import { cpuSnapshot, othersLoad } from './cpu.mjs';
import { asrArgs, chunkFiles, parseAsrStderr, parseAsrStdout, parseVadAsrStdout, vadAsrArgs } from './sherpa.mjs';

/** 命令行里除了文件以外的长度：程序路径加各参数，每项按 长度 + 3（空格与一对引号）算 */
function baseLength(exe, args) {
  return exe.length + 3 + args.reduce((n, a) => n + a.length + 3, 0);
}

async function runChecked(run, req, what, snapshot) {
  const before = snapshot();
  const m = await run(req);
  const after = snapshot();
  if (m.timedOut) throw new Error(`${what}：超时被杀掉`);
  if (m.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(`${what}：退出码 ${m.exitCode}，见 ${req.stderrFile}`);
  return { m, others: othersLoad(before, after, m.cpuMs) };
}

function batchRecord(m, others, parsed, files) {
  return { files, loadSec: parsed.loadSec, elapsedSec: parsed.elapsedSec, audioSec: parsed.audioSec, wallMs: m.wallMs, cpuMs: m.cpuMs, peakWorkingSetMb: m.peakWorkingSetMb, peakPrivateMb: m.peakPrivateMb, othersLoad: others };
}

/**
 * 逐文件识别：按命令行长度分批，每批一次调用；返回 { texts（与 files 同序）, batches }。
 * run：async ({ exe, args, stdoutFile, stderrFile }) => 测量结果（见 measure.mjs）；snapshot：取整机 CPU 快照的函数（测试时换成假的）。
 */
export async function decodeFiles({ run, exe, candId, modelsRoot, threads, files, outDir, chunk = {}, snapshot = cpuSnapshot }) {
  const base = asrArgs(candId, modelsRoot, { threads });
  fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
  const texts = [];
  const batches = [];
  const parts = chunkFiles(files, { baseLen: baseLength(exe, base), ...chunk });
  for (const [i, part] of parts.entries()) {
    const stdoutFile = path.join(outDir, `batch-${i}.out.txt`);
    const stderrFile = path.join(outDir, `batch-${i}.err.txt`);
    const { m, others } = await runChecked(run, { exe, args: [...base, ...part], stdoutFile, stderrFile }, `${candId} 第 ${i + 1}/${parts.length} 批`, snapshot);
    texts.push(...parseAsrStdout(fs.readFileSync(stdoutFile, 'utf8'), part.length));
    batches.push(batchRecord(m, others, parseAsrStderr(fs.readFileSync(stderrFile, 'utf8')), part.length));
  }
  return { texts, batches };
}

/** VAD + 识别一体：对一整条录音，返回 { segments: [{start,end,text}], batch } */
export async function decodeWithVad({ run, exe, candId, modelsRoot, threads, vadModel, wav, outDir, tag, snapshot = cpuSnapshot }) {
  const args = [...vadAsrArgs(candId, modelsRoot, { threads, vadModel }), wav];
  fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
  const stdoutFile = path.join(outDir, `${tag}.out.txt`);
  const stderrFile = path.join(outDir, `${tag}.err.txt`);
  const { m, others } = await runChecked(run, { exe, args, stdoutFile, stderrFile }, `${candId} VAD 端到端 ${tag}`, snapshot);
  const segments = parseVadAsrStdout(fs.readFileSync(stdoutFile, 'utf8'));
  return { segments, batch: batchRecord(m, others, parseAsrStderr(fs.readFileSync(stderrFile, 'utf8')), 1) };
}

const sum = (xs) => xs.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);

/**
 * 汇总各批：RTF 用“解码用时之和 ÷ 音频时长之和”（程序打印的解码用时不含模型加载）；加载用时取平均；内存取最大；
 * 别的程序的 CPU 占用按各批耗时加权平均，并给出最大值。
 */
export function summarizeBatches(batches) {
  if (batches.length === 0) throw new Error('没有任何批次可汇总');
  const elapsedSec = sum(batches.map((b) => b.elapsedSec));
  const audioSec = sum(batches.map((b) => b.audioSec));
  const loads = batches.map((b) => b.loadSec).filter((x) => x !== null);
  const wall = sum(batches.map((b) => b.wallMs));
  return {
    batches: batches.length,
    files: sum(batches.map((b) => b.files)),
    audioSec,
    elapsedSec,
    rtf: audioSec > 0 ? elapsedSec / audioSec : Infinity,
    loadSecMean: loads.length ? sum(loads) / loads.length : null,
    wallSec: wall / 1000,
    cpuSec: sum(batches.map((b) => b.cpuMs)) / 1000,
    peakWorkingSetMb: Math.max(...batches.map((b) => b.peakWorkingSetMb)),
    peakPrivateMb: Math.max(...batches.map((b) => b.peakPrivateMb)),
    othersLoadMean: wall > 0 ? sum(batches.map((b) => b.othersLoad * b.wallMs)) / wall : 0,
    othersLoadMax: Math.max(...batches.map((b) => b.othersLoad)),
  };
}

/** 对一批 [参考, 识别结果] 打分：CER（合计）与识别结果里阿拉伯数字的个数（参考里一个都没有，用来看逆文本规范化的影响） */
export function scorePairs(pairs) {
  const digits = (s) => (s.normalize('NFKC').match(/[0-9]/g) ?? []).length;
  return { cer: cerTotal(pairs), digitsInHyp: pairs.reduce((n, [, h]) => n + digits(h), 0), emptyHyps: pairs.filter(([, h]) => h.trim() === '').length };
}

/** 识别结果里标点占的比例（标点个数 ÷ 非空白字符数）；没有任何字符时是 0。用来判断这个识别器自己会不会输出标点。 */
export function punctuationRate(texts) {
  let punct = 0;
  let all = 0;
  for (const t of texts) {
    for (const ch of t) {
      if (/\s/u.test(ch)) continue;
      all++;
      if (/\p{P}/u.test(ch)) punct++;
    }
  }
  return all === 0 ? 0 : punct / all;
}
```

## 任务 14：说话人分离评测

**目标：** 声纹模型 × 聚类阈值（或已知人数）的任务清单、跑一次官方分离程序并打分、把各场结果汇总成 `decide.mjs` 要的曲线。真正起进程的同样是调用方给的 `run`，用假进程测。`diar.mjs` 是六个阶段（`sweep`、`status`、`confirm`、`far`、`rotations`、`timing`）的命令行脚本。

**文件：**

- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/diar-eval.test.mjs` — `diar-eval.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/diar-eval.mjs` — 分离评测：任务清单、跑一次并打分、汇总成曲线
- 创建：`scripts/s4/diar.mjs` — 命令行：说话人分离评测（sweep、status、confirm、far、rotations、timing）

**接口：**

- 依赖：`der.mjs`、`sherpa.mjs`
- 提供：`SEGMENTATION_FILE`；`EMBEDDINGS`；`THRESHOLDS`；`jobKey(mixId, embeddingId, { threshold, clusters })`；`diarJobs({ mixes, embeddings = EMBEDDINGS, thresholds = THRESHOLDS, knownCount = true })`；`speakerCounts(ref, hyp)`；`runDiarJob({ run, exe, segmentationModel, embeddingDir, job, outDir, threads = 1, priority = 'BelowNormal' })`；`aggregate(results)`；`curves(results, { sessions })`
- 测试：`diar-eval.test.mjs` 10 个

- [ ] **步骤 1：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/diar-eval.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `diar-eval.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { aggregate, curves, diarJobs, EMBEDDINGS, jobKey, runDiarJob, SEGMENTATION_FILE, speakerCounts, THRESHOLDS } from './diar-eval.mjs';

const tmp = () => fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'record-s4-diareval-'));

// 参考：两个人各 10 秒，轮流说；音轨共 30 秒
const REF = [
  { start: 0, end: 10, speaker: 'SPK1' },
  { start: 10, end: 20, speaker: 'SPK2' },
  { start: 20, end: 30, speaker: 'SPK1' },
];
const mix = (id = 'R1_ME1') => ({ id, wav: `E:/work/${id}.wav`, ref: REF, durationSec: 30, actualSpeakers: 2 });

function fakeDiar({ stdout, exitCode = 0, timedOut = false, duration = 30, elapsed = 6 }) {
  const calls = [];
  const run = async (req) => {
    calls.push(req);
    fs.writeFileSync(req.stdoutFile, `Started\n${stdout}\n`);
    fs.writeFileSync(req.stderrFile, `progress 100%\nDuration : ${duration} s\nElapsed seconds: ${elapsed} s\nReal time factor (RTF): ${elapsed} / ${duration} = ${elapsed / duration}\n`);
    return { exitCode, timedOut, wallMs: 7000, cpuMs: 6500, peakWorkingSetMb: 210, peakPrivateMb: 190 };
  };
  return { run, calls };
}

const base = { exe: 'E:/t/diar.exe', segmentationModel: 'E:/m/seg.onnx', embeddingDir: 'E:/d' };

test('候选与范围：五个声纹模型、阈值 0.3 到 1.0 共八档；分割模型文件', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(EMBEDDINGS.map((e) => e.id), ['campplus', 'eres2net-base', 'eres2net-large', 'eres2netv2', 'wespeaker-resnet34']);
  assert.ok(EMBEDDINGS.every((e) => e.file.endsWith('.onnx')));
  assert.equal(new Set(EMBEDDINGS.map((e) => e.file)).size, 5);
  assert.deepEqual(THRESHOLDS, [0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0]);
  assert.equal(SEGMENTATION_FILE, 'sherpa-onnx-pyannote-segmentation-3-0/model.onnx');
});

test('任务键：阈值保留一位小数，已知人数用 k', () => {
  assert.equal(jobKey('R1_ME1', 'campplus', { threshold: 0.5 }), 'R1_ME1__campplus__t0.50');
  assert.equal(jobKey('R1_ME1', 'campplus', { threshold: 0.30000000000000004 }), 'R1_ME1__campplus__t0.30');
  assert.equal(jobKey('R1_ME1', 'campplus', { threshold: 0.55 }), 'R1_ME1__campplus__t0.55'); // 区间中点不在 0.1 的格子上，键不能和 0.6 混
  assert.equal(jobKey('R1_ME1', 'eres2netv2', { clusters: 3 }), 'R1_ME1__eres2netv2__k3');
});

test('任务清单：每个音轨 × 每个模型 × （每个阈值 + 已知人数）；已知人数用参考里的人数；键都不重复', () => {
  const mixes = [mix('A'), { ...mix('B'), actualSpeakers: 3 }];
  const jobs = diarJobs({ mixes, embeddings: EMBEDDINGS.slice(0, 2), thresholds: [0.4, 0.6, 0.8] });
  assert.equal(jobs.length, 2 * 2 * (3 + 1));
  assert.equal(new Set(jobs.map((j) => j.key)).size, jobs.length);
  const known = jobs.filter((j) => j.clusters !== undefined);
  assert.equal(known.length, 4);
  assert.deepEqual(known.filter((j) => j.mix.id === 'B').map((j) => j.clusters), [3, 3]);
  assert.ok(jobs.filter((j) => j.clusters === undefined).every((j) => typeof j.threshold === 'number' && j.key.includes('__t')));
  assert.equal(diarJobs({ mixes: [mix()], embeddings: EMBEDDINGS.slice(0, 1), thresholds: [0.5], knownCount: false }).length, 1);
  assert.equal(diarJobs({ mixes: [mix()] }).length, 5 * (8 + 1)); // 默认：五个模型、八档阈值
});

test('说话人数：出现过的人数；3 秒以下的碎片另外给一个数', () => {
  const hyp = [{ start: 0, end: 10, speaker: 'speaker_00' }, { start: 10, end: 20, speaker: 'speaker_01' }, { start: 20, end: 21, speaker: 'speaker_02' }];
  assert.deepEqual(speakerCounts(REF, hyp), { actual: 2, estimated: 3, estimated3s: 2 });
});

test('跑一个任务：命令行、优先级、解析输出、按参考打分（说话人编号对不上也能映射）', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const { run, calls } = fakeDiar({ stdout: '0.000 -- 10.000 speaker_01\n10.000 -- 20.000 speaker_00\n20.000 -- 30.000 speaker_01' });
  const job = diarJobs({ mixes: [mix('R1_ME1')], embeddings: [EMBEDDINGS[0]], thresholds: [0.5], knownCount: false })[0];
  const r = await runDiarJob({ run, ...base, job, outDir: dir });
  assert.deepEqual(calls[0].args, [
    '--segmentation.pyannote-model=E:/m/seg.onnx',
    `--embedding.model=${path.join('E:/d', EMBEDDINGS[0].file)}`,
    '--segmentation.num-threads=1',
    '--embedding.num-threads=1',
    '--clustering.cluster-threshold=0.5',
    'E:/work/R1_ME1.wav',
  ]);
  assert.equal(calls[0].priority, 'BelowNormal');
  assert.equal(calls[0].stdoutFile, path.join(dir, 'R1_ME1__campplus__t0.50.out.txt'));
  assert.equal(r.der, 0);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.total - 28.5) < 1e-6, `参与评分的说话量 ${r.total}`); // 30 秒扣掉各边界前后 0.25 秒的容差区共 1.5 秒
  assert.equal(r.actualSpeakers, 2);
  assert.equal(r.estimatedSpeakers, 2);
  assert.equal(r.threshold, 0.5);
  assert.equal(r.clusters, null);
  assert.equal(r.embedding, 'campplus');
  assert.equal(r.mixId, 'R1_ME1');
  assert.equal(r.key, 'R1_ME1__campplus__t0.50');
  assert.equal(r.durationSec, 30);
  assert.equal(r.elapsedSec, 6);
  assert.equal(r.rtf, 0.2);
  assert.equal(r.wallMs, 7000);
  assert.equal(r.cpuMs, 6500);
  assert.equal(r.peakWorkingSetMb, 210);
  assert.deepEqual(r.hyp, [[0, 10, 'speaker_01'], [10, 20, 'speaker_00'], [20, 30, 'speaker_01']]);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('跑一个任务：已知人数的命令行；漏掉一个人时 DER 里是漏检；人数多出的碎片计入估计人数', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const { run, calls } = fakeDiar({ stdout: '0.000 -- 10.000 speaker_00\n20.000 -- 30.000 speaker_00\n12.000 -- 13.000 speaker_01' });
  const job = diarJobs({ mixes: [mix()], embeddings: [EMBEDDINGS[1]], thresholds: [], knownCount: true })[0];
  const r = await runDiarJob({ run, ...base, job, outDir: dir, threads: 4, priority: 'Idle' });
  assert.ok(calls[0].args.includes('--clustering.num-clusters=2'));
  assert.ok(!calls[0].args.some((a) => a.startsWith('--clustering.cluster-threshold')));
  assert.ok(calls[0].args.includes('--segmentation.num-threads=4'));
  assert.equal(calls[0].priority, 'Idle');
  assert.equal(r.clusters, 2);
  assert.equal(r.threshold, null);
  assert.equal(r.estimatedSpeakers, 2);
  assert.equal(r.estimatedSpeakers3s, 1);
  // SPK2 的 10～20 秒里只有 12～13 秒被碎片对上；其余扣掉两头的容差区后是 1.75 + 6.75 = 8.5 秒漏检，没有误检与混淆
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.miss - 8.5) < 1e-6, `漏检 ${r.miss} 秒`);
  assert.ok(r.fa < 1e-9 && r.conf < 1e-9);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.der - 8.5 / 28.5) < 1e-9, `DER ${r.der}`);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('跑一个任务：多出一个碎片说话人时估计人数比实际多一；评分只算到音轨末尾，之后的输出不计误检', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const { run } = fakeDiar({ stdout: '0.000 -- 10.000 speaker_00\n10.000 -- 20.000 speaker_01\n20.000 -- 30.000 speaker_00\n25.000 -- 26.000 speaker_02\n30.500 -- 33.000 speaker_00' });
  const job = diarJobs({ mixes: [mix()], embeddings: [EMBEDDINGS[0]], thresholds: [0.5], knownCount: false })[0];
  const r = await runDiarJob({ run, ...base, job, outDir: dir });
  assert.equal(r.actualSpeakers, 2);
  assert.equal(r.estimatedSpeakers, 3);
  assert.equal(r.estimatedSpeakers3s, 2);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(r.fa - 1) < 1e-6, `误检 ${r.fa} 秒：只有 25～26 秒的碎片，30 秒之后的不计`);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('跑一个任务：退出码不是 0 或超时都明确报错', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const job = diarJobs({ mixes: [mix()], embeddings: [EMBEDDINGS[0]], thresholds: [0.5], knownCount: false })[0];
  await assert.rejects(runDiarJob({ run: fakeDiar({ stdout: '', exitCode: 2 }).run, ...base, job, outDir: dir }), /R1_ME1__campplus__t0\.50：退出码 2，见 .*\.err\.txt/);
  await assert.rejects(runDiarJob({ run: fakeDiar({ stdout: '', timedOut: true }).run, ...base, job, outDir: dir }), /超时被杀掉/);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

const res = (o) => ({ total: 100, miss: 5, fa: 3, conf: 2, actualSpeakers: 3, estimatedSpeakers: 3, ...o });

test('汇总：DER 按总量合计（不是各场比例的平均），人数相差不超过 1 的场次数', () => {
  const a = aggregate([res({ total: 100, miss: 10, fa: 0, conf: 0 }), res({ total: 300, miss: 0, fa: 30, conf: 0, estimatedSpeakers: 5 }), res({ total: 100, miss: 0, fa: 0, conf: 20, estimatedSpeakers: 2 })]);
  assert.equal(a.sessions, 3);
  assert.equal(a.total, 500);
  assert.equal(a.der, 60 / 500); // 各场比例的平均是 0.1，不是这个
  assert.equal(a.miss, 10 / 500);
  assert.equal(a.fa, 30 / 500);
  assert.equal(a.conf, 20 / 500);
  assert.equal(a.countHits, 2); // 差 0、2、1：差 2 的那场不算
  assert.ok(Math.abs(a.countMeanAbsError - 1) < 1e-12);
  assert.throws(() => aggregate([]), /没有结果/);
});

test('曲线：按模型分组、阈值升序、已知人数单独放；设置缺了某场的结果就报错', () => {
  const r = (embedding, threshold, extra = {}) => ({ ...res(extra), embedding, threshold, clusters: null });
  const known = (embedding) => ({ ...res({}), embedding, threshold: null, clusters: 3 });
  const all = [];
  for (const s of [1, 2]) {
    all.push(r('a', 0.7, { miss: 7 }), r('a', 0.3, { miss: 3 }), known('a'), r('b', 0.5, { miss: 1 }));
  }
  const c = curves(all, { sessions: 2 });
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(c), ['a', 'b']);
  assert.deepEqual(c.a.points.map((p) => p.threshold), [0.3, 0.7]);
  assert.equal(c.a.points[0].der, (3 + 3 + 2) / 100);
  assert.equal(c.a.points[1].sessions, 2);
  assert.equal(c.a.knownCount.der, 0.1);
  assert.equal(c.b.knownCount, null);
  assert.deepEqual(c.b.points.map((p) => p.threshold), [0.5]);
  assert.throws(() => curves(all.slice(1), { sessions: 2 }), /只有 1\/2 场/);
  assert.throws(() => curves([...all, r('a', 0.3, {})], { sessions: 2 }), /只有 3\/2 场/);
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 3：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/diar-eval.mjs`**

```js
// 说话人分离评测：声纹模型 × 聚类阈值（或已知人数）的任务清单、跑一次官方分离程序并打分、把各场结果汇总成 decide.mjs 要的曲线。
// 真正起进程的是调用方给的 run（生产里是 measureAsync 的包装），所以这里可以用假进程测。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { countSpeakers, scoreDer } from './der.mjs';
import { diarArgs, parseDiarStderr, parseDiarStdout } from './sherpa.mjs';

export const SEGMENTATION_FILE = 'sherpa-onnx-pyannote-segmentation-3-0/model.onnx';

/** 声纹模型候选：编号与文件名（在 downloads 目录里） */
export const EMBEDDINGS = Object.freeze([
  { id: 'campplus', file: '3dspeaker_speech_campplus_sv_zh-cn_16k-common.onnx' },
  { id: 'eres2net-base', file: '3dspeaker_speech_eres2net_base_sv_zh-cn_3dspeaker_16k.onnx' },
  { id: 'eres2net-large', file: '3dspeaker_speech_eres2net_large_sv_zh-cn_3dspeaker_16k.onnx' },
  { id: 'eres2netv2', file: '3dspeaker_speech_eres2netv2_sv_zh-cn_16k-common.onnx' },
  { id: 'wespeaker-resnet34', file: 'wespeaker_zh_cnceleb_resnet34.onnx' },
]);

/**
 * 聚类阈值扫描范围：0.3～1.0，步长 0.1。设计文档写的是 0.3～0.9；真数据上冒烟时（前 400 秒，一场会议）
 * 多数声纹模型的 DER 最低点落在 0.9 这个边上，所以把 1.0 也扫了，免得最低点被范围卡住。
 */
export const THRESHOLDS = Object.freeze([0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.8, 0.9, 1.0]);

/** 任务键：同一场、同一模型、同一设置得到同一个键，也用作结果文件名 */
export function jobKey(mixId, embeddingId, { threshold, clusters }) {
  return `${mixId}__${embeddingId}__${clusters !== undefined ? `k${clusters}` : `t${threshold.toFixed(2)}`}`;
}

/**
 * 任务清单：每个会议音轨 × 每个声纹模型 × （每个阈值 + 已知人数）。
 * mix：{ id, wav, ref, durationSec, actualSpeakers }；已知人数取参考里的说话人数。
 */
export function diarJobs({ mixes, embeddings = EMBEDDINGS, thresholds = THRESHOLDS, knownCount = true }) {
  const jobs = [];
  for (const mix of mixes) {
    for (const embedding of embeddings) {
      for (const threshold of thresholds) jobs.push({ key: jobKey(mix.id, embedding.id, { threshold }), mix, embedding, threshold });
      if (knownCount) jobs.push({ key: jobKey(mix.id, embedding.id, { clusters: mix.actualSpeakers }), mix, embedding, clusters: mix.actualSpeakers });
    }
  }
  return jobs;
}

/** 假设里估计的人数与参考的人数：都按“出现过的说话人”数 */
export function speakerCounts(ref, hyp) {
  return { actual: countSpeakers(ref), estimated: countSpeakers(hyp), estimated3s: countSpeakers(hyp, 3) };
}

/**
 * 跑一个任务：调分离程序（单线程，后台优先级），解析输出，按参考打分（容差 0.25 秒，计重叠）。
 * run：async ({ exe, args, stdoutFile, stderrFile, priority }) => 测量结果（见 measure.mjs）。
 */
export async function runDiarJob({ run, exe, segmentationModel, embeddingDir, job, outDir, threads = 1, priority = 'BelowNormal' }) {
  const { mix, embedding } = job;
  const args = [...diarArgs({ segmentationModel, embeddingModel: path.join(embeddingDir, embedding.file), threshold: job.threshold, clusters: job.clusters, threads }), mix.wav];
  fs.mkdirSync(outDir, { recursive: true });
  const stdoutFile = path.join(outDir, `${job.key}.out.txt`);
  const stderrFile = path.join(outDir, `${job.key}.err.txt`);
  const m = await run({ exe, args, stdoutFile, stderrFile, priority });
  if (m.timedOut) throw new Error(`${job.key}：超时被杀掉`);
  if (m.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(`${job.key}：退出码 ${m.exitCode}，见 ${stderrFile}`);
  const hyp = parseDiarStdout(fs.readFileSync(stdoutFile, 'utf8'));
  const e = parseDiarStderr(fs.readFileSync(stderrFile, 'utf8'));
  const score = scoreDer(mix.ref, hyp, { collar: 0.25, duration: mix.durationSec });
  const counts = speakerCounts(mix.ref, hyp);
  return {
    key: job.key,
    mixId: mix.id,
    embedding: embedding.id,
    threshold: job.threshold ?? null,
    clusters: job.clusters ?? null,
    durationSec: e.durationSec,
    elapsedSec: e.elapsedSec,
    rtf: e.rtf,
    wallMs: m.wallMs,
    cpuMs: m.cpuMs,
    peakWorkingSetMb: m.peakWorkingSetMb,
    total: score.total,
    miss: score.miss,
    fa: score.fa,
    conf: score.conf,
    der: score.der,
    actualSpeakers: counts.actual,
    estimatedSpeakers: counts.estimated,
    estimatedSpeakers3s: counts.estimated3s,
    hyp: hyp.map((s) => [s.start, s.end, s.speaker]),
  };
}

/**
 * 把同一个“声纹模型 + 设置”在各场上的结果合成一个点：DER 按总量合计（总误差 ÷ 总参考说话量，不是各场比例的平均），
 * 人数相差不超过 1 的场次数。
 */
export function aggregate(results) {
  if (results.length === 0) throw new Error('没有结果可汇总');
  const sum = (k) => results.reduce((a, r) => a + r[k], 0);
  const total = sum('total');
  const errors = sum('miss') + sum('fa') + sum('conf');
  return {
    sessions: results.length,
    total,
    miss: sum('miss') / total,
    fa: sum('fa') / total,
    conf: sum('conf') / total,
    der: errors / total,
    countHits: results.filter((r) => Math.abs(r.estimatedSpeakers - r.actualSpeakers) <= 1).length,
    countMeanAbsError: results.reduce((a, r) => a + Math.abs(r.estimatedSpeakers - r.actualSpeakers), 0) / results.length,
  };
}

/**
 * 全部结果 → { 声纹模型: { points: [{ threshold, ...aggregate }], knownCount: aggregate|null } }，points 按阈值升序。
 * 每个设置必须在所有场次上都有结果（缺了就报错，不拿残缺的数字去比）。
 */
export function curves(results, { sessions }) {
  const byModel = new Map();
  for (const r of results) {
    if (!byModel.has(r.embedding)) byModel.set(r.embedding, new Map());
    const cfg = r.clusters !== null ? 'k' : `t${r.threshold.toFixed(2)}`;
    const m = byModel.get(r.embedding);
    if (!m.has(cfg)) m.set(cfg, []);
    m.get(cfg).push(r);
  }
  const out = {};
  for (const [emb, cfgs] of byModel) {
    const points = [];
    let knownCount = null;
    for (const [cfg, list] of cfgs) {
      if (list.length !== sessions) throw new Error(`${emb} 的设置 ${cfg} 只有 ${list.length}/${sessions} 场的结果`);
      const a = aggregate(list);
      if (cfg === 'k') knownCount = a;
      else points.push({ threshold: list[0].threshold, ...a });
    }
    points.sort((a, b) => a.threshold - b.threshold);
    out[emb] = { points, knownCount };
  }
  return out;
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 4：运行测试，确认通过**

```powershell
node --test scripts/s4/lib/diar-eval.test.mjs
```

预期：输出末尾的统计里有 `# pass 10` 和 `# fail 0`（其余的 `# …` 行不用管）。

- [ ] **步骤 5：创建 `scripts/s4/diar.mjs`**

```js
// 说话人分离评测。结果写在 <S4 目录>\results\diar\<阶段>\<任务键>.json，已有的结果不重跑（加 --force 重跑）。
//   node scripts/s4/diar.mjs sweep --home <目录> [--workers N]
//       8 场会议的“系统声音”（“我”之外的人各自静音掉没说话的部分再相加）× 5 个声纹模型 ×（阈值 0.3～1.0 步长 0.1 + 已知人数）= 360 个任务，
//       每个任务单线程、后台优先级，同时跑 N 个（默认 CPU 核数减 6）。
//   node scripts/s4/diar.mjs status --home <目录>
//       扫描完成后：各声纹模型的最低 DER 排名、胜出模型的阈值区间与中点（按 decide.mjs 里写死的规则）。
//   node scripts/s4/diar.mjs confirm --home <目录>
//       中点不在已测的阈值上时，在中点把 8 场再测一遍（核对门槛用）。
//   node scripts/s4/diar.mjs far --home <目录>
//       远场：8 场阵列第 1 通道（含所有参会者），用胜出的模型与阈值。
//   node scripts/s4/diar.mjs rotations --home <目录>
//       补充（不是门槛）：“我”依次换成每位参会者，其余 17 种组合用胜出的模型与阈值再测。
//   node scripts/s4/diar.mjs timing --home <目录>
//       计时：三场（头、中、尾）的系统声音，用胜出的模型与阈值，8 线程、正常优先级、一个一个地跑（这时别的程序不要占 CPU）。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { cpuSnapshot, othersLoad } from './lib/cpu.mjs';
import { log, parseArgs, pickThree, runPool } from './lib/cli.mjs';
import { pickEmbedding } from './lib/decide.mjs';
import { curves, diarJobs, EMBEDDINGS, runDiarJob, SEGMENTATION_FILE } from './lib/diar-eval.mjs';
import { loadSessions } from './lib/dataset.mjs';
import { homeFrom, readJson, s4Paths, writeJsonAtomic } from './lib/home.mjs';
import { measureAsync } from './lib/measure.mjs';
import { buildFarMix, buildMix } from './lib/prep.mjs';

const { positional, flags } = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
const stage = positional[0];
const paths = s4Paths(homeFrom(flags.home));
const sessions = loadSessions(paths.dataset);
const run = (req) => measureAsync({ priority: 'Normal', ...req });
const force = flags.force === true;
const workers = Number(flags.workers ?? Math.max(1, os.cpus().length - 6));
const exe = paths.exe('sherpa-onnx-offline-speaker-diarization');
const segmentationModel = path.join(paths.models, SEGMENTATION_FILE);

const resultDir = (sub) => path.join(paths.results, 'diar', sub);
const resultFile = (sub, key) => path.join(resultDir(sub), `${key}.json`);

function readAll(sub) {
  const dir = resultDir(sub);
  return fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.json')).sort().map((f) => readJson(path.join(dir, f))) : [];
}

const primaryMixes = () => sessions.map((s) => buildMix({ work: paths.work, session: s, me: s.me }));

async function runJobs(jobs, sub, { threads = 1, priority = 'BelowNormal' } = {}) {
  const todo = jobs.filter((j) => force || !fs.existsSync(resultFile(sub, j.key)));
  log(`${sub}：共 ${jobs.length} 个任务，已有 ${jobs.length - todo.length} 个，要跑 ${todo.length} 个，同时 ${workers} 个`);
  let done = 0;
  await runPool(todo, workers, async (job) => {
    const r = await runDiarJob({ run, exe, segmentationModel, embeddingDir: paths.downloads, job, outDir: path.join(paths.work, 'diar-out', sub), threads, priority });
    writeJsonAtomic(resultFile(sub, job.key), r);
    done++;
    log(`  (${done}/${todo.length}) ${job.key}  DER ${(r.der * 100).toFixed(1)}%  人数 ${r.estimatedSpeakers}/${r.actualSpeakers}  ${(r.wallMs / 1000).toFixed(0)} 秒`);
  });
}

/** 扫描结果 → 各声纹模型的曲线与胜出者（任何一个设置缺了哪一场都会报错，不拿残缺的数字去比） */
function winner() {
  const results = readAll('sweep');
  if (results.length === 0) throw new Error('还没有扫描结果，先跑 sweep');
  const c = curves(results, { sessions: sessions.length });
  const models = Object.entries(c).map(([name, v]) => ({ name, points: v.points.map(({ threshold, der, countHits }) => ({ threshold, der, countHits })) }));
  return { pick: pickEmbedding(models), curves: c };
}

const embeddingOf = (pick) => EMBEDDINGS.find((e) => e.id === pick.model);

async function sweep() {
  await runJobs(diarJobs({ mixes: primaryMixes() }), 'sweep');
}

function status() {
  const { pick, curves: c } = winner();
  log('各声纹模型的最低 DER（越低越好）：');
  for (const r of pick.ranking) log(`  ${r.name.padEnd(20)} ${(r.minDer * 100).toFixed(2)}%   已知人数时 ${(c[r.name].knownCount.der * 100).toFixed(2)}%`);
  log(`胜出：${pick.model}，最低 DER ${(pick.minDer * 100).toFixed(2)}%，距最低点 1 个百分点内的阈值区间 ${pick.band.lo}～${pick.band.hi}，取中点 ${pick.threshold}`);
  log(pick.needsConfirm ? '中点不在已测的阈值上，要跑 confirm' : `中点就是已测的阈值：DER ${(pick.point.der * 100).toFixed(2)}%，人数合格 ${pick.point.countHits}/${sessions.length} 场`);
}

async function confirm() {
  const { pick } = winner();
  if (!pick.needsConfirm) return log('区间中点已经在测过的阈值上，不需要再测');
  await runJobs(diarJobs({ mixes: primaryMixes(), embeddings: [embeddingOf(pick)], thresholds: [pick.threshold], knownCount: false }), 'confirm');
}

async function far() {
  const { pick } = winner();
  const mixes = sessions.map((s) => buildFarMix({ work: paths.work, session: s }));
  await runJobs(diarJobs({ mixes, embeddings: [embeddingOf(pick)], thresholds: [pick.threshold], knownCount: false }), 'far');
}

async function rotations() {
  const { pick } = winner();
  const mixes = sessions.flatMap((s) => s.tracks.filter((t) => t.speaker !== s.me).map((t) => buildMix({ work: paths.work, session: s, me: t.speaker })));
  await runJobs(diarJobs({ mixes, embeddings: [embeddingOf(pick)], thresholds: [pick.threshold], knownCount: false }), 'rotations');
}

async function timing() {
  const { pick } = winner();
  const file = path.join(resultDir('timing'), 'timing.json');
  if (fs.existsSync(file) && !force) return log(`已有，跳过：${file}`);
  const embedding = embeddingOf(pick);
  const mixes = pickThree(sessions).map((s) => buildMix({ work: paths.work, session: s, me: s.me }));
  const jobs = diarJobs({ mixes, embeddings: [embedding], thresholds: [pick.threshold], knownCount: false });
  const runs = [];
  for (const job of jobs) {
    log(`计时：${job.key}，8 线程`);
    const before = cpuSnapshot();
    const r = await runDiarJob({ run, exe, segmentationModel, embeddingDir: paths.downloads, job: { ...job, key: `${job.key}__timing` }, outDir: path.join(paths.work, 'diar-out', 'timing'), threads: 8, priority: 'Normal' });
    const others = othersLoad(before, cpuSnapshot(), r.cpuMs);
    runs.push({ mixId: r.mixId, durationSec: r.durationSec, wallMs: r.wallMs, elapsedSec: r.elapsedSec, rtfElapsed: r.rtf, rtfWall: r.wallMs / 1000 / r.durationSec, peakWorkingSetMb: r.peakWorkingSetMb, cpuMs: r.cpuMs, othersLoad: others });
    log(`  实时倍数（含加载）${runs.at(-1).rtfWall.toFixed(4)}  峰值内存 ${r.peakWorkingSetMb} MB  别的程序占用 ${(others * 100).toFixed(0)}%`);
  }
  const sum = (k) => runs.reduce((a, r) => a + r[k], 0);
  writeJsonAtomic(file, { threads: 8, embedding: embedding.id, threshold: pick.threshold, runs, summary: { rtfWall: sum('wallMs') / 1000 / sum('durationSec'), peakWorkingSetMb: Math.max(...runs.map((r) => r.peakWorkingSetMb)), othersLoadMean: sum('othersLoad') / runs.length }, finishedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
}

const stages = { sweep, status, confirm, far, rotations, timing };
if (!stages[stage]) throw new Error('用法：node scripts/s4/diar.mjs <sweep|status|confirm|far|rotations|timing> --home <S4 目录> [--workers N] [--force]');
await stages[stage]();
log(`${stage} 完成`);
```

- [ ] **步骤 6：确认 `scripts/s4/diar.mjs` 能加载**

脚本一加载就会把它引用的模块全部导入；没给 `--home` 也没设 `S4_HOME` 时它在读目录这一步停下并说明原因。能停在这一句，说明引用的模块都找得到（真正的运行在后面的任务里）。

```powershell
Remove-Item Env:S4_HOME -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
node scripts/s4/diar.mjs
```

预期：**失败**（没给 S4 目录，脚本按设计报错）。

- [ ] **步骤 7：提交**

```powershell
git add -A
git commit -m "feat(s4): 说话人分离评测"
```

## 任务 15：aec-harness：回声消除评测小程序（自成工作区的 Rust 工程）

**目标：** 把 corti-aec 与 aec3 包成同一个命令行：`aec-harness <none|corti|aec3> <采样率> <远端.f32> <麦克风.f32> <输出.f32> [键=值 ...]`（`none` 是原样输出，给评测当对照）。输入输出都是原始的、小端的、单声道 f32 文件（由评测脚本转换），不在这里解析 WAV。通用选项 `align=1` 先估计整体延迟并把远端向后平移对齐（`max_delay_ms` 限定搜索范围，`margin_ms` 少平移几毫秒）；标准输出一行 JSON：算法、采样率、样点数、估计的延迟、实际平移的毫秒数、耗时。它自成一个工作区（`[workspace]`），不并入仓库根目录的工作区，不随产品发布，依赖锁在它自己的 `Cargo.lock` 里。

几点需要知道的事：

- **`--ignore-rust-version`**：corti-aec 0.5.1 声明需要 rustc 1.96，作者的机器是 1.95.0，不加这个开关 cargo 直接拒绝。加上之后编译通过、下面 9 个单元测试通过，但上游并没有承诺它在 1.95 上可用。你的 rustc 够新就不需要它，去掉也无妨。
- **依赖树检查**：设计文档 3.2 要求编译前先看依赖树，确认没有 build 脚本联网、没有网络依赖。下面这一步用 `cargo metadata`（只下载并解析，不编译、不运行任何 build 脚本）列出全部依赖：共 19 个包（含它自己），其中 5 个带 build 脚本（`crossbeam-deque`、`crossbeam-epoch`、`crossbeam-utils`、`num-traits`、`rayon-core`），都只有几行到五十来行：四个只是向 cargo 打印指令，`num-traits` 的那个通过 `autocfg` 调用 `rustc` 探测编译器的能力；没有任何网络相关的包。**作者的实际顺序是先编译试了一遍、之后才补做这一步检查**（结果通过）；照着计划做就是先检查、后编译。
- **编译产物放在仓库之外**：下面的命令都先把环境变量 `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` 指到 `<S4 目录>\target-aec`（只在当前这个 PowerShell 窗口里有效，不改系统环境变量；任务 27 的最后一步会把它清除）；评测脚本默认到那里找 `aec-harness.exe`。
- **测试用 `--release`**：其中一个测试拿 30 秒的音频跑两个回声消除器，调试构建会慢很多。

**文件：**

- 创建：`scripts/s4/aec-harness/Cargo.toml` — aec-harness 的依赖（自成工作区）
- 创建：`scripts/s4/aec-harness/Cargo.lock` — aec-harness 的依赖锁定
- 创建：`scripts/s4/aec-harness/src/main.rs` — aec-harness：把 corti-aec 与 aec3 包成同一个命令行（含 9 个单元测试）

**接口：**

- 测试：9 个：`shift_right_pads_front_and_keeps_length`、`f32_files_round_trip_and_reject_bad_length`、`delay_estimate_finds_the_true_delay_even_with_near_end_interference`、`delay_estimate_gives_none_for_silent_far_end_or_short_input`、`none_keeps_the_microphone_signal_and_unknown_algorithms_are_rejected`、`prepare_far_shifts_by_the_estimated_delay_only_when_asked`、`margin_shifts_less_than_the_estimated_delay_and_never_below_zero`、`bad_option_values_are_reported_not_ignored`、`both_algorithms_reduce_a_pure_delayed_echo`

- [ ] **步骤 1：创建 `scripts/s4/aec-harness/Cargo.toml`**

`aec3`、`corti-aec`、`rustfft` 都用 `=` 固定版本；`[workspace]` 让它不并入仓库根目录的工作区。

```toml
[package]
name = "aec-harness"
version = "0.1.0"
edition = "2024"
publish = false
description = "S4 评测用的回声消除小程序：把 corti-aec 与 aec3 包成同一个命令行（不属于主工作区，不随产品发布）"

[dependencies]
aec3 = "=0.4.0"
corti-aec = "=0.5.1"
rustfft = "=6.4.1"

# 自成一个工作区：不并入仓库根目录的工作区，依赖锁定在自己的 Cargo.lock 里
[workspace]

[profile.release]
opt-level = 3
```

先只写测试。这时文件里没有实现，测试引用的函数都不存在（连 `main` 也没有）。

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    /// 可复现的白噪声，范围 [-amp, amp]
    fn noise(n: usize, seed: u32, amp: f32) -> Vec<f32> {
        let mut s = seed;
        (0..n)
            .map(|_| {
                s = s.wrapping_mul(1664525).wrapping_add(1013904223);
                ((s >> 8) as f32 / 8_388_608.0 - 1.0) * amp
            })
            .collect()
    }

    fn energy(x: &[f32]) -> f64 {
        x.iter().map(|v| (*v as f64) * (*v as f64)).sum()
    }

    #[test]
    fn shift_right_pads_front_and_keeps_length() {
        assert_eq!(shift_right(&[1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0], 2), vec![0.0, 0.0, 1.0, 2.0]);
        assert_eq!(shift_right(&[1.0, 2.0], 0), vec![1.0, 2.0]);
        assert_eq!(shift_right(&[1.0, 2.0], 5), vec![0.0, 0.0]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn f32_files_round_trip_and_reject_bad_length() {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("aec-harness-test-{}", std::process::id()));
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        let p = dir.join("a.f32");
        let x = vec![0.0, -1.5, 3.25, f32::MIN_POSITIVE];
        write_f32(p.to_str().unwrap(), &x).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(read_f32(p.to_str().unwrap()).unwrap(), x);
        std::fs::write(&p, [1u8, 2, 3]).unwrap();
        assert!(read_f32(p.to_str().unwrap()).is_err());
        std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir).ok();
    }

    #[test]
    fn delay_estimate_finds_the_true_delay_even_with_near_end_interference() {
        let rate = 16000usize;
        let far = noise(rate * 20, 1, 0.5);
        for d in [0usize, 320, 1600, 4800] {
            let mut mic = shift_right(&far, d).iter().map(|v| v * 0.4).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let near = noise(mic.len(), 2, 0.2);
            for (m, n) in mic.iter_mut().zip(&near) {
                *m += n;
            }
            assert_eq!(estimate_delay(&far, &mic, 8000), Some(d), "延迟 {d} 个样点");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn delay_estimate_gives_none_for_silent_far_end_or_short_input() {
        let rate = 16000usize;
        let mic = noise(rate * 20, 3, 0.5);
        assert_eq!(estimate_delay(&vec![0.0; rate * 20], &mic, 8000), None);
        assert_eq!(estimate_delay(&noise(1000, 4, 0.5), &noise(1000, 5, 0.5), 800), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn none_keeps_the_microphone_signal_and_unknown_algorithms_are_rejected() {
        let far = noise(100, 6, 0.5);
        let mic = noise(100, 7, 0.5);
        assert_eq!(run("none", &far, &mic, 16000, &HashMap::new()).unwrap(), mic);
        assert!(run("bogus", &far, &mic, 16000, &HashMap::new()).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("不认识的算法"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn prepare_far_shifts_by_the_estimated_delay_only_when_asked() {
        let rate = 16000u32;
        let far = noise(rate as usize * 20, 8, 0.5);
        let mic: Vec<f32> = shift_right(&far, 1600).iter().map(|v| v * 0.4).collect();
        let (same, none, no_shift) = prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &HashMap::new()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(same, far);
        assert_eq!(none, None);
        assert_eq!(no_shift, None);
        let align = HashMap::from([("align".to_string(), "1".to_string())]);
        let (shifted, ms, shift_ms) = prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &align).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(shifted, shift_right(&far, 1600));
        assert!((ms.unwrap() - 100.0).abs() < 1e-9, "估计的延迟 {ms:?} 毫秒");
        assert!((shift_ms.unwrap() - 100.0).abs() < 1e-9, "平移 {shift_ms:?} 毫秒");
        // 搜索范围不够大时，不会估出一个错的数当作对的
        let (_, small, _) = prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &HashMap::from([("align".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("max_delay_ms".to_string(), "50".to_string())])).unwrap();
        assert_ne!(small, Some(100.0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn margin_shifts_less_than_the_estimated_delay_and_never_below_zero() {
        let rate = 16000u32;
        let far = noise(rate as usize * 20, 8, 0.5);
        let mic: Vec<f32> = shift_right(&far, 1600).iter().map(|v| v * 0.4).collect();
        let with = |margin: &str| HashMap::from([("align".to_string(), "1".to_string()), ("margin_ms".to_string(), margin.to_string())]);
        // 估计的延迟仍是 100 ms，实际只平移 100 - 10 = 90 ms（1440 个样点）
        let (shifted, ms, shift_ms) = prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &with("10")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(shifted, shift_right(&far, 1440));
        assert!((ms.unwrap() - 100.0).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!((shift_ms.unwrap() - 90.0).abs() < 1e-9);
        // 余量比延迟还大：不平移（不会减成负数绕回去）
        let (zero, _, shift_ms) = prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &with("250")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(zero, far);
        assert_eq!(shift_ms, Some(0.0));
        // 负数、NaN（它和任何数比大小都是假的）与写错的值都报错
        assert!(prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &with("-1")).is_err());
        assert!(prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &with("NaN")).is_err());
        assert!(prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &with("abc")).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn bad_option_values_are_reported_not_ignored() {
        let far = noise(100, 6, 0.5);
        let o = HashMap::from([("filter_len".to_string(), "abc".to_string())]);
        assert!(run_corti(&far, &far, 16000, &o).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("filter_len=abc"));
        let o = HashMap::from([("align".to_string(), "yes".to_string())]);
        assert!(prepare_far(&far, &far, 16000, &o).is_err());
    }

    /// 纯回声（麦克风里只有远端延迟 + 衰减）：两个算法在后半段都应把能量压下去至少 10 dB。
    #[test]
    fn both_algorithms_reduce_a_pure_delayed_echo() {
        let rate = 16000u32;
        let far = noise(rate as usize * 30, 7, 0.5);
        let mic: Vec<f32> = shift_right(&far, 1600).iter().map(|v| v * 0.4).collect();
        let half = mic.len() / 2;
        let before = energy(&mic[half..]);

        let mut opts = HashMap::new();
        opts.insert("filter_len".to_string(), "16384".to_string());
        let corti = run_corti(&far, &mic, rate, &opts).unwrap();
        let reduction = 10.0 * (before / energy(&corti[half..]).max(1e-30)).log10();
        assert!(reduction >= 10.0, "corti 对齐前只压了 {reduction:.1} dB");

        let aligned = shift_right(&far, estimate_delay(&far, &mic, 8000).unwrap());
        let aec3 = run_aec3(&aligned, &mic, rate, &HashMap::new()).unwrap();
        let reduction = 10.0 * (before / energy(&aec3[half..]).max(1e-30)).log10();
        assert!(reduction >= 10.0, "aec3 对齐后只压了 {reduction:.1} dB");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 6：把实现补到 `scripts/s4/aec-harness/src/main.rs` 的最前面**

把下面这段放在文件最上面，和后面的 `#[cfg(test)]` 之间空一行：

```rust
//! S4 评测用的回声消除小程序。
//!
//! 用法：`aec-harness <算法> <采样率> <远端.f32> <麦克风.f32> <输出.f32> [键=值 ...]`
//!
//! - 输入输出都是原始的、小端的、单声道 f32 文件（由评测脚本转换），不在这里解析 WAV，也不引入别的依赖。
//! - 算法：`none`（原样输出，用来核对读写）、`corti`（corti-aec）、`aec3`（WebRTC AEC3 的 Rust 移植）。
//! - 通用选项：`align=1` 先估计整体延迟并把远端向后平移对齐；`max_delay_ms=500` 是延迟搜索范围；`margin_ms=0` 是少平移的毫秒数（让远端比回声的直达声早到一点）。
//! - corti 选项：`filter_len mu eps power_smoothing double_talk_ratio passes suppress_residual`，缺省用库的缺省值。
//! - aec3 选项：`delay_ms` 初始延迟提示（缺省 0）。
//! - 标准输出一行 JSON：算法、采样率、样点数、估计的延迟与实际平移的毫秒数（没估计则为 null）、耗时（秒）。

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::error::Error;
use std::time::Instant;

use rustfft::{FftPlanner, num_complex::Complex};

type Res<T> = Result<T, Box<dyn Error>>;

fn read_f32(path: &str) -> Res<Vec<f32>> {
    let bytes = std::fs::read(path)?;
    if bytes.len() % 4 != 0 {
        return Err(format!("{path}：长度不是 4 的倍数，不像原始 f32 文件").into());
    }
    Ok(bytes.chunks_exact(4).map(|c| f32::from_le_bytes([c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]])).collect())
}

fn write_f32(path: &str, samples: &[f32]) -> Res<()> {
    let mut bytes = Vec::with_capacity(samples.len() * 4);
    for s in samples {
        bytes.extend_from_slice(&s.to_le_bytes());
    }
    std::fs::write(path, bytes)?;
    Ok(())
}

/// 把信号整体向后平移 d 个样点（前面补 0，长度不变）。
fn shift_right(x: &[f32], d: usize) -> Vec<f32> {
    let mut out = vec![0.0; x.len()];
    if d < x.len() {
        out[d..].copy_from_slice(&x[..x.len() - d]);
    }
    out
}

const SEG_LEN: usize = 32768;
const MAX_SEGS: usize = 64;

/// 估计 mic 相对 far 的整体延迟（样点数，不小于 0，不超过 max_lag）：mic[n] ≈ far[n - d]。
/// 做法：取最多 MAX_SEGS 段远端有声的片段，每段做 PHAT 加权的互相关（互功率谱除以自己的模），把各段累加后反变换，取峰值位置。
/// 远端全程无声或太短时返回 None。
fn estimate_delay(far: &[f32], mic: &[f32], max_lag: usize) -> Option<usize> {
    let n = far.len().min(mic.len());
    if n < SEG_LEN + max_lag {
        return None;
    }
    let fft_len = (SEG_LEN + max_lag).next_power_of_two();
    let starts: Vec<usize> = (0..MAX_SEGS).map(|i| i * (n - SEG_LEN - max_lag) / (MAX_SEGS - 1).max(1)).collect();
    let energy = |s: usize| far[s..s + SEG_LEN].iter().map(|v| (*v as f64) * (*v as f64)).sum::<f64>();
    let top = starts.iter().map(|&s| energy(s)).fold(0.0, f64::max);
    if top <= 0.0 {
        return None;
    }
    let mut planner = FftPlanner::<f32>::new();
    let fwd = planner.plan_fft_forward(fft_len);
    let inv = planner.plan_fft_inverse(fft_len);
    let mut acc = vec![Complex::new(0.0f32, 0.0); fft_len];
    let mut used = 0;
    let mut last = usize::MAX;
    for &s in &starts {
        if s == last || energy(s) < 0.25 * top {
            continue;
        }
        last = s;
        let mut x = vec![Complex::new(0.0f32, 0.0); fft_len];
        let mut y = vec![Complex::new(0.0f32, 0.0); fft_len];
        for i in 0..SEG_LEN {
            x[i].re = far[s + i];
        }
        for i in 0..SEG_LEN + max_lag {
            y[i].re = mic[s + i];
        }
        fwd.process(&mut x);
        fwd.process(&mut y);
        for k in 0..fft_len {
            let c = x[k].conj() * y[k];
            let m = c.norm();
            if m > 1e-12 {
                acc[k] += c / m;
            }
        }
        used += 1;
    }
    if used == 0 {
        return None;
    }
    inv.process(&mut acc);
    (0..=max_lag).max_by(|&a, &b| acc[a].re.partial_cmp(&acc[b].re).unwrap())
}

fn opt<T: std::str::FromStr>(o: &HashMap<String, String>, key: &str) -> Res<Option<T>> {
    match o.get(key) {
        None => Ok(None),
        Some(v) => v.parse::<T>().map(Some).map_err(|_| format!("选项 {key}={v} 的值不对").into()),
    }
}

fn run_corti(far: &[f32], mic: &[f32], rate: u32, o: &HashMap<String, String>) -> Res<Vec<f32>> {
    let mut cfg = corti_aec::AecConfig::default();
    if let Some(v) = opt(o, "filter_len")? {
        cfg.filter_len = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = opt(o, "mu")? {
        cfg.mu = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = opt(o, "eps")? {
        cfg.eps = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = opt(o, "power_smoothing")? {
        cfg.power_smoothing = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = opt(o, "double_talk_ratio")? {
        cfg.double_talk_ratio = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = opt(o, "passes")? {
        cfg.passes = v;
    }
    if let Some(v) = opt(o, "suppress_residual")? {
        cfg.suppress_residual = v;
    }
    Ok(corti_aec::cancel(mic, far, rate, &cfg))
}

fn run_aec3(far: &[f32], mic: &[f32], rate: u32, o: &HashMap<String, String>) -> Res<Vec<f32>> {
    use aec3::nodes::audio::AudioFormat;
    use aec3::pipelines::linear;
    let delay_ms: i32 = opt(o, "delay_ms")?.unwrap_or(0);
    let format = AudioFormat::ten_ms(rate, 1);
    let mut pipeline = linear::builder(format, format).initial_delay_ms(delay_ms).build()?;
    let frame = format.sample_count();
    let mut out = Vec::with_capacity(mic.len());
    let padded = |x: &[f32], at: usize| {
        let mut f = vec![0.0f32; frame];
        if at < x.len() {
            let n = (x.len() - at).min(frame);
            f[..n].copy_from_slice(&x[at..at + n]);
        }
        f
    };
    let mut at = 0;
    while at < mic.len() {
        pipeline.handle_render_frame(&padded(far, at))?;
        let valid = (mic.len() - at).min(frame);
        let mut processed = vec![0.0f32; frame];
        let produced = pipeline.process_capture_frame(&padded(mic, at), &mut processed)?;
        if produced {
            out.extend_from_slice(&processed[..valid]);
        } else {
            out.resize(out.len() + valid, 0.0);
        }
        at += valid;
    }
    Ok(out)
}

/// `align=1` 时先估计整体延迟并把远端向后平移对齐；返回（用于消除的远端，估计的延迟毫秒，实际平移的毫秒）。没开 `align` 或估计不出来时原样返回。
/// `margin_ms`（缺省 0）：少平移这么多，让远端比回声的直达声早到 margin_ms。零余量时直达声恰好落在滤波器的第 0 点，估计差一个样点它就跑到参考信号前面去，因果滤波器消不掉。
fn prepare_far(far: &[f32], mic: &[f32], rate: u32, o: &HashMap<String, String>) -> Res<(Vec<f32>, Option<f64>, Option<f64>)> {
    if opt::<u8>(o, "align")?.unwrap_or(0) == 0 {
        return Ok((far.to_vec(), None, None));
    }
    let max_ms: f64 = opt(o, "max_delay_ms")?.unwrap_or(500.0);
    let margin_ms: f64 = opt(o, "margin_ms")?.unwrap_or(0.0);
    if margin_ms.is_nan() || margin_ms < 0.0 {
        return Err(format!("选项 margin_ms={margin_ms} 要是不小于 0 的数").into());
    }
    let max_lag = (max_ms * rate as f64 / 1000.0) as usize;
    let margin = (margin_ms * rate as f64 / 1000.0).round() as usize;
    Ok(match estimate_delay(far, mic, max_lag) {
        Some(d) => {
            let shift = d.saturating_sub(margin);
            (shift_right(far, shift), Some(d as f64 * 1000.0 / rate as f64), Some(shift as f64 * 1000.0 / rate as f64))
        }
        None => (far.to_vec(), None, None),
    })
}

/// 按算法名处理：none 原样返回麦克风信号，corti、aec3 调各自的库。
fn run(algo: &str, far: &[f32], mic: &[f32], rate: u32, o: &HashMap<String, String>) -> Res<Vec<f32>> {
    match algo {
        "none" => Ok(mic.to_vec()),
        "corti" => run_corti(far, mic, rate, o),
        "aec3" => run_aec3(far, mic, rate, o),
        other => Err(format!("不认识的算法 {other}").into()),
    }
}

fn main() -> Res<()> {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();
    if args.len() < 5 {
        return Err("用法：aec-harness <none|corti|aec3> <采样率> <远端.f32> <麦克风.f32> <输出.f32> [键=值 ...]".into());
    }
    let algo = args[0].as_str();
    let rate: u32 = args[1].parse().map_err(|_| format!("采样率 {} 不对", args[1]))?;
    let mut options = HashMap::new();
    for kv in &args[5..] {
        let (k, v) = kv.split_once('=').ok_or_else(|| format!("选项 {kv} 不是 键=值 的形式"))?;
        options.insert(k.to_string(), v.to_string());
    }
    let far = read_f32(&args[2])?;
    let mic = read_f32(&args[3])?;
    if far.len() != mic.len() {
        return Err(format!("远端与麦克风长度不同：{} 与 {} 个样点", far.len(), mic.len()).into());
    }

    let started = Instant::now();
    let (far_used, delay_ms, shift_ms) = prepare_far(&far, &mic, rate, &options)?;
    let out = run(algo, &far_used, &mic, rate, &options)?;
    let wall = started.elapsed().as_secs_f64();
    write_f32(&args[4], &out)?;
    let ms = |v: Option<f64>| v.map_or("null".to_string(), |d| format!("{d:.2}"));
    println!("{{\"algo\":\"{algo}\",\"rate\":{rate},\"samples\":{},\"delay_ms\":{},\"shift_ms\":{},\"wall_s\":{wall:.3}}}", out.len(), ms(delay_ms), ms(shift_ms));
    Ok(())
}
```

## 任务 16：回声消除评测

**目标：** 回声消除评测的逻辑：合成回声的条件网格（3 种通路 × 3 档能量比）、5 种处理方式（不处理、corti、corti + 对齐、aec3、aec3 + 对齐）、近端段与远端单讲段的挑选、一个条件下各处理方式的评估（近端 CER、远端单讲段里识别出的字数）、各场结果汇总成 `decide.mjs` 要的格式。处理（调 `aec-harness`）与识别都由调用方注入，所以这里用假的测。`echo.mjs` 是把它们接到真实程序上的命令行脚本。

**文件：**

- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/echo-eval.test.mjs` — `echo-eval.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/echo-eval.mjs` — 回声消除评测：条件网格、近端段与远端单讲段、各处理方式的评估与汇总
- 创建：`scripts/s4/echo.mjs` — 命令行：回声消除评测（合成回声）

**接口：**

- 依赖：`cer.mjs`、`dataset.mjs`、`echo.mjs`、`wav.mjs`
- 提供：`SCENARIOS`；`RATIOS_DB`；`ALIGN_MARGIN_MS`；`VARIANTS`；`conditions()`；`excerptSegments(segments, endSec)`；`farOnlySegments(meSegments, otherSegments, guard = 0.25)`；`makeMic({ near, far, meSegments, otherSegments, scenario, ratioDb, rate })`；`cutSignal(samples, segments, rate, dir, prefix)`；`keepOnly(samples, mask)`；`evalVariants({ rate, far, mic, nearSegs, farOnlySegs, variants = VARIANTS, deps, dir })`；`scoreOutput({ nearRefs, farRefs }, { nearHyps, farText })`；`baselineCounts(nearRefs, nearHyps)`；`aggregateEcho(sessions)`
- 测试：`echo-eval.test.mjs` 11 个

- [ ] **步骤 1：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/echo-eval.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `echo-eval.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { aggregateEcho, ALIGN_MARGIN_MS, baselineCounts, conditions, cutSignal, evalVariants, excerptSegments, farOnlySegments, keepOnly, makeMic, RATIOS_DB, SCENARIOS, scoreOutput, VARIANTS } from './echo-eval.mjs';
import { mulberry32 } from './echo.mjs';
import { pickAec } from './decide.mjs';
import { readWavChannel, readWavInfo } from './wav.mjs';

const tmp = () => fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'record-s4-echoeval-'));
const RATE = 8000;

test('条件网格：三种通路 × 三档能量比 = 九个，编号不重复；处理方式五种', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(SCENARIOS.map((s) => [s.delayMs, s.rt60]), [[20, 0.2], [100, 0.4], [300, 0.6]]);
  assert.deepEqual(RATIOS_DB, [-10, 0, 5]);
  const c = conditions();
  assert.equal(c.length, 9);
  assert.equal(new Set(c.map((x) => x.id)).size, 9);
  assert.deepEqual(c.slice(0, 3).map((x) => x.id), ['d020r02_r-10', 'd020r02_r0', 'd020r02_r5']);
  assert.equal(c[8].id, 'd300r06_r5');
  assert.equal(c[8].scenario.delayMs, 300);
  assert.equal(c[8].ratioDb, 5);
  assert.deepEqual(VARIANTS.map((v) => v.id), ['none', 'corti', 'corti-align', 'aec3', 'aec3-align']);
  assert.ok(VARIANTS.filter((v) => v.algo === 'corti').every((v) => v.opts.includes('filter_len=16384')));
  assert.deepEqual(VARIANTS.filter((v) => v.id.endsWith('-align')).map((v) => v.opts.includes('align=1')), [true, true]);
  assert.ok(VARIANTS.filter((v) => !v.id.endsWith('-align')).every((v) => !v.opts.includes('align=1') && !v.opts.some((o) => o.startsWith('margin_ms='))));
  assert.equal(ALIGN_MARGIN_MS, 10);
  assert.ok(VARIANTS.filter((v) => v.id.endsWith('-align')).every((v) => v.opts.includes('margin_ms=10')), '对齐要留余量，零余量会让 aec3 失效');
});

test('截取与远端单讲段：终点不超过截取点；与“我”的段（前后放宽 guard 秒）重叠的不算', () => {
  const segs = [{ start: 0, end: 5 }, { start: 5, end: 10 }, { start: 8, end: 10.5 }];
  assert.deepEqual(excerptSegments(segs, 10), [segs[0], segs[1]]);
  assert.deepEqual(excerptSegments(segs, 10.5), segs);
  const me = [{ start: 10, end: 12 }];
  const others = [{ start: 4, end: 9.75 }, { start: 4, end: 9.76 }, { start: 12.25, end: 14 }, { start: 12.24, end: 14 }, { start: 0, end: 1 }];
  assert.deepEqual(farOnlySegments(me, others, 0.25), [others[0], others[2], others[4]]); // 恰好碰到放宽边界的不算重叠
  assert.deepEqual(farOnlySegments([], others, 0.25), others);
  assert.deepEqual(farOnlySegments(me, others, 0), [others[0], others[1], others[2], others[3], others[4]]);
});

// 一个小场景：20 秒，“我”说三段，对方说三段，其中 9～11 秒与“我”的 8～10 秒重叠（双讲，不算远端单讲）
const ME = [{ start: 1, end: 3, text: '我说第一段' }, { start: 8, end: 10, text: '我说第二段' }, { start: 14, end: 16, text: '我说第三段' }];
const OTHERS = [{ start: 4, end: 6, text: '对方第一段话' }, { start: 9, end: 11, text: '对方第二段话' }, { start: 17, end: 19, text: '对方第三段话' }];
function signals() {
  const r = mulberry32(5);
  const burst = (segs) => {
    const x = new Float32Array(RATE * 20);
    for (const s of segs) for (let i = Math.round(s.start * RATE); i < Math.round(s.end * RATE); i++) x[i] = (r() - 0.5) * 0.4;
    return x;
  };
  return { near: burst(ME), far: burst(OTHERS) };
}
const rms = (x) => Math.sqrt(x.reduce((a, v) => a + v * v, 0) / x.length);

test('合成麦克风信号：近端 + 回声 + 底噪，同样的输入得到同样的结果', () => {
  const { near, far } = signals();
  const args = { near, far, meSegments: ME, otherSegments: OTHERS, scenario: SCENARIOS[1], ratioDb: 0, rate: RATE };
  const a = makeMic(args);
  const b = makeMic(args);
  assert.deepEqual(a.mic, b.mic);
  assert.equal(a.mic.length, near.length);
  // 回声段（远端单讲的 4～6 秒）里：麦克风 = 回声 + 很小的底噪
  const slice = (x) => x.subarray(4 * RATE, 6 * RATE);
  const resid = Float32Array.from(slice(a.mic), (v, i) => v - slice(a.echo)[i]);
  assert.ok(rms(resid) < 0.01 * rms(slice(a.mic)) * 10 && rms(slice(a.echo)) > 0.02, `回声 ${rms(slice(a.echo))}，残差 ${rms(resid)}`);
  for (const sc of [SCENARIOS[0], SCENARIOS[2]]) {
    const { echo } = makeMic({ ...args, scenario: sc });
    const from = (t) => Math.round(t * RATE);
    const early = rms(echo.subarray(from(4), from(4 + sc.delayMs / 1000 - 0.005))); // 对方 4 秒开口，直达声要晚 delayMs 才到
    const late = rms(echo.subarray(from(4 + sc.delayMs / 1000 + 0.01), from(4 + sc.delayMs / 1000 + 0.3)));
    assert.ok(early < 1e-6 && late > 0.01, `延迟 ${sc.delayMs} ms：之前 ${early}，之后 ${late}`);
  }
  const louder = makeMic({ ...args, ratioDb: 5 });
  assert.ok(rms(louder.echo) > 1.5 * rms(a.echo), '能量比抬高 5 dB 回声应更响');
});

test('合成麦克风信号：能量比取负无穷就是没有回声（只有近端和底噪），底噪与有回声时相同', () => {
  const { near, far } = signals();
  const args = { near, far, meSegments: ME, otherSegments: OTHERS, scenario: SCENARIOS[0], rate: RATE };
  const none = makeMic({ ...args, ratioDb: -Infinity });
  assert.ok(none.echo.every((v) => v === 0));
  const withEcho = makeMic({ ...args, ratioDb: 0 });
  for (let i = 0; i < near.length; i += 997) assert.ok(Math.abs(none.mic[i] - (withEcho.mic[i] - withEcho.echo[i])) < 1e-5, `第 ${i} 点：底噪应当相同`);
  const quiet = rms(none.mic.subarray(4 * RATE, 6 * RATE)); // 远端单讲的 4～6 秒：近端静默、没有回声，只剩底噪（约为近端有声段的 1%）
  const speech = rms(near.subarray(RATE, 3 * RATE));
  assert.ok(quiet > 0.005 * speech && quiet < 0.02 * speech, `底噪 ${quiet}，近端 ${speech}`);
});

test('切信号：按段写 wav，文件名带前缀与序号，帧数对得上；有段落在信号之外就报错', () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const x = Float32Array.from({ length: RATE * 20 }, (_, i) => Math.sin(i / 30) * 0.5);
  const files = cutSignal(x, ME, RATE, dir, 'v-near-');
  assert.deepEqual(files.map((f) => path.basename(f)), ['v-near-0000.wav', 'v-near-0001.wav', 'v-near-0002.wav']);
  assert.deepEqual(files.map((f) => readWavInfo(f).frames), [16000, 16000, 16000]);
  const got = readWavChannel(files[1], 0).samples;
  assert.ok(Math.abs(got[100] - x[8 * RATE + 100]) < 1e-3);
  assert.throws(() => cutSignal(x, [{ start: 25, end: 26, text: '外' }], RATE, dir, 'v-'), /1 个段落在信号之外/);
  assert.deepEqual(cutSignal(x, [], RATE, dir, 'v-'), []);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

// 假的回声消除与假的识别：识别器只认能量（均方根大于 0.05 就“认出”参考文本），所以回声残留与否直接决定远端单讲段有没有文字
function fakes(echo, expect) {
  const calls = { aec: [], decode: [], far: [] };
  const factors = { corti: (m, e) => m - e, 'corti-align': (m) => m, aec3: (m, e) => m - 0.7 * e, 'aec3-align': (m) => m * 0.01 };
  const deps = {
    aec: async (v, far, mic) => {
      calls.aec.push(v.id);
      assert.equal(far, expect.far, '远端参考应当是原来的远端信号');
      assert.equal(mic, expect.mic, '要处理的应当是麦克风信号');
      return { samples: Float32Array.from(mic, (m, i) => factors[v.id](m, echo[i])), info: { delay_ms: 100, wall_s: 1.5 } };
    },
    decode: async (files) => {
      calls.decode.push(files);
      return files.map((f) => {
        const m = /-near-(\d{4})\.wav$/.exec(f);
        assert.ok(m, `只有近端段才逐段识别：${f}`);
        const x = readWavChannel(f, 0).samples;
        return rms(x) > 0.05 ? ME[Number(m[1])].text : '';
      });
    },
    // 假的 VAD + 识别：整条信号里，每个远端单讲段只要有能量就“识别”出参考文本；并检查远端单讲段之外确实被置成了 0
    decodeFar: async (file) => {
      calls.far.push(path.basename(file));
      const x = readWavChannel(file, 0).samples;
      assert.equal(x.length, RATE * 20);
      const inside = new Uint8Array(x.length);
      for (const s of OTHERS_FAR_ONLY) inside.fill(1, Math.round(s.start * RATE), Math.round(s.end * RATE));
      for (let i = 0; i < x.length; i++) if (!inside[i]) assert.equal(x[i], 0, `远端单讲段之外应当置零，第 ${i} 点`);
      return OTHERS_FAR_ONLY.filter((s) => rms(x.subarray(Math.round(s.start * RATE), Math.round(s.end * RATE))) > 0.05).map((s) => s.text).join('');
    },
  };
  return { deps, calls };
}
const OTHERS_FAR_ONLY = farOnlySegments(ME, OTHERS, 0.25);

test('一个条件下各处理方式：回声消减量、近端识别结果、远端单讲段（VAD + 识别）的文字各归各的', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const { near, far } = signals();
  const { mic, echo } = makeMic({ near, far, meSegments: ME, otherSegments: OTHERS, scenario: SCENARIOS[0], ratioDb: 0, rate: RATE });
  assert.deepEqual(OTHERS_FAR_ONLY.map((s) => s.text), ['对方第一段话', '对方第三段话']); // 第二段与“我”重叠
  const { deps, calls } = fakes(echo, { far, mic });
  const out = await evalVariants({ rate: RATE, far, mic, nearSegs: ME, farOnlySegs: OTHERS_FAR_ONLY, deps, dir });
  assert.deepEqual(out.map((o) => o.variant), VARIANTS.map((v) => v.id));
  assert.deepEqual(calls.aec, ['corti', 'corti-align', 'aec3', 'aec3-align']); // none 不调用
  assert.equal(calls.decode.length, 1); // 所有近端文件一次交给识别
  assert.equal(calls.decode[0].length, 5 * 3);
  assert.deepEqual(calls.decode[0].slice(0, 4).map((f) => path.basename(f)), ['none-near-0000.wav', 'none-near-0001.wav', 'none-near-0002.wav', 'corti-near-0000.wav']);
  assert.deepEqual(calls.far, VARIANTS.map((v) => `${v.id}-far.wav`)); // 每种处理一条整条信号，按处理方式的顺序
  const by = Object.fromEntries(out.map((o) => [o.variant, o]));
  const refsNear = ME.map((s) => s.text);
  const textFar = OTHERS_FAR_ONLY.map((s) => s.text).join('');

  assert.equal(by.none.erleDb, 0);
  assert.equal(by.none.info, null);
  assert.deepEqual(by.none.nearHyps, refsNear); // 近端有声，认得出
  assert.equal(by.none.farText, textFar); // 没处理：回声被认成了对方的话

  assert.ok(by.corti.erleDb > 30, `完全消掉时 ERLE ${by.corti.erleDb}`);
  assert.deepEqual(by.corti.nearHyps, refsNear);
  assert.equal(by.corti.farText, '');
  assert.deepEqual(by.corti.info, { delay_ms: 100, wall_s: 1.5 });

  assert.ok(Math.abs(by['corti-align'].erleDb) < 1e-9); // 原样返回
  assert.equal(by['corti-align'].farText, textFar);

  assert.ok(by.aec3.erleDb > 9 && by.aec3.erleDb < 12, `只消掉 70% 的幅度时 ERLE 约 10.5 dB，实际 ${by.aec3.erleDb}`);
  assert.equal(by.aec3.farText, ''); // 剩下的回声低于识别器的听觉门槛

  assert.ok(Math.abs(by['aec3-align'].erleDb - 40) < 0.01); // 整体压到 1%：回声压下去了，近端也压没了
  assert.deepEqual(by['aec3-align'].nearHyps, ['', '', '']);
  assert.equal(by['aec3-align'].farText, '');
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('一个条件下各处理方式：处理后长度变了、识别返回的个数不对，都明确报错', async () => {
  const dir = tmp();
  const { near, far } = signals();
  const { mic } = makeMic({ near, far, meSegments: ME, otherSegments: OTHERS, scenario: SCENARIOS[0], ratioDb: 0, rate: RATE });
  const base = { rate: RATE, far, mic, nearSegs: ME, farOnlySegs: OTHERS_FAR_ONLY, dir, variants: [VARIANTS[1]] };
  const decodeFar = async () => '';
  await assert.rejects(evalVariants({ ...base, deps: { aec: async () => ({ samples: mic.subarray(1), info: null }), decode: async (f) => f.map(() => ''), decodeFar } }), /corti：处理后长度 \d+ 与输入 \d+ 不同/);
  await assert.rejects(evalVariants({ ...base, deps: { aec: async () => ({ samples: mic, info: null }), decode: async (f) => f.slice(1).map(() => ''), decodeFar } }), /识别返回了 2 个结果，应当是 3 个/);
  fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

test('只留掩码内的点：其余置零、长度不变、不改原信号', () => {
  const x = Float32Array.from([0.5, -0.25, 0.75, 0.125, -1]);
  const kept = keepOnly(x, Uint8Array.from([0, 1, 1, 0, 1]));
  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(kept), [0, -0.25, 0.75, 0, -1]);
  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(x), [0.5, -0.25, 0.75, 0.125, -1]);
  assert.notEqual(kept, x);
  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(keepOnly(x, new Uint8Array(5))), [0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);
  assert.deepEqual(Array.from(keepOnly(new Float32Array(0), new Uint8Array(0))), []);
});

test('打分：近端 CER 的计数与回声插入的计数（按规整后的字数）', () => {
  const s = scoreOutput({ nearRefs: ['你好世界', '再见'], farRefs: ['对方的话', '。，'] }, { nearHyps: ['你好世', '再见了'], farText: '对，方的话啊啊！' });
  assert.equal(s.nearRefLen, 6);
  assert.equal(s.nearErrors, 2); // 删一个字、多一个字
  assert.equal(s.insHyp, 6); // 标点不算字：对方的话啊啊
  assert.equal(s.insRef, 4);
  assert.throws(() => scoreOutput({ nearRefs: ['a'], farRefs: [] }, { nearHyps: [], farText: '' }), /个数对不上/);
  assert.equal(scoreOutput({ nearRefs: [], farRefs: [] }, { nearHyps: [], farText: '嗯 嗯。' }).insHyp, 2); // 远端文字是整条信号的，不要求与参考段数对应
  assert.deepEqual(baselineCounts(['你好世界'], ['你好']), { errors: 2, refLen: 4 });
  assert.throws(() => baselineCounts(['a', 'b'], ['a']), /个数对不上/);
});

const cell = (o) => ({ nearErrors: 5, nearRefLen: 100, insHyp: 2, insRef: 100, erleDb: 20, ...o });
const floorOf = (o) => ({ nearErrors: 4, nearRefLen: 100, insHyp: 3, insRef: 100, ...o });

test('汇总：基线与各格都按总量合计，近端 CER 增加 = 该格 CER − 基线；ERLE 取各场的平均', () => {
  const s1 = { baseline: { errors: 10, refLen: 100 }, floor: floorOf({}), conditions: { c1: { none: cell({ insHyp: 50, erleDb: 0 }), corti: cell({ nearErrors: 12, insHyp: 0, erleDb: 30 }) } } };
  const s2 = { baseline: { errors: 10, refLen: 300 }, floor: floorOf({ nearRefLen: 300, nearErrors: 6, insHyp: 5, insRef: 300 }), conditions: { c1: { none: cell({ nearRefLen: 300, nearErrors: 15, insHyp: 150, insRef: 300, erleDb: 0 }), corti: cell({ nearRefLen: 300, nearErrors: 18, insHyp: 6, insRef: 300, erleDb: 10 }) } } };
  const a = aggregateEcho([s1, s2]);
  assert.equal(a.baselineCer, 20 / 400);
  assert.equal(a.floor.insertion, 8 / 400); // 底线也按总量合计：(3 + 5) / (100 + 300)
  assert.equal(a.floor.nearCer, 10 / 400);
  const none = a.variants.none.conditions[0];
  assert.equal(none.id, 'c1');
  assert.equal(none.insertion, 200 / 400);
  assert.equal(none.nearCer, 20 / 400);
  assert.equal(none.nearCerIncrease, 0);
  const corti = a.variants.corti.conditions[0];
  assert.equal(corti.insertion, 6 / 400);
  assert.equal(corti.nearCer, 30 / 400);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(corti.nearCerIncrease - 10 / 400) < 1e-12);
  assert.equal(corti.erleDb, 20);
});

test('汇总：可以直接交给 pickAec（去掉 none）；缺条件、缺处理方式、没有远端参考字、没有底线都报错', () => {
  const good = (insHyp, nearErrors) => ({ baseline: { errors: 5, refLen: 100 }, floor: floorOf({}), conditions: { a: { none: cell({}), corti: cell({ insHyp, nearErrors }) }, b: { none: cell({}), corti: cell({ insHyp, nearErrors }) } } });
  const ok = aggregateEcho([good(4, 6)]);
  const pick = pickAec(Object.entries(ok.variants).filter(([n]) => n !== 'none').map(([name, v]) => ({ name, conditions: v.conditions })));
  assert.equal(pick.status, 'ok');
  assert.equal(pick.chosen, 'corti');
  const bad = aggregateEcho([good(6, 6)]); // 插入率 6% 超过 5%
  assert.equal(pickAec(Object.entries(bad.variants).filter(([n]) => n !== 'none').map(([name, v]) => ({ name, conditions: v.conditions }))).status, 'none');

  assert.throws(() => aggregateEcho([]), /没有会议/);
  assert.throws(() => aggregateEcho([good(1, 1), { baseline: { errors: 1, refLen: 10 }, floor: floorOf({}), conditions: { a: good(1, 1).conditions.a } }]), /缺条件 b/);
  assert.throws(() => aggregateEcho([good(1, 1), { baseline: { errors: 1, refLen: 10 }, floor: floorOf({}), conditions: { a: { none: cell({}) }, b: { none: cell({}) } } }]), /缺 corti 在条件 a 下的结果/);
  assert.throws(() => aggregateEcho([{ baseline: { errors: 1, refLen: 10 }, floor: floorOf({}), conditions: { a: { none: cell({ insRef: 0 }) } } }]), /没有远端单讲段的参考字/);
  const { floor, ...noFloor } = good(1, 1);
  assert.throws(() => aggregateEcho([good(1, 1), noFloor]), /没有底线结果/);
  assert.throws(() => aggregateEcho([{ ...good(1, 1), floor: floorOf({ insRef: 0 }) }]), /底线：没有远端单讲段的参考字/);
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 3：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/echo-eval.mjs`**

```js
// 回声消除评测：合成回声的条件网格、近端段与远端单讲段的挑选、一个条件下各处理方式的评估、结果汇总。
// 处理（回声消除程序）与识别都由调用方注入（deps.aec、deps.decode），所以这里可以用假的测。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { cerTotal, normalizeText } from './cer.mjs';
import { cutPlan } from './dataset.mjs';
import { erleDb, makeRir, maskFromSegments, mixEcho } from './echo.mjs';
import { writeWavMono } from './wav.mjs';

/** 三种回声通路：直达延迟与混响时间（设计文档 3.5：延迟 20～300 ms，混响时间 0.2～0.6 秒） */
export const SCENARIOS = Object.freeze([
  { id: 'd020r02', delayMs: 20, rt60: 0.2 },
  { id: 'd100r04', delayMs: 100, rt60: 0.4 },
  { id: 'd300r06', delayMs: 300, rt60: 0.6 },
]);

/** 回声与近端语音的能量比（dB） */
export const RATIOS_DB = Object.freeze([-10, 0, 5]);

/**
 * 对齐时少平移的毫秒数。第一次冒烟时零余量对齐让 aec3 彻底失效：延迟估得再准，直达声也恰好落在参考信号的第 0 点上，
 * 回声一比参考信号早到就消不掉。探查（2 场会议各 600 秒 × 3 个条件）：零余量时 aec3 的回声插入 70%～92%、消减量为负；
 * 余量 5、10、20 ms 时 0.6%～4.4%；corti 不受余量影响。取 10 ms：盖得住延迟估计的误差，又小于最短的 20 ms 延迟。
 */
export const ALIGN_MARGIN_MS = 10;

/** 处理方式：不处理、corti-aec、aec3，后两个各有“先估计整体延迟并对齐（留 ALIGN_MARGIN_MS 余量）”与“不对齐”；corti 的滤波器长 16384 点（16 kHz 下约 1 秒，盖得住最长的 300 ms 延迟加 0.6 秒混响） */
export const VARIANTS = Object.freeze([
  { id: 'none', algo: 'none', opts: [] },
  { id: 'corti', algo: 'corti', opts: ['filter_len=16384'] },
  { id: 'corti-align', algo: 'corti', opts: ['filter_len=16384', 'align=1', `margin_ms=${ALIGN_MARGIN_MS}`] },
  { id: 'aec3', algo: 'aec3', opts: [] },
  { id: 'aec3-align', algo: 'aec3', opts: ['align=1', `margin_ms=${ALIGN_MARGIN_MS}`] },
]);

/** 九个条件：三种通路 × 三档能量比 */
export function conditions() {
  const out = [];
  for (const scenario of SCENARIOS) for (const ratioDb of RATIOS_DB) out.push({ id: `${scenario.id}_r${ratioDb}`, scenario, ratioDb });
  return out;
}

/** 只留终点不超过 endSec 的段（跨过截取终点的整段不要） */
export function excerptSegments(segments, endSec) {
  return segments.filter((s) => s.end <= endSec);
}

/**
 * 远端单讲段：对方的发言段里，与“我”的任何发言段（前后各放宽 guard 秒）都不重叠的那些。
 * 回声插入率只在这些段里量：这里“我”没有说话，识别出来的字都是回声残留。
 */
export function farOnlySegments(meSegments, otherSegments, guard = 0.25) {
  return otherSegments.filter((o) => !meSegments.some((m) => o.start < m.end + guard && m.start - guard < o.end));
}

/** 按条件合成麦克风信号：近端 + 远端经脉冲响应得到的回声（按能量比）+ 底噪。返回 { mic, echo }。 */
export function makeMic({ near, far, meSegments, otherSegments, scenario, ratioDb, rate }) {
  const rir = makeRir({ rate, delayMs: scenario.delayMs, rt60: scenario.rt60, seed: 1 });
  const nearMask = maskFromSegments(meSegments, rate, near.length);
  const farMask = maskFromSegments(otherSegments, rate, near.length);
  const { mic, echo } = mixEcho({ near, far, rir, nearMask, farMask, ratioDb, noiseDb: -40, seed: 2 });
  return { mic, echo };
}

/** 把一条信号按段切成 wav，文件名 <前缀><序号>.wav；有的段落在信号之外就报错（评测用的段都应当在截取范围内） */
export function cutSignal(samples, segments, rate, dir, prefix) {
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  const plan = cutPlan(segments, rate, samples.length);
  if (plan.length !== segments.length) throw new Error(`${prefix}：${segments.length - plan.length} 个段落在信号之外`);
  return plan.map((p) => {
    const file = path.join(dir, `${prefix}${String(p.index).padStart(4, '0')}.wav`);
    writeWavMono(file, samples.subarray(p.startFrame, p.startFrame + p.frames), rate);
    return file;
  });
}

/** 只留掩码为 1 的点，其余置零（数字静音）；返回新数组，不改原信号 */
export function keepOnly(samples, mask) {
  const out = new Float32Array(samples.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < samples.length; i++) if (mask[i]) out[i] = samples[i];
  return out;
}

/**
 * 一个条件下各处理方式：处理麦克风信号 → 远端单讲段的回声消减量 → 切出近端段并识别（所有近端文件一次交给 decode）；
 * 回声插入不能把远端单讲段逐段直接丢给识别器：没有回声、只有底噪的段，识别器自己也会吐出“好的”“嗯”之类的字（冒烟探查：没有回声时的“插入”9%～23%），
 * 量不出 5% 的门槛。所以按产品里的做法，先过 VAD 再识别：整条处理后的信号只保留远端单讲段、其余置零，交给 deps.decodeFar（VAD + 识别）。
 * deps.aec(variant, far, mic) → { samples, info }；deps.decode(files) → 与 files 同序的文本；deps.decodeFar(wav 文件) → 整条信号识别出的全部文字。
 * 返回 [{ variant, erleDb, info, nearHyps, farText }]。
 */
export async function evalVariants({ rate, far, mic, nearSegs, farOnlySegs, variants = VARIANTS, deps, dir }) {
  const farOnlyMask = maskFromSegments(farOnlySegs, rate, mic.length);
  const staged = [];
  const files = [];
  for (const v of variants) {
    const { samples, info } = v.algo === 'none' ? { samples: mic, info: null } : await deps.aec(v, far, mic);
    if (samples.length !== mic.length) throw new Error(`${v.id}：处理后长度 ${samples.length} 与输入 ${mic.length} 不同`);
    const nearFiles = cutSignal(samples, nearSegs, rate, dir, `${v.id}-near-`);
    const farFile = path.join(dir, `${v.id}-far.wav`);
    writeWavMono(farFile, keepOnly(samples, farOnlyMask), rate);
    staged.push({ variant: v.id, erleDb: v.algo === 'none' ? 0 : erleDb(mic, samples, farOnlyMask), info, nearCount: nearFiles.length, farFile });
    files.push(...nearFiles);
  }
  const texts = await deps.decode(files);
  if (texts.length !== files.length) throw new Error(`识别返回了 ${texts.length} 个结果，应当是 ${files.length} 个`);
  const out = [];
  let at = 0;
  for (const s of staged) {
    out.push({ variant: s.variant, erleDb: s.erleDb, info: s.info, nearHyps: texts.slice(at, at + s.nearCount), farText: await deps.decodeFar(s.farFile) });
    at += s.nearCount;
  }
  return out;
}

const chars = (s) => Array.from(normalizeText(s)).length;

/** 近端 CER 的计数（错误数与参考字数）与回声插入的计数（远端单讲段里识别出的字数与参考字数） */
export function scoreOutput({ nearRefs, farRefs }, { nearHyps, farText }) {
  if (nearRefs.length !== nearHyps.length) throw new Error('参考与识别结果的个数对不上');
  const near = cerTotal(nearRefs.map((r, i) => [r, nearHyps[i]]));
  return { nearErrors: near.errors, nearRefLen: near.refLen, insHyp: chars(farText), insRef: farRefs.reduce((n, r) => n + chars(r), 0) };
}

/** 干净近端（没有回声、不处理）的 CER 计数，作为“近端 CER 增加了多少”的基线 */
export function baselineCounts(nearRefs, nearHyps) {
  if (nearRefs.length !== nearHyps.length) throw new Error('参考与识别结果的个数对不上');
  const near = cerTotal(nearRefs.map((r, i) => [r, nearHyps[i]]));
  return { errors: near.errors, refLen: near.refLen };
}

/**
 * 汇总各场会议：基线 CER、没有回声时的底线，与每个（处理方式 × 条件）的 { insertion, nearCer, nearCerIncrease, erleDb }，都按总量合计。
 * 底线 = 麦克风里只有近端和底噪、没有回声时（不处理）：同一套 VAD + 识别在远端单讲段里识别出的字数 ÷ 参考字数（insertion），与近端 CER（nearCer）。
 * sessions：[{ baseline: { errors, refLen }, floor: { nearErrors, nearRefLen, insHyp, insRef }, conditions: { [条件编号]: { [处理方式]: { nearErrors, nearRefLen, insHyp, insRef, erleDb } } } }]
 * 返回 { baselineCer, floor: { insertion, nearCer }, variants: { [处理方式]: { conditions: [{ id, insertion, nearCer, nearCerIncrease, erleDb }] } } }，variants 可直接交给 decide.pickAec（去掉 none 之后）。
 */
export function aggregateEcho(sessions) {
  if (sessions.length === 0) throw new Error('没有会议可汇总');
  const base = sessions.reduce((a, s) => ({ errors: a.errors + s.baseline.errors, refLen: a.refLen + s.baseline.refLen }), { errors: 0, refLen: 0 });
  const baselineCer = base.errors / base.refLen;
  for (const s of sessions) if (!s.floor) throw new Error('有的会议没有底线结果（没有回声时的识别）');
  const floorSum = (k) => sessions.reduce((a, s) => a + s.floor[k], 0);
  if (floorSum('insRef') === 0) throw new Error('底线：没有远端单讲段的参考字，量不出底线');
  const floor = { insertion: floorSum('insHyp') / floorSum('insRef'), nearCer: floorSum('nearErrors') / floorSum('nearRefLen') };
  const ids = Object.keys(sessions[0].conditions);
  const variants = {};
  for (const id of ids) {
    for (const s of sessions) if (!s.conditions[id]) throw new Error(`有的会议缺条件 ${id}`);
    const names = Object.keys(sessions[0].conditions[id]);
    for (const name of names) {
      const cells = sessions.map((s) => {
        if (!s.conditions[id][name]) throw new Error(`有的会议缺 ${name} 在条件 ${id} 下的结果`);
        return s.conditions[id][name];
      });
      const sum = (k) => cells.reduce((a, c) => a + c[k], 0);
      if (sum('insRef') === 0) throw new Error(`${name} 在条件 ${id} 下没有远端单讲段的参考字，量不出回声插入率`);
      const nearCer = sum('nearErrors') / sum('nearRefLen');
      (variants[name] ??= { conditions: [] }).conditions.push({ id, insertion: sum('insHyp') / sum('insRef'), nearCer, nearCerIncrease: nearCer - baselineCer, erleDb: sum('erleDb') / cells.length });
    }
  }
  return { baselineCer, floor, variants };
}
```
