- [ ] **步骤 5：创建 `scripts/s4/echo.mjs`**

它需要上一个任务编译好的 `aec-harness.exe`（默认到 `<S4 目录>\target-aec\release\` 找，没有就报错并提示怎么编译），并要用 `--asr` 指定识别候选。

```js
// 回声消除评测（合成回声）。结果写在 <S4 目录>\results\echo\<会议>.json，已有的结果不重跑（加 --force 重跑）。
//   node scripts/s4/echo.mjs --home <目录> --asr <识别候选编号> [--seconds 600] [--batch 32] [--harness <aec-harness.exe>]
// 三场会议（头、中、尾）各取前 --seconds 秒：“我”的近场录音（只留自己说话的部分）当近端；其余参会者各自静音掉没说话的部分后相加当远端。
// 对 3 种回声通路 × 3 档能量比共 9 个条件，把远端经脉冲响应混进近端当作麦克风信号，分别用 不处理 / corti-aec / aec3（各有对齐与不对齐）处理，
// 再用 --asr 指定的识别器识别：近端段逐段识别量近端 CER；回声插入在远端单讲段（我没说话、对方在说）里量，
// 做法是整条处理后的信号只留远端单讲段、其余置零，交给 VAD + 识别一体程序（和产品里一样先过 VAD），数识别出的字。
// 另外每场会议还量一个“底线”：麦克风里没有回声时，同一套办法在远端单讲段里识别出多少字（VAD 偶尔把底噪当成话，识别器会吐“嗯”“好的”）。
// 要先编译 aec-harness：见 scripts/s4/aec-harness；默认在 <S4 目录>\target-aec\release\aec-harness.exe。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { decodeFiles, decodeWithVad } from './lib/asr-eval.mjs';
import { log, parseArgs, pickThree } from './lib/cli.mjs';
import { loadSessions, mixTracks, RATE } from './lib/dataset.mjs';
import { baselineCounts, conditions, cutSignal, evalVariants, excerptSegments, farOnlySegments, makeMic, SCENARIOS, scoreOutput, VARIANTS } from './lib/echo-eval.mjs';
import { homeFrom, s4Paths, writeJsonAtomic } from './lib/home.mjs';
import { measureAsync } from './lib/measure.mjs';
import { gateToSpeech } from './lib/prep.mjs';
import { ASR_CANDIDATES } from './lib/sherpa.mjs';
import { readWavChannel, readWavInfo } from './lib/wav.mjs';

const { flags } = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
const paths = s4Paths(homeFrom(flags.home));
const asrId = flags.asr;
if (!ASR_CANDIDATES.some((c) => c.id === asrId)) throw new Error(`要用 --asr 指定识别候选：${ASR_CANDIDATES.map((c) => c.id).join('、')}`);
const seconds = Number(flags.seconds ?? 600);
const batchSize = Number(flags.batch ?? 32);
const force = flags.force === true;
const harness = flags.harness ?? path.join(paths.home, 'target-aec', 'release', 'aec-harness.exe');
if (!fs.existsSync(harness)) throw new Error(`没有找到 ${harness}。先编译：cd scripts/s4/aec-harness; cargo build --release --ignore-rust-version（环境变量 CARGO_TARGET_DIR 指到 <S4 目录>\\target-aec）`);
const run = (req) => measureAsync({ priority: 'Normal', ...req });

const writeF32 = (file, samples) => fs.writeFileSync(file, Buffer.from(samples.buffer, samples.byteOffset, samples.byteLength));
function readF32(file) {
  const b = fs.readFileSync(file);
  return new Float32Array(b.buffer.slice(b.byteOffset, b.byteOffset + b.length));
}

/** 回声消除：把远端与麦克风信号写成 f32 文件交给 aec-harness，读回处理后的信号与它打印的信息 */
async function aec(v, far, mic, dir) {
  fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
  const [farF, micF, outF, stdoutFile, stderrFile] = ['far.f32', 'mic.f32', `${v.id}.f32`, `${v.id}.out.txt`, `${v.id}.err.txt`].map((n) => path.join(dir, n));
  writeF32(farF, far);
  writeF32(micF, mic);
  const m = await run({ exe: harness, args: [v.algo, String(RATE), farF, micF, outF, ...v.opts], stdoutFile, stderrFile });
  if (m.timedOut || m.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(`${v.id}：aec-harness 退出码 ${m.exitCode}，见 ${stderrFile}`);
  const line = fs.readFileSync(stdoutFile, 'utf8').trim().split(/\r?\n/).at(-1);
  const samples = readF32(outF);
  fs.rmSync(outF);
  return { samples, info: { ...JSON.parse(line), wallMs: m.wallMs, cpuMs: m.cpuMs, peakWorkingSetMb: m.peakWorkingSetMb } };
}

/** 识别：按文件长度从短到长排序后分批（官方程序一次调用的文件按同一个批次补齐解码，长短相差大时又慢又占内存），结果按原来的顺序还回去 */
async function decode(files, dir) {
  const frames = files.map((f) => readWavInfo(f).frames);
  const order = files.map((_, i) => i).sort((a, b) => frames[a] - frames[b]);
  const { texts } = await decodeFiles({ run, exe: paths.exe('sherpa-onnx-offline'), candId: asrId, modelsRoot: paths.models, threads: 8, files: order.map((i) => files[i]), outDir: path.join(dir, 'decode'), chunk: { maxCount: batchSize } });
  const out = new Array(files.length);
  order.forEach((src, k) => (out[src] = texts[k]));
  return out;
}

/** 远端单讲段的整条信号：先过 VAD 再识别（和产品里一样），返回识别出的全部文字 */
async function decodeFar(file) {
  const { segments } = await decodeWithVad({
    run,
    exe: paths.exe('sherpa-onnx-vad-with-offline-asr'),
    candId: asrId,
    modelsRoot: paths.models,
    threads: 8,
    vadModel: path.join(paths.downloads, 'silero_vad.onnx'),
    wav: file,
    outDir: path.join(path.dirname(file), 'vad'),
    tag: path.basename(file, '.wav'),
  });
  return segments.map((x) => x.text).join('');
}

const pct = (a, b) => `${((a / b) * 100).toFixed(1)}%`;

async function evalSession(s) {
  const file = path.join(paths.results, 'echo', `${s.id}.json`);
  if (fs.existsSync(file) && !force) return log(`已有，跳过：${file}`);
  const me = s.tracks.find((t) => t.speaker === s.me);
  const others = s.tracks.filter((t) => t !== me);
  const want = Math.round(seconds * RATE);
  const near = gateToSpeech(readWavChannel(me.wav, 0, 0, want).samples, me.segments);
  const n = near.length;
  const far = new Float32Array(n);
  far.set(mixTracks(others.map((t) => gateToSpeech(readWavChannel(t.wav, 0, 0, n).samples, t.segments))).samples.subarray(0, n));
  const meSegs = excerptSegments(me.segments, seconds);
  const otherSegs = excerptSegments(others.flatMap((t) => t.segments), seconds).sort((a, b) => a.start - b.start || a.end - b.end);
  const farOnly = farOnlySegments(meSegs, otherSegs);
  const refs = { nearRefs: meSegs.map((x) => x.text), farRefs: farOnly.map((x) => x.text) };
  log(`${s.id}：前 ${(n / RATE).toFixed(0)} 秒，“我”${meSegs.length} 段，对方 ${otherSegs.length} 段，其中远端单讲 ${farOnly.length} 段`);

  const base = path.join(paths.work, 'echo', s.id);
  fs.rmSync(base, { recursive: true, force: true });
  const baseline = baselineCounts(refs.nearRefs, await decode(cutSignal(near, meSegs, RATE, path.join(base, 'clean'), 'clean-'), path.join(base, 'clean')));
  log(`  基线（干净近端，不处理）CER ${((baseline.errors / baseline.refLen) * 100).toFixed(2)}%`);

  const evalAt = (scenario, ratioDb, dir, variants) => {
    const { mic } = makeMic({ near, far, meSegments: meSegs, otherSegments: otherSegs, scenario, ratioDb, rate: RATE });
    return evalVariants({ rate: RATE, far, mic, nearSegs: meSegs, farOnlySegs: farOnly, variants, deps: { aec: (v, f, m) => aec(v, f, m, dir), decode: (files) => decode(files, dir), decodeFar }, dir });
  };

  // 底线：麦克风里只有近端和底噪（能量比取负无穷 = 没有回声），不处理
  const floorDir = path.join(base, 'floor');
  const [floorRow] = await evalAt(SCENARIOS[0], -Infinity, floorDir, VARIANTS.filter((v) => v.id === 'none'));
  const floor = scoreOutput(refs, { nearHyps: floorRow.nearHyps, farText: floorRow.farText });
  fs.rmSync(floorDir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  log(`  底线（没有回声、不处理）：近端 CER ${pct(floor.nearErrors, floor.nearRefLen)}，远端单讲段里识别出 ${floor.insHyp} 字（参考 ${floor.insRef} 字，${pct(floor.insHyp, floor.insRef)}）`);

  const cells = {};
  for (const cond of conditions()) {
    const dir = path.join(base, cond.id);
    const rows = await evalAt(cond.scenario, cond.ratioDb, dir, VARIANTS);
    cells[cond.id] = Object.fromEntries(rows.map((r) => [r.variant, { ...scoreOutput(refs, { nearHyps: r.nearHyps, farText: r.farText }), farText: r.farText, erleDb: r.erleDb, aec: r.info }]));
    fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
    log(`  ${cond.id}：${rows.map((r) => `${r.variant} 消减 ${r.erleDb.toFixed(1)} dB 插入 ${pct(cells[cond.id][r.variant].insHyp, cells[cond.id][r.variant].insRef)}`).join('；')}`);
  }
  writeJsonAtomic(file, { session: s.id, me: s.me, asr: asrId, seconds: n / RATE, batchSize, baseline, floor, nearRefChars: baseline.refLen, conditions: cells, finishedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
}

for (const s of pickThree(loadSessions(paths.dataset))) await evalSession(s);
log('回声消除评测完成');
```

## 任务 17：前后处理速度测量

**目标：** 前后处理的速度：ffmpeg 把 FLAC 解成 16 kHz 单声道、官方 VAD 程序、官方标点程序。这里是测速用到的小函数：找 ffmpeg（`--ffmpeg`、环境变量 `RECORD_FFMPEG`、`vendor/ffmpeg/bin/`，不经 PATH）、取中位数、实时倍数、由两种文本长度的用时拆出“加载”与“每千个单位”。`speed.mjs` 把它们接到真实程序上。

**文件：**

- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/speed.test.mjs` — `speed.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/speed.mjs` — 前后处理速度测量的小函数
- 创建：`scripts/s4/speed.mjs` — 命令行：前后处理速度（解码、VAD、标点）

**接口：**

- 依赖：无（不依赖本工作区的其他模块）
- 提供：`findFfmpeg({ flag, env = process.env, root, exists = fs.existsSync })`；`median(xs)`；`rtfOf(wallMs, durationSec)`；`punctFit(points)`
- 测试：`speed.test.mjs` 6 个

- [ ] **步骤 1：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/speed.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `speed.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import path from 'node:path';
import { findFfmpeg, median, punctFit, rtfOf } from './speed.mjs';

const has = (...files) => (p) => files.includes(p);

test('找 ffmpeg：参数优先，其次环境变量，最后仓库里的 vendor；不去 PATH 里找', () => {
  const vendor = path.join('R', 'vendor', 'ffmpeg', 'bin', 'ffmpeg.exe');
  assert.equal(findFfmpeg({ flag: 'A.exe', env: { RECORD_FFMPEG: 'B.exe' }, root: 'R', exists: has('A.exe', 'B.exe', vendor) }), 'A.exe');
  assert.equal(findFfmpeg({ env: { RECORD_FFMPEG: 'B.exe' }, root: 'R', exists: has('B.exe', vendor) }), 'B.exe');
  assert.equal(findFfmpeg({ env: {}, root: 'R', exists: has(vendor) }), vendor);
  assert.equal(findFfmpeg({ flag: '', env: { RECORD_FFMPEG: '' }, root: 'R', exists: has(vendor) }), vendor); // 空字符串当作没给
});

test('找 ffmpeg：给了的那个不存在就报错，不换下一个；什么都没给也报错', () => {
  const vendor = path.join('R', 'vendor', 'ffmpeg', 'bin', 'ffmpeg.exe');
  assert.throws(() => findFfmpeg({ flag: 'A.exe', env: { RECORD_FFMPEG: 'B.exe' }, root: 'R', exists: has('B.exe', vendor) }), /没有找到 ffmpeg：A\.exe（不会去 PATH 里找）/);
  assert.throws(() => findFfmpeg({ env: { RECORD_FFMPEG: 'B.exe' }, root: 'R', exists: has(vendor) }), /没有找到 ffmpeg：B\.exe/);
  assert.throws(() => findFfmpeg({ env: {}, root: 'R', exists: has() }), /没有找到 ffmpeg：.*ffmpeg\.exe/);
  assert.throws(() => findFfmpeg({ env: {}, exists: has() }), /没有 ffmpeg 的位置/);
});

test('中位数：奇数个取中间，偶数个取中间两个的平均，不要求有序，不改原数组；空的报错', () => {
  assert.equal(median([3, 1, 2]), 2);
  assert.equal(median([10, 9, 100]), 10); // 按数值排，不是按字符串排
  assert.equal(median([4, 1, 3, 2]), 2.5);
  assert.equal(median([7]), 7);
  const xs = [9, 1, 5];
  median(xs);
  assert.deepEqual(xs, [9, 1, 5]);
  assert.throws(() => median([]), /没有数/);
});

test('实时倍数：毫秒数除以音频秒数再除以 1000；时长不是正数就报错', () => {
  assert.equal(rtfOf(3000, 1500), 0.002);
  assert.equal(rtfOf(0, 10), 0);
  assert.throws(() => rtfOf(1, 0), /音频时长要大于 0/);
  assert.throws(() => rtfOf(1, -3), /音频时长要大于 0/);
});

test('标点模型：由两种文本长度的用时解出加载用时与每千个单位的用时，与点的顺序无关', () => {
  const fit = punctFit([{ size: 1000, sec: 2 }, { size: 10000, sec: 11 }]);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(fit.secPer1000 - 1) < 1e-12);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(fit.loadSec - 1) < 1e-12);
  assert.deepEqual(punctFit([{ size: 10000, sec: 11 }, { size: 1000, sec: 2 }]), fit);
  // 加载项解出来是负的（测量噪声）：取 0，不返回负数
  const neg = punctFit([{ size: 1000, sec: 1 }, { size: 10000, sec: 20 }]);
  assert.equal(neg.loadSec, 0);
  assert.ok(Math.abs(neg.secPer1000 - 19 / 9) < 1e-12);
});

test('标点模型：点数不对、单位数相同、长文本反而更快，都报错', () => {
  assert.throws(() => punctFit([{ size: 1, sec: 1 }]), /恰好两个点/);
  assert.throws(() => punctFit([{ size: 1, sec: 1 }, { size: 2, sec: 1 }, { size: 3, sec: 1 }]), /恰好两个点/);
  assert.throws(() => punctFit([{ size: 1000, sec: 1 }, { size: 1000, sec: 2 }]), /一样长/);
  assert.throws(() => punctFit([{ size: 1000, sec: 3 }, { size: 10000, sec: 2 }]), /长文本 10000 个单位用时 2 秒不比短文本 1000 个单位的 3 秒长：测量噪声盖过了差别/);
  assert.throws(() => punctFit([{ size: 10000, sec: 2 }, { size: 1000, sec: 3 }]), /长文本 10000 个单位用时 2 秒不比短文本 1000 个单位的 3 秒长/); // 与点的顺序无关
  assert.throws(() => punctFit([{ size: 1000, sec: 2 }, { size: 10000, sec: 2 }]), /测量噪声盖过了差别/);
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 3：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/speed.mjs`**

```js
// 前后处理的速度测量用到的小函数：找 ffmpeg、取中位数、实时倍数、由两种文本长度的用时拆出“加载”与“每千个单位”。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

/**
 * 找 ffmpeg：--ffmpeg 参数，没有就用环境变量 RECORD_FFMPEG，再没有就用仓库里的 vendor/ffmpeg/bin/ffmpeg.exe（root 是仓库根目录）。
 * 只认第一个给出的；它不存在就报错，不悄悄换下一个，更不去 PATH 里找。
 */
export function findFfmpeg({ flag, env = process.env, root, exists = fs.existsSync }) {
  const vendor = root ? path.join(root, 'vendor', 'ffmpeg', 'bin', 'ffmpeg.exe') : undefined;
  const picked = [flag, env.RECORD_FFMPEG, vendor].find((x) => typeof x === 'string' && x !== '');
  if (!picked) throw new Error('没有 ffmpeg 的位置：用 --ffmpeg 或环境变量 RECORD_FFMPEG 指定（不会去 PATH 里找）');
  if (!exists(picked)) throw new Error(`没有找到 ffmpeg：${picked}（不会去 PATH 里找）`);
  return picked;
}

/** 中位数；个数为偶数时取中间两个的平均 */
export function median(xs) {
  if (xs.length === 0) throw new Error('没有数可取中位数');
  const s = [...xs].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const m = s.length >> 1;
  return s.length % 2 === 1 ? s[m] : (s[m - 1] + s[m]) / 2;
}

/** 实时倍数：处理用时（毫秒）÷ 音频时长（秒）÷ 1000 */
export function rtfOf(wallMs, durationSec) {
  if (!(durationSec > 0)) throw new Error(`音频时长要大于 0：${durationSec}`);
  return wallMs / 1000 / durationSec;
}

/**
 * 标点模型：每次调用 = 加载 + 每千个单位的处理。用两种文本长度各测一次，解出两项。
 * points：[{ size, sec }, { size, sec }]（顺序不限；size 是文本里的单位数，单位由调用方定，同一次测量里要一致）。
 * 单位数相同或者长的反而更快（测量噪声盖过了差别）就报错，换更长的文本或重测。
 */
export function punctFit(points) {
  if (points.length !== 2) throw new Error('要恰好两个点才能解出加载用时与每千个单位的用时');
  const [a, b] = [...points].sort((x, y) => x.size - y.size);
  if (a.size === b.size) throw new Error('两种文本一样长，解不出每千个单位的用时');
  const secPer1000 = (b.sec - a.sec) / ((b.size - a.size) / 1000);
  if (!(secPer1000 > 0)) throw new Error(`长文本 ${b.size} 个单位用时 ${b.sec} 秒不比短文本 ${a.size} 个单位的 ${a.sec} 秒长：测量噪声盖过了差别，重测或换更长的文本`);
  const loadSec = a.sec - (secPer1000 * a.size) / 1000;
  return { loadSec: Math.max(0, loadSec), secPer1000 };
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 5：创建 `scripts/s4/speed.mjs`**

```js
// 前后处理的速度：ffmpeg 把 FLAC 解成 16 kHz 单声道、官方 VAD 程序、官方标点程序。结果写在 <S4 目录>\results\speed.json。
//   node scripts/s4/speed.mjs --home <目录> [--ffmpeg <ffmpeg.exe>] [--runs 3] [--force]
// 这是计时：跑的时候别的程序不要占 CPU（别的程序的占用记在结果里）。每项测 --runs 次取中位数。
// 输入：第一场会议里“我”的那条近场录音（16 kHz 单声道），先用 ffmpeg 变成 48 kHz 立体声的 FLAC（和录音软件的系统声音轨一个格式，是较重的一种），再计时解回 16 kHz 单声道。
// 标点程序：中文文本经命令行传给官方程序时，会被本机的 ANSI 代码页（GBK）转一次，程序按 UTF-8 读，全是乱码，所以不能拿真实的中文来测；
// 改用英文文本当替身（两种长度：400 与 4000 词），算出“每千个词”的用时，换算时 1 个词按 1 个汉字算。这是个替身，结果里写明；它的用时本来就很小（见结果）。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { cpuSnapshot, othersLoad } from './lib/cpu.mjs';
import { log, parseArgs } from './lib/cli.mjs';
import { loadSessions } from './lib/dataset.mjs';
import { homeFrom, s4Paths, writeJsonAtomic } from './lib/home.mjs';
import { measureAsync } from './lib/measure.mjs';
import { findFfmpeg, median, punctFit, rtfOf } from './lib/speed.mjs';

const { flags } = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
if (flags.ffmpeg === true) throw new Error('--ffmpeg 后面要跟 ffmpeg.exe 的路径');
const paths = s4Paths(homeFrom(flags.home));
const root = path.join(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..');
const ffmpeg = findFfmpeg({ flag: flags.ffmpeg, root });
const runs = Number(flags.runs ?? 3);
if (!Number.isInteger(runs) || runs < 1) throw new Error(`--runs 要是正整数：${flags.runs}`);
const file = path.join(paths.results, 'speed.json');
if (fs.existsSync(file) && flags.force !== true) {
  log(`已有，跳过：${file}`);
  process.exit(0);
}

const work = path.join(paths.work, 'speed');
fs.rmSync(work, { recursive: true, force: true });
fs.mkdirSync(work, { recursive: true });

/** 起一个程序、计时，返回 { wallMs, cpuMs, peakWorkingSetMb, othersLoad }；失败（退出码非零、超时）直接报错 */
async function timed(what, exe, args, tag) {
  const stdoutFile = path.join(work, `${tag}.out.txt`);
  const stderrFile = path.join(work, `${tag}.err.txt`);
  const before = cpuSnapshot();
  const m = await measureAsync({ exe, args, stdoutFile, stderrFile, priority: 'Normal', timeoutSec: 900 });
  const others = othersLoad(before, cpuSnapshot(), m.cpuMs);
  if (m.timedOut || m.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error(`${what}：退出码 ${m.exitCode}${m.timedOut ? '（超时）' : ''}，见 ${stderrFile}`);
  return { wallMs: m.wallMs, cpuMs: m.cpuMs, peakWorkingSetMb: m.peakWorkingSetMb, othersLoad: others };
}

async function repeat(what, exe, args, tag) {
  const out = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < runs; i++) {
    out.push(await timed(what, exe, args, `${tag}-${i}`));
    log(`  ${what} 第 ${i + 1}/${runs} 次：${(out.at(-1).wallMs / 1000).toFixed(2)} 秒，别的程序占用 ${(out.at(-1).othersLoad * 100).toFixed(0)}%`);
  }
  return out;
}

const sessions = loadSessions(paths.dataset);
const session = sessions[0];
const track = session.tracks.find((t) => t.speaker === session.me);
const flac = path.join(work, 'test.mka');
const wav16 = path.join(work, 'test16k.wav');

log(`输入：${session.id} 的 ${track.speaker}，${track.durationSec.toFixed(1)} 秒`);
await timed('生成 FLAC', ffmpeg, ['-nostdin', '-y', '-i', track.wav, '-ar', '48000', '-ac', '2', '-c:a', 'flac', flac], 'make-flac');
await timed('ffmpeg 版本', ffmpeg, ['-version'], 'version');
const version = fs.readFileSync(path.join(work, 'version.out.txt'), 'utf8');
const decodeRuns = await repeat('ffmpeg 解 FLAC 并转 16 kHz 单声道', ffmpeg, ['-nostdin', '-y', '-i', flac, '-ac', '1', '-ar', '16000', '-c:a', 'pcm_s16le', wav16], 'decode');
const vadRuns = await repeat('VAD', paths.exe('sherpa-onnx-vad'), [`--silero-vad-model=${path.join(paths.downloads, 'silero_vad.onnx')}`, wav16, path.join(work, 'vad-out.wav')], 'vad');

const sentence = 'we will discuss the current state of the catering industry and its future development';
const words = sentence.split(' ');
const proxy = (n) => Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => words[i % words.length]).join(' ');
const model = path.join(paths.models, 'sherpa-onnx-punct-ct-transformer-zh-en-vocab272727-2024-04-12-int8', 'model.int8.onnx');
const sizes = [400, 4000];
const punctRuns = {};
for (const n of sizes) punctRuns[n] = await repeat(`标点（英文替身 ${n} 词）`, paths.exe('sherpa-onnx-offline-punctuation'), [`--ct-transformer=${model}`, proxy(n)], `punct-${n}`);
const fit = punctFit(sizes.map((n) => ({ size: n, sec: median(punctRuns[n].map((r) => r.wallMs)) / 1000 })));

const summary = (list) => ({ runs: list, medianWallMs: median(list.map((r) => r.wallMs)), peakWorkingSetMb: Math.max(...list.map((r) => r.peakWorkingSetMb)), othersLoadMean: list.reduce((a, r) => a + r.othersLoad, 0) / list.length });
const decode = summary(decodeRuns);
const vad = summary(vadRuns);
writeJsonAtomic(file, {
  track: { session: session.id, speaker: track.speaker, durationSec: track.durationSec },
  ffmpegVersion: version.split(/\r?\n/)[0],
  decode: { ...decode, rtf: rtfOf(decode.medianWallMs, track.durationSec), format: '48 kHz 立体声 FLAC → 16 kHz 单声道 wav' },
  vad: { ...vad, rtf: rtfOf(vad.medianWallMs, track.durationSec) },
  punct: { proxy: '英文文本，1 个词按 1 个汉字算（中文参数经 ANSI 代码页转换后程序读不了）', sizes, bySize: Object.fromEntries(sizes.map((n) => [n, summary(punctRuns[n])])), loadSec: fit.loadSec, secPer1000: fit.secPer1000 },
  runs,
  finishedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
});
for (const f of ['test.mka', 'test16k.wav', 'vad-out.wav']) fs.rmSync(path.join(work, f), { force: true });
log(`解码实时倍数 ${rtfOf(decode.medianWallMs, track.durationSec).toFixed(4)}；VAD 实时倍数 ${rtfOf(vad.medianWallMs, track.durationSec).toFixed(4)}；标点 加载 ${fit.loadSec.toFixed(2)} 秒 + 每千词 ${fit.secPer1000.toFixed(3)} 秒`);
```

## 任务 18：结果汇总与排版

**目标：** `report`：从各阶段的结果算出设计文档 3.9 需要的数字，并按 `decide.mjs` 里事先写死的门槛与决策规则下结论——纯函数，缺哪一项都报错并说明先跑什么，不拿残缺的数字下结论。`render`：把汇总写成 Markdown，只负责排版，数字全部来自汇总结果，达标与否的结论来自决策规则（这里不重新比大小，免得和规则在边界上不一致）。`fixtures` 是这两个模块的测试共用的造数据函数（评测集 8 场会议，远端说话人数与真实评测集一致：三场 1 人、一场 2 人、四场 3 人）。`report.mjs` 把全部结果读进来、汇总并写出 `report.md`。识别评测的命令行脚本 `asr.mjs` 也放在这个任务里写：它要用 `report` 里的 `finalists`、`asrOnlyMinutes`、`speechFractions` 来选决赛圈、估计预筛用时（所以不能放在任务 13）。

**文件：**

- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/fixtures.mjs` — 汇总与排版的测试共用的造数据函数（只给测试用）
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/report.test.mjs` — `report.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/report.mjs` — 各阶段结果的汇总与结论
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/render.test.mjs` — `render.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/render.mjs` — 把汇总写成 Markdown（填设计文档 3.9 用）
- 创建：`scripts/s4/asr.mjs` — 命令行：识别评测（prescreen、full、vad）
- 创建：`scripts/s4/report.mjs` — 命令行：汇总全部结果、按事先写死的门槛下结论、写出 report.md

**接口：**

- 依赖：`cer.mjs`、`decide.mjs`、`diar-eval.mjs`、`echo-eval.mjs`、`estimate.mjs`
- 提供：`MEETING_MIN`；`PUNCT_RATE_MIN`；`speechFractions(sessions)`；`asrOnlyMinutes(speechFrac, rtf, meetingMin = MEETING_MIN)`；`peakMemGb(summary)`；`finalists({ cands, speechFrac, n = 3 })`；`charsPerSpeechSec(sessions)`；`meetingChars({ charsPerSec, speechFrac, meetingMin = MEETING_MIN })`；`punctMinutes(punct, chars)`；`asrRow(cand, ctx)`；`asrSection(cands, ctx)`；`diarSection({ sweep, confirm = [], far = [], rotations = [], timing = null, sessionCount })`；`aecRtf(sessionResults, variant)`；`echoSection(sessionResults)`；`buildReport({ sessions, speed, candidates, diar, echo })`；`pct`；`table(header, rows)`；`renderReport(r, { generatedAt })`
- 测试：`report.test.mjs` 31 个；`render.test.mjs` 13 个

- [ ] **步骤 1：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/fixtures.mjs`**

```js
// 汇总（report.mjs）与排版（render.mjs）的测试共用的造数据函数；只给测试用。
// 评测集里 8 场会议，远端说话人数与真实评测集一致：三场 1 人、一场 2 人、四场 3 人。
export const summaryOf = (rtf, { ws = 500, priv = 400, load = 3 } = {}) => ({ rtf, loadSecMean: load, peakWorkingSetMb: ws, peakPrivateMb: priv });
export const fullOf = (cer, rtf, opts = {}, punctRate = 0) => ({ score: { cer: { rate: cer } }, summary: summaryOf(rtf, opts), punctRate });
export const vadOf = (cer, wallSec, audioSec, { ws = 500, priv = 400 } = {}) => ({ score: { cer: { rate: cer } }, summary: { wallSec, audioSec, peakWorkingSetMb: ws, peakPrivateMb: priv } });
export const pre8 = { score: { cer: { rate: 0.09 } }, summary: { rtf: 0.12 } };

// ---- 说话人分离：造 8 场会议的结果。真实人数（远端说话人数）与评测集一致：三场 1 人、一场 2 人、四场 3 人 ----
export const MIXES = ['s1', 's2', 's3', 's4', 's5', 's6', 's7', 's8'];
export const ACTUAL = { s1: 1, s2: 1, s3: 1, s4: 2, s5: 3, s6: 3, s7: 3, s8: 3 };
export const dr = (embedding, mixId, { threshold = null, clusters = null, der, est }) => ({
  key: `${mixId}__${embedding}__${clusters !== null ? `k${clusters}` : `t${threshold.toFixed(2)}`}`,
  mixId,
  embedding,
  threshold,
  clusters,
  total: 100,
  miss: der * 50,
  fa: der * 25,
  conf: der * 25,
  der,
  actualSpeakers: ACTUAL[mixId],
  estimatedSpeakers: est ? est(mixId) : ACTUAL[mixId],
  estimatedSpeakers3s: est ? est(mixId) : ACTUAL[mixId],
});
export const sweepOf = (table, est) => Object.entries(table).flatMap(([model, byT]) => Object.entries(byT).flatMap(([t, der]) => MIXES.map((s) => dr(model, s, { threshold: Number(t), der, est }))));
// m1 的最低点在 0.5（0.10），0.7 也只高 0.5 个百分点：区间 0.5～0.7，中点 0.6 不在已测的阈值里
export const TABLE = { m1: { 0.3: 0.2, 0.5: 0.1, 0.7: 0.105, 0.9: 0.3 }, m2: { 0.3: 0.25, 0.5: 0.12, 0.7: 0.13, 0.9: 0.4 } };
export const overCluster = (s) => (ACTUAL[s] === 3 ? 6 : ACTUAL[s]); // 三个远端说话人的场次都估成 6 个：人数相差 3，不合格

export const ecell = (o) => ({ nearErrors: 5, nearRefLen: 100, insHyp: 2, insRef: 100, erleDb: 20, farText: '', aec: { wallMs: 1000 }, ...o });
export const esession = (session, seconds, conditions) => ({ session, seconds, baseline: { errors: 5, refLen: 100 }, floor: { nearErrors: 5, nearRefLen: 100, insHyp: 1, insRef: 100 }, conditions });
export const none = (o) => ecell({ insHyp: 100, erleDb: 0, aec: null, ...o });

// ---- 汇总：8 场的评测集（“我”说 100 秒、对方说 200 秒，各 1000 秒）加各阶段的结果 ----
export const dataset = MIXES.map((id) => ({
  id,
  me: 'ME',
  tracks: [
    { speaker: 'ME', durationSec: 1000, segments: [{ start: 0, end: 100, text: '一二三四五' }] },
    { speaker: 'R1', durationSec: 1000, segments: [{ start: 0, end: 200, text: '一二三四五六七八' }] },
  ],
}));
export const candidate = (id, cer, vad) => ({ id, name: id.toUpperCase(), prescreen8: pre8, prescreen4: null, full: fullOf(cer, 0.1, { load: 3 }), vad });
export const inputs = (over = {}) => ({
  sessions: dataset,
  speed: { decode: { rtf: 0.002 }, vad: { rtf: 0.005 }, punct: { loadSec: 0.6, secPer1000: 0.12 } },
  candidates: [candidate('a', 0.1, vadOf(0.11, 6, 100)), candidate('b', 0.2, null)],
  diar: {
    sweep: sweepOf(TABLE),
    confirm: MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.108 })),
    far: MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.28 })),
    rotations: ['s1', 's2', 's3'].map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.2 })),
    timing: { summary: { rtfWall: 0.07, peakWorkingSetMb: 300, othersLoadMean: 0.3 } },
  },
  echo: MIXES.map((s) => ({ ...esession(s, 600, { c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ aec: { wallMs: 3000 } }) } }), asr: 'a' })),
  ...over,
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 2：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/report.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `report.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { ACTUAL, candidate, dataset, dr, ecell, esession, fullOf, inputs, MIXES, none, overCluster, pre8, sweepOf, TABLE, vadOf } from './fixtures.mjs';
import { aecRtf, asrOnlyMinutes, asrRow, asrSection, buildReport, charsPerSpeechSec, diarSection, echoSection, finalists, MEETING_MIN, meetingChars, peakMemGb, PUNCT_RATE_MIN, punctMinutes, speechFractions } from './report.mjs';

const track = (speaker, durationSec, segs) => ({ speaker, durationSec, segments: segs.map(([start, end]) => ({ start, end })) });

test('说话占比：各场合在一起算；“我”的轨与“其余人合起来”的轨分开；重叠的说话只算一次', () => {
  const sessions = [
    // 100 秒：我说 20 秒；对方两人 30 + 20 秒，重叠 10 秒 → 合起来 40 秒
    { me: 'A', tracks: [track('A', 100, [[0, 20]]), track('B', 100, [[10, 40]]), track('C', 100, [[30, 50]])] },
    // 300 秒：我说 100 秒；对方 50 秒
    { me: 'D', tracks: [track('D', 300, [[0, 60], [100, 140]]), track('E', 300, [[200, 250]])] },
  ];
  const f = speechFractions(sessions);
  assert.equal(f.me, (20 + 100) / (100 + 300));
  assert.equal(f.sys, (40 + 50) / (100 + 300));
});

test('说话占比：“我”自己的标注段重叠时也只算一次', () => {
  const f = speechFractions([{ me: 'A', tracks: [track('A', 100, [[0, 10], [5, 15]]), track('B', 100, [[50, 60]])] }]);
  assert.equal(f.me, 15 / 100);
  assert.equal(f.sys, 10 / 100);
});

test('说话占比：对方音轨长短不一时取最长的作分母；没有会议、没有“我”、只有“我”都报错', () => {
  const s = { me: 'A', tracks: [track('A', 100, [[0, 10]]), track('B', 90, [[0, 9]]), track('C', 120, [[0, 12]])] };
  assert.equal(speechFractions([s]).sys, (12) / 120);
  assert.throws(() => speechFractions([]), /没有会议/);
  assert.throws(() => speechFractions([{ me: 'Z', tracks: [track('A', 1, [])] }]), /没有“我”：Z/);
  assert.throws(() => speechFractions([{ me: 'A', tracks: [track('A', 1, [])] }]), /只有“我”一个人/);
});

test('只算识别的用时：两条轨的说话时间 × 实时倍数；默认按 60 分钟', () => {
  assert.equal(MEETING_MIN, 60);
  assert.equal(asrOnlyMinutes({ me: 0.3, sys: 0.7 }, 0.1), 6);
  assert.equal(asrOnlyMinutes({ me: 0.3, sys: 0.7 }, 0.1, 30), 3);
});

test('峰值内存：工作集与私有内存取大的，1 GB = 1024 MB', () => {
  assert.equal(peakMemGb({ peakWorkingSetMb: 2048, peakPrivateMb: 1024 }), 2);
  assert.equal(peakMemGb({ peakWorkingSetMb: 1024, peakPrivateMb: 3072 }), 3);
});

const full = (cer, rtf, wsMb = 500, privMb = 400) => ({ score: { cer: { rate: cer } }, summary: { rtf, peakWorkingSetMb: wsMb, peakPrivateMb: privMb } });
const frac = { me: 0.4, sys: 0.6 }; // 合计 1.0：只算识别的用时 = 60 × rtf 分钟

test('决赛圈：速度与内存都达标的里面 CER 最低的前三；跳过的、没跑的不参加', () => {
  const cands = [
    { id: 'a', full: full(0.1, 0.1) }, // 6 分钟
    { id: 'b', full: full(0.05, 0.6) }, // 36 分钟：太慢
    { id: 'c', full: full(0.07, 0.2, 5000, 100) }, // 12 分钟，但内存 4.9 GB：太大
    { id: 'd', full: full(0.12, 0.1) },
    { id: 'e', full: { skipped: true, reason: '太慢' } },
    { id: 'f', full: null },
    { id: 'g', full: full(0.08, 0.3) }, // 18 分钟
    { id: 'h', full: full(0.2, 0.01) },
  ];
  const r = finalists({ cands, speechFrac: frac });
  assert.deepEqual(r.map((x) => x.id), ['g', 'a', 'd']);
  assert.deepEqual(r[0], { id: 'g', cer: 0.08, asrOnlyMin: 60 * 0.3 * 1.0, peakMemGb: 500 / 1024 });
  assert.deepEqual(finalists({ cands, speechFrac: frac, n: 1 }).map((x) => x.id), ['g']);
  assert.deepEqual(finalists({ cands, speechFrac: frac, n: 10 }).map((x) => x.id), ['g', 'a', 'd', 'h']);
});

test('决赛圈：刚好 30 分钟、刚好 4 GB 算达标；CER 一样时按给定的先后', () => {
  const edge = [
    { id: 'x', full: full(0.1, 0.5, 4096, 100) }, // 30 分钟，4 GB
    { id: 'y', full: full(0.1, 0.5001) }, // 30.006 分钟
    { id: 'z', full: full(0.1, 0.1, 4097, 100) }, // 超过 4 GB 一点点
    { id: 'w', full: full(0.1, 0.1) },
  ];
  assert.deepEqual(finalists({ cands: edge, speechFrac: frac }).map((x) => x.id), ['x', 'w']);
  assert.deepEqual(finalists({ cands: [], speechFrac: frac }), []);
});

const near = (a, b, eps = 1e-9) => assert.ok(Math.abs(a - b) < eps, `${a} 与 ${b} 相差超过 ${eps}`);

test('转写稿字数密度：规整后的参考字数 ÷ 各条音轨自己的说话时长之和；同一条音轨里重叠的说话只算一次', () => {
  const seg = (start, end, text) => ({ start, end, text });
  // 4 + 2 + 6（abc 与 123 变成的“一二三”）= 12 字；20 + 10 = 30 秒
  near(charsPerSpeechSec([{ tracks: [{ segments: [seg(0, 10, '你好，世界！'), seg(10, 20, '再见')] }, { segments: [seg(5, 15, 'abc 123')] }] }]), 12 / 30);
  near(charsPerSpeechSec([{ tracks: [{ segments: [seg(0, 10, '一二三四五'), seg(5, 15, '六七八九十')] }] }]), 10 / 15); // 重叠的 5 秒只算一次
  near(charsPerSpeechSec([{ tracks: [{ segments: [seg(0, 10, '一二')] }] }, { tracks: [{ segments: [seg(0, 30, '三四五六')] }] }]), 6 / 40); // 各场合在一起算
  assert.throws(() => charsPerSpeechSec([{ tracks: [{ segments: [] }] }]), /没有说话时间/);
});

test('一场会议的转写稿字数与加标点的用时', () => {
  near(meetingChars({ charsPerSec: 0.4, speechFrac: { me: 0.3, sys: 0.7 } }), 0.4 * 1.0 * 60 * 60);
  near(meetingChars({ charsPerSec: 2, speechFrac: { me: 0.25, sys: 0.25 }, meetingMin: 30 }), 2 * 0.5 * 30 * 60);
  near(punctMinutes({ loadSec: 0.6, secPer1000: 0.12 }, 25000), (0.6 + 0.12 * 25) / 60);
});

const ctx = { speechFrac: { me: 0.4, sys: 0.6 }, rtf: { decode: 0.002, vad: 0.005, aec: 0.03, diar: 0.1 }, useAec: false, diarMic: false, punct: { loadSec: 0.6, secPer1000: 0.12 }, chars: 25000 };

test('识别候选的一行：批识别估计 = 解码 + VAD + 分离 + 识别 + 两次加载 + （不带标点时）加标点', () => {
  const row = asrRow({ id: 'a', name: 'A', prescreen8: pre8, prescreen4: { summary: { rtf: 0.15 } }, full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1), vad: null }, ctx);
  assert.equal(row.status, 'ok');
  near(row.stages.decode, 2 * 60 * 0.002);
  near(row.stages.vad, 2 * 60 * 0.005);
  near(row.stages.diar, 60 * 0.1);
  near(row.stages.asr, 1.0 * 60 * 0.1);
  assert.equal(row.stages.aec, 0);
  assert.equal(row.needsPunct, true);
  near(row.punctMin, (0.6 + 0.12 * 25) / 60);
  near(row.batchMin, 0.24 + 0.6 + 6 + 6 + (2 * 3) / 60 + 0.06);
  assert.equal(row.e2e, null);
  near(row.estMinutes, row.batchMin);
  assert.deepEqual([row.cer, row.rtf8, row.loadSec, row.punctRate], [0.1, 0.1, 3, 0]);
  near(row.peakMemGb, 500 / 1024);
  // 预筛（三场会议、每人前 5 分钟的标注段）：CER、两档线程的实时倍数、按 8 线程预筛算的“只算识别的估计用时”
  near(row.pre.cer, 0.09);
  near(row.pre.rtf8, 0.12);
  near(row.pre.rtf4, 0.15);
  near(row.pre.asrOnlyMin, 60 * 0.12);
  assert.equal(asrRow({ id: 'a', name: 'A', prescreen8: pre8, full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1), vad: null }, ctx).pre.rtf4, null); // 没做 4 线程的预筛
  assert.equal(asrRow({ id: 'a', name: 'A', full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1), vad: null }, ctx).pre, null); // 没做预筛
});

test('识别候选的一行：识别器自己带标点（占比不低于 1%）就不另加标点的用时', () => {
  assert.equal(PUNCT_RATE_MIN, 0.01);
  const at = (rate) => asrRow({ id: 'a', name: 'A', full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1, {}, rate), vad: null }, ctx);
  assert.equal(at(0.2).needsPunct, false);
  assert.equal(at(0.2).punctMin, 0);
  near(at(0.2).batchMin, 0.24 + 0.6 + 6 + 6 + 0.1);
  assert.equal(at(PUNCT_RATE_MIN).needsPunct, false); // 恰好 1% 算带标点
  assert.equal(at(PUNCT_RATE_MIN - 1e-6).needsPunct, true);
});

test('识别候选的一行：做过 VAD 端到端的取两种估计里大的，内存也取大的', () => {
  const slow = asrRow({ id: 'a', name: 'A', full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1, { ws: 2048, priv: 1024 }), vad: vadOf(0.11, 30, 100, { ws: 1024, priv: 3072 }) }, ctx);
  near(slow.e2e.rtfWall, 0.3);
  near(slow.e2e.totalMin, 0.24 + 6 + 2 * 60 * 0.3 + 0.06); // 解码 + 分离 + 两条轨的端到端 + 加标点；VAD 与识别不再单算
  assert.equal(slow.e2e.cer, 0.11);
  near(slow.e2e.peakMemGb, 3);
  near(slow.estMinutes, 42.3);
  near(slow.peakMemGb, 3);
  const fast = asrRow({ id: 'a', name: 'A', full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1, { ws: 2048, priv: 1024 }), vad: vadOf(0.11, 1, 100, { ws: 512, priv: 256 }) }, ctx);
  near(fast.e2e.totalMin, 0.24 + 6 + 1.2 + 0.06);
  near(fast.estMinutes, fast.batchMin); // 端到端更快：取批识别估计
  near(fast.peakMemGb, 2); // 批识别的内存更大
});

test('识别候选的一行：做回声消除与麦克风轨也分人时，对应阶段加进估计（批识别与端到端都加）', () => {
  const both = { ...ctx, useAec: true, diarMic: true };
  const row = asrRow({ id: 'a', name: 'A', full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1), vad: vadOf(0.1, 30, 100) }, both);
  near(row.stages.aec, 60 * 0.03);
  near(row.stages.diar, 2 * 60 * 0.1);
  near(row.e2e.totalMin, 0.24 + 1.8 + 12 + 36 + 0.06);
});

test('识别候选的一行：没跑、被跳过、没有加载用时都明确说出来', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(asrRow({ id: 'x', name: 'X', full: null }, ctx), { id: 'x', name: 'X', status: 'not-run', pre: null });
  assert.deepEqual(asrRow({ id: 'x', name: 'X', full: { skipped: true, reason: '太慢' } }, ctx), { id: 'x', name: 'X', status: 'skipped', reason: '太慢', pre: null });
  // 被跳过的候选只有预筛的数字，照样带在行里
  const skipped = asrRow({ id: 'x', name: 'X', prescreen8: pre8, prescreen4: { summary: { rtf: 0.2 } }, full: { skipped: true, reason: '太慢' } }, ctx);
  assert.deepEqual([skipped.pre.cer, skipped.pre.rtf8, skipped.pre.rtf4], [0.09, 0.12, 0.2]);
  near(skipped.pre.asrOnlyMin, 7.2);
  assert.equal(asrRow({ id: 'x', name: 'X', prescreen8: pre8, full: null }, ctx).pre.rtf4, null);
  assert.throws(() => asrRow({ id: 'x', name: 'X', full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1, { load: null }) }, ctx), /x：没有解析到模型加载用时/);
});

test('识别部分：只有跑完全量的候选参加挑选；CER 差在 0.5 个百分点内取更快的', () => {
  const c = (id, cer, rtf) => ({ id, name: id.toUpperCase(), full: fullOf(cer, rtf, { load: 2 }) });
  const cands = [c('a', 0.1, 0.2), c('b', 0.104, 0.05), c('c', 0.2, 0.01), { id: 'd', name: 'D', full: null }, { id: 'e', name: 'E', full: { skipped: true, reason: 'x' } }];
  const s = asrSection(cands, ctx);
  assert.deepEqual(s.rows.map((r) => [r.id, r.status]), [['a', 'ok'], ['b', 'ok'], ['c', 'ok'], ['d', 'not-run'], ['e', 'skipped']]);
  assert.equal(s.pick.status, 'ok');
  assert.equal(s.pick.chosen.name, 'b');
  assert.equal(s.pick.chosen.estMinutes, s.rows[1].estMinutes);
  // 都太慢：没有候选达标
  const slow = asrSection([c('a', 0.1, 1.0)], ctx);
  assert.equal(slow.pick.status, 'none-eligible');
  // 都不够准：速度达标的里最好的 CER 仍高于 15%
  const bad = asrSection([c('a', 0.2, 0.1)], ctx);
  assert.equal(bad.pick.status, 'cer-gate-failed');
});

test('识别部分：达标与否看“批识别与端到端取大”的用时；CER 用全量的，端到端的 CER 只报告不参加挑选', () => {
  const cand = (id, cer, vad) => ({ id, name: id.toUpperCase(), full: fullOf(cer, 0.1, { load: 3 }), vad });
  // a：批识别 13 分钟，但端到端 42.3 分钟，超过 30：不达标；b：CER 差一点，两种估计都达标
  const s = asrSection([cand('a', 0.1, vadOf(0.1, 30, 100)), cand('b', 0.12, vadOf(0.12, 1, 100))], ctx);
  assert.ok(s.rows[0].batchMin < 30 && s.rows[0].e2e.totalMin > 30);
  assert.equal(s.pick.chosen.name, 'b');
  // c 的端到端 CER 很差，但全量的 CER 最好：照样选 c
  const t = asrSection([cand('c', 0.1, vadOf(0.3, 1, 100)), cand('d', 0.12, vadOf(0.12, 1, 100))], ctx);
  assert.equal(t.pick.chosen.name, 'c');
  assert.equal(t.pick.chosen.cer, 0.1);
});

test('分离部分：中点不在已测的阈值上又没补测：只给出胜出的模型与阈值，不下结论', () => {
  const d = diarSection({ sweep: sweepOf(TABLE), sessionCount: 8 });
  assert.equal(d.pick.model, 'm1');
  near(d.pick.threshold, 0.6);
  assert.equal(d.pick.needsConfirm, true);
  assert.equal(d.status, 'needs-confirm');
  assert.equal(d.point, null);
  assert.equal(d.gate, null);
  assert.deepEqual(d.groups, []);
  assert.deepEqual(d.perSession, []);
  assert.equal(d.far, null);
  assert.equal(d.rotations, null);
  assert.equal(d.timing, null);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(d.curves).sort(), ['m1', 'm2']);
});

test('分离部分：补测之后用中点的结果核对门槛；补测里别的模型与阈值的结果不算；缺场次报错', () => {
  const confirm = MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.108 }));
  const stray = [...MIXES.map((s) => dr('m2', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.5 })), ...MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.65, der: 0.5 }))];
  const d = diarSection({ sweep: sweepOf(TABLE), confirm: [...[...confirm].reverse(), ...stray], sessionCount: 8 }); // 结果的顺序是乱的，输出按会议编号排
  assert.equal(d.status, 'pass');
  near(d.point.der, 0.108);
  assert.equal(d.point.countHits, 8);
  assert.deepEqual(d.gate, { derOk: true, countOk: true, pass: true });
  assert.deepEqual(d.perSession.map((r) => [r.mixId, r.actual, r.estimated]), MIXES.map((s) => [s, ACTUAL[s], ACTUAL[s]]));
  assert.deepEqual(d.groups.map((g) => [g.actual, g.sessions, g.countHits]), [[1, 3, 3], [2, 1, 1], [3, 4, 4]]);
  near(d.groups[0].der, 0.108);
  assert.throws(() => diarSection({ sweep: sweepOf(TABLE), confirm: confirm.slice(1), sessionCount: 8 }), /中点 0\.6 的补测只有 7\/8 场/);
});

test('分离部分：DER 达标而人数不合格（把远端说话人数估多了）：结论是不通过，两项分开报', () => {
  const sweep = sweepOf(TABLE, overCluster);
  const confirm = MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.108, est: overCluster }));
  const d = diarSection({ sweep, confirm, sessionCount: 8 });
  assert.equal(d.status, 'fail');
  assert.deepEqual(d.gate, { derOk: true, countOk: false, pass: false });
  assert.equal(d.point.countHits, 4);
  assert.deepEqual(d.groups.map((g) => [g.actual, g.countHits]), [[1, 3], [2, 1], [3, 0]]);
  assert.deepEqual(d.perSession.filter((r) => r.actual === 3).map((r) => r.estimated), [6, 6, 6, 6]);
  const high = diarSection({ sweep: sweepOf({ m1: { 0.5: 0.3, 0.6: 0.4, 0.7: 0.5 } }), sessionCount: 8 }); // 最低点 30% 就是区间中点，已测过
  assert.equal(high.status, 'fail');
  assert.deepEqual(high.gate, { derOk: false, countOk: true, pass: false }); // DER 超过 25%
});

test('分离部分：区间中点算出来带浮点误差时，补测写的阈值仍然对得上', () => {
  // 区间 0.3～0.6，中点 (0.3 + 0.6) / 2 = 0.44999999999999996，补测写的是 0.45
  const table = { m1: { 0.3: 0.105, 0.4: 0.1, 0.5: 0.103, 0.6: 0.104, 0.7: 0.3 } };
  const confirm = MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.45, der: 0.108 }));
  const d = diarSection({ sweep: sweepOf(table), confirm, sessionCount: 8 });
  assert.equal(d.pick.needsConfirm, true);
  assert.notEqual(d.pick.threshold, 0.45);
  near(d.pick.threshold, 0.45);
  assert.equal(d.status, 'pass');
  near(d.point.der, 0.108);
});

test('分离部分：区间中点本来就测过，直接用扫描里的结果（已知人数的结果不混进来）', () => {
  const table = { m1: { 0.3: 0.2, 0.5: 0.1, 0.6: 0.102, 0.7: 0.105, 0.9: 0.3 } };
  const known = MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { clusters: ACTUAL[s], der: 0.01 }));
  const d = diarSection({ sweep: [...sweepOf(table), ...known], sessionCount: 8 });
  assert.equal(d.pick.needsConfirm, false);
  assert.equal(d.status, 'pass');
  near(d.point.der, 0.102);
  // 补测与不补测两条路径给出的 point 字段一样全（渲染要用漏检、误检、混淆、场数与人数误差）
  near(d.point.miss, 0.051);
  near(d.point.fa, 0.0255);
  near(d.point.conf, 0.0255);
  assert.deepEqual([d.point.sessions, d.point.countHits, d.point.countMeanAbsError], [8, 8, 0]);
  assert.deepEqual(d.groups.map((g) => [g.actual, g.sessions, g.countHits]), [[1, 3, 3], [2, 1, 1], [3, 4, 4]]);
  assert.equal(d.perSession.length, 8);
  near(d.perSession[0].der, 0.102);
  near(d.curves.m1.knownCount.der, 0.01);
});

test('分离部分：远场、轮换、计时各自汇总；远场的场次不齐就报错', () => {
  const confirm = MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.108 }));
  const far = (der) => MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der }));
  const rotations = ['s1', 's2', 's3'].flatMap((s) => [0.2, 0.3].map((der) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der })));
  const timing = { summary: { rtfWall: 0.07, peakWorkingSetMb: 300 } };
  const d = diarSection({ sweep: sweepOf(TABLE), confirm, far: far(0.28), rotations, timing, sessionCount: 8 });
  near(d.far.point.der, 0.28);
  assert.deepEqual(d.far.gate, { pass: true });
  assert.equal(d.far.perSession.length, 8);
  assert.deepEqual(diarSection({ sweep: sweepOf(TABLE), confirm, far: far(0.31), sessionCount: 8 }).far.gate, { pass: false });
  assert.deepEqual(d.rotations, { sessions: 6, der: 0.25, countHits: 6, countMeanAbsError: 0 });
  assert.deepEqual(d.timing, { rtfWall: 0.07, peakWorkingSetMb: 300 });
  assert.throws(() => diarSection({ sweep: sweepOf(TABLE), confirm, far: far(0.28).slice(1), sessionCount: 8 }), /远场只有 7\/8 场/);
});

// ---- 回声消除 ----

test('回声消除的实时倍数：各条件各场的进程用时合计 ÷ 处理的音频时长合计', () => {
  const sessions = [
    esession('s1', 600, { c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ aec: { wallMs: 3000 } }) }, c2: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ aec: { wallMs: 6000 } }) } }),
    esession('s2', 300, { c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ aec: { wallMs: 1500 } }) }, c2: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ aec: { wallMs: 1500 } }) } }),
  ];
  near(aecRtf(sessions, 'corti'), (3 + 6 + 1.5 + 1.5) / (600 + 600 + 300 + 300));
  assert.throws(() => aecRtf(sessions, 'aec3'), /会议 s1 缺处理方式 aec3/);
  assert.throws(() => aecRtf(sessions, 'none'), /处理方式 none 没有回声消除程序的计时/);
});

test('回声消除部分：不处理不参加挑选；只有一个处理方式全部条件达标就选它，并给出它的实时倍数', () => {
  const sessions = [esession('s1', 600, { c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ aec: { wallMs: 3000 } }), aec3: ecell({ insHyp: 6 }) }, c2: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ aec: { wallMs: 6000 } }), aec3: ecell({}) } })];
  const e = echoSection(sessions);
  assert.equal(e.pick.status, 'ok');
  assert.equal(e.pick.chosen, 'corti');
  assert.deepEqual(e.pick.report.map((r) => r.name), ['corti', 'aec3']);
  assert.deepEqual(e.pick.report.find((r) => r.name === 'aec3').failed, ['c1']); // 插入率 6% 超过 5%
  near(e.rtfAec, 9 / 1200);
  near(e.baselineCer, 0.05);
  near(e.floor.insertion, 0.01);
  assert.deepEqual(e.none.map((c) => c.id), ['c1', 'c2']);
  near(e.none[0].insertion, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(e.variants).sort(), ['aec3', 'corti', 'none']);
});

test('回声消除部分：选中的不是第一个处理方式时，实时倍数用选中的那个', () => {
  const sessions = [esession('s1', 600, { c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ insHyp: 50, aec: { wallMs: 3000 } }), aec3: ecell({ aec: { wallMs: 12000 } }) } })];
  const e = echoSection(sessions);
  assert.equal(e.pick.chosen, 'aec3');
  near(e.rtfAec, 12 / 600);
});

test('回声消除部分：没有处理方式达标就不做回声消除，没有实时倍数', () => {
  const sessions = [esession('s1', 600, { c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ insHyp: 50 }) } })];
  const e = echoSection(sessions);
  assert.equal(e.pick.status, 'none');
  assert.equal(e.rtfAec, null);
});

const punctOf = (chars) => (0.6 + (0.12 * chars) / 1000) / 60;

test('汇总：各阶段齐了就给出报告；回声消除选中、远场过关，估算里就带上这两段（会议的先后顺序不影响）', () => {
  const r = buildReport(inputs({ sessions: [...dataset].reverse(), echo: inputs().echo.reverse() }));
  near(r.speechFrac.me, 0.1);
  near(r.speechFrac.sys, 0.2);
  near(r.chars, (13 / 300) * 0.3 * 3600); // 13 字 ÷ 300 秒说话 × 0.3 × 60 分钟
  assert.deepEqual([r.useAec, r.diarMic], [true, true]);
  near(r.rtf.decode, 0.002);
  near(r.rtf.vad, 0.005);
  near(r.rtf.aec, 8 * 3 / (8 * 600)); // 8 场 × 1 个条件 × 3 秒 ÷ 8 场 × 600 秒
  near(r.rtf.diar, 0.07);
  assert.deepEqual(r.punct, { loadSec: 0.6, secPer1000: 0.12 });
  assert.equal(r.asr.pick.status, 'ok');
  assert.equal(r.asr.pick.chosen.name, 'a');
  const a = r.asr.rows[0];
  near(a.stages.aec, 60 * 0.005);
  near(a.stages.diar, 2 * 60 * 0.07);
  near(a.batchMin, 0.24 + 0.6 + 0.3 + 8.4 + 1.8 + 0.1 + punctOf(r.chars));
  near(a.e2e.totalMin, 0.24 + 0.3 + 8.4 + 7.2 + punctOf(r.chars));
  assert.equal(r.diar.status, 'pass');
  assert.equal(r.echo.pick.chosen, 'corti');
  assert.equal(r.echo.asr, 'a');
  assert.equal(r.meetingMin, 60);
});

test('汇总：没有回声消除方式达标、远场没过关时，估算里不带这两段', () => {
  const noAec = inputs({ echo: MIXES.map((s) => ({ ...esession(s, 600, { c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ insHyp: 50 }) } }), asr: 'a' })) });
  const r1 = buildReport(noAec);
  assert.deepEqual([r1.useAec, r1.rtf.aec, r1.asr.rows[0].stages.aec], [false, 0, 0]);
  const farFail = inputs();
  farFail.diar.far = MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.31 }));
  const r2 = buildReport(farFail);
  assert.equal(r2.diarMic, false);
  near(r2.asr.rows[0].stages.diar, 60 * 0.07);
  assert.equal(r2.diar.far.gate.pass, false);
});

test('汇总：缺哪一项都报错，并说明先跑什么', () => {
  const noPre = inputs();
  noPre.candidates[1] = { ...noPre.candidates[1], prescreen8: null };
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(noPre), /b 还没有预筛结果，先跑 asr prescreen/);
  const noFull = inputs();
  noFull.candidates[1] = { ...noFull.candidates[1], full: null };
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(noFull), /b 还没有全量结果，先跑 asr full/);
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(inputs({ echo: inputs().echo.slice(1) })), /回声评测的会议与评测集不一致：有 s2、s3.*应有 s1、s2/);
  const mixedAsr = inputs();
  mixedAsr.echo[0] = { ...mixedAsr.echo[0], asr: 'b' };
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(mixedAsr), /各场回声评测用的识别候选不一致：a、b/);
  const noSweep = inputs();
  noSweep.diar.sweep = [];
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(noSweep), /分离：没有扫描结果，先跑 diar sweep/);
  const noConfirm = inputs();
  noConfirm.diar.confirm = [];
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(noConfirm), /区间中点 0\.6 还没有补测的结果，先跑 diar confirm/);
  const noFar = inputs();
  noFar.diar.far = [];
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(noFar), /缺远场结果，先跑 diar far/);
  const noRot = inputs();
  noRot.diar.rotations = [];
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(noRot), /缺“我”轮换的结果，先跑 diar rotations/);
  const noTiming = inputs();
  noTiming.diar.timing = null;
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(noTiming), /缺计时结果，先跑 diar timing/);
  const e2e = inputs();
  e2e.candidates[0] = { ...e2e.candidates[0], vad: null };
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(e2e), /选出的 a 没有 VAD 端到端的结果，先跑 asr vad --cands a/);
  // 选中的是第二个、而第一个做过端到端：还是要报错
  const second = inputs({ candidates: [candidate('a', 0.2, vadOf(0.2, 6, 100)), candidate('b', 0.1, null)] });
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(second), /选出的 b 没有 VAD 端到端的结果/);
});

test('汇总：没有候选达标时照常出报告（没有选中的识别器，也就不要求端到端）', () => {
  const tooSlow = { ...candidate('a', 0.1, null), full: fullOf(0.1, 5, { load: 3 }) }; // 只算识别就要 0.3 × 60 × 5 = 90 分钟
  const r = buildReport(inputs({ candidates: [tooSlow] }));
  assert.equal(r.asr.pick.status, 'none-eligible');
  assert.equal(r.asr.pick.chosen, undefined);
});

test('汇总：估算要用的实测数不是有限的数时报错，不让 NaN 悄悄变成“没有候选达标”', () => {
  const bad = (patch) => inputs({ speed: { ...inputs().speed, ...patch } });
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(bad({ decode: {} })), /解码实时倍数不是有限的数：undefined/);
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(bad({ vad: { rtf: NaN } })), /VAD 实时倍数不是有限的数：NaN/);
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(bad({ punct: { loadSec: 1 } })), /标点每千字用时不是有限的数/);
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(bad({ punct: { secPer1000: 1 } })), /标点加载用时不是有限的数/);
  const noDiarRtf = inputs();
  noDiarRtf.diar.timing = { summary: { rtfWall: Infinity } };
  assert.throws(() => buildReport(noDiarRtf), /分离实时倍数不是有限的数：Infinity/);
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 4：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/report.mjs`**

```js
// S4 结果的汇总：从各阶段的结果算出设计文档 3.9 需要的数字，并按 decide.mjs 里事先写死的门槛与决策规则下结论。
// 这里只有纯函数（读文件的是 report.mjs 脚本与各阶段脚本），所以可以用造出来的数据测。
import { normalizeText } from './cer.mjs';
import { checkDiarGate, checkFarFieldGate, GATES, pickAec, pickAsr, pickEmbedding } from './decide.mjs';
import { aggregate, curves } from './diar-eval.mjs';
import { aggregateEcho } from './echo-eval.mjs';
import { estimatePipeline, unionDuration } from './estimate.mjs';

/** 估算按一场多长的会议算（设计文档 3.5：60 分钟） */
export const MEETING_MIN = 60;

/** 识别器自己输出的标点占非空白字符的比例低于这个数，就认为它不带标点，流水线里要另加标点模型（估算里加上标点的用时） */
export const PUNCT_RATE_MIN = 0.01;

/**
 * 两条音轨里“有人说话的时间”占音轨时长的比例，把各场合在一起算（总说话时长 ÷ 总音轨时长）：
 * me 是“我”那条麦克风轨；sys 是其余参会者合起来的系统声音轨（重叠的说话只算一次）。
 * sessions：[{ me, tracks: [{ speaker, durationSec, segments: [{start,end}] }] }]。
 */
export function speechFractions(sessions) {
  if (sessions.length === 0) throw new Error('没有会议可算说话占比');
  let meSpeech = 0;
  let meTotal = 0;
  let sysSpeech = 0;
  let sysTotal = 0;
  for (const s of sessions) {
    const me = s.tracks.find((t) => t.speaker === s.me);
    if (!me) throw new Error(`会议里没有“我”：${s.me}`);
    const others = s.tracks.filter((t) => t !== me);
    if (others.length === 0) throw new Error(`会议只有“我”一个人：${s.me}`);
    meSpeech += unionDuration(me.segments);
    meTotal += me.durationSec;
    sysSpeech += unionDuration(others.flatMap((t) => t.segments));
    sysTotal += Math.max(...others.map((t) => t.durationSec));
  }
  return { me: meSpeech / meTotal, sys: sysSpeech / sysTotal };
}

/** 只算识别这一段、不含 VAD 与分离等别的阶段时，一场 meetingMin 分钟的会议要多少分钟：两条轨的说话时间 × 实时倍数 */
export function asrOnlyMinutes(speechFrac, rtf, meetingMin = MEETING_MIN) {
  return (speechFrac.me + speechFrac.sys) * meetingMin * rtf;
}

/** 峰值内存（GB）：工作集与私有内存的峰值里取大的那个，1 GB = 1024 MB（保守） */
export function peakMemGb(summary) {
  return Math.max(summary.peakWorkingSetMb, summary.peakPrivateMb) / 1024;
}

/**
 * 决赛圈：全量识别已经跑完、速度与内存都达标（只算识别的估计用时不超过 30 分钟、峰值内存不超过 4 GB）的候选里，
 * CER 最低的前 n 个（CER 一样时按给定的先后）。只有这些候选会再做 VAD 端到端的评测。
 * cands：[{ id, full }]，full 是全量识别的结果（跳过的候选 full.skipped 为真，不进决赛圈）。
 */
export function finalists({ cands, speechFrac, n = 3 }) {
  const g = GATES.asr;
  return cands
    .filter((c) => c.full && !c.full.skipped)
    .map((c, order) => ({ id: c.id, order, cer: c.full.score.cer.rate, asrOnlyMin: asrOnlyMinutes(speechFrac, c.full.summary.rtf), peakMemGb: peakMemGb(c.full.summary) }))
    .filter((c) => c.asrOnlyMin <= g.estMinutesMax && c.peakMemGb <= g.peakMemGbMax)
    .sort((a, b) => a.cer - b.cer || a.order - b.order)
    .slice(0, n)
    .map(({ order, ...rest }) => rest);
}

/**
 * 转写稿的字数密度：评测集里规整后的参考字数 ÷ 各条音轨自己的说话时长之和（字/秒）。
 * sessions：[{ tracks: [{ segments: [{ start, end, text }] }] }]。
 */
export function charsPerSpeechSec(sessions) {
  let chars = 0;
  let sec = 0;
  for (const s of sessions) {
    for (const t of s.tracks) {
      chars += t.segments.reduce((n, x) => n + Array.from(normalizeText(x.text)).length, 0);
      sec += unionDuration(t.segments);
    }
  }
  if (sec === 0) throw new Error('评测集里没有说话时间，算不出字数密度');
  return chars / sec;
}

/** 一场 meetingMin 分钟的会议转写稿大约多少字：字数密度 × 两条轨的说话时间 */
export function meetingChars({ charsPerSec, speechFrac, meetingMin = MEETING_MIN }) {
  return charsPerSec * (speechFrac.me + speechFrac.sys) * meetingMin * 60;
}

/** 加标点的用时（分钟）：一次加载 + 每千个单位（词或字）的处理 × 字数 */
export function punctMinutes(punct, chars) {
  return (punct.loadSec + (punct.secPer1000 * chars) / 1000) / 60;
}

/**
 * 一个识别候选的一行数字。cand：{ id, name, prescreen8, prescreen4, full, vad }（各阶段的结果文件内容，没跑的是 null；full 跳过的有 skipped）。
 * ctx：{ speechFrac, rtf: { decode, vad, aec, diar }, useAec, diarMic, punct: { loadSec, secPer1000 }, chars（一场会议的转写稿字数） }；会议长度固定按 MEETING_MIN。
 *
 * 一场 60 分钟会议“保存 → 出转写稿”要多久，按两种办法估，取大的（保守）：
 *  - 批识别估计：解码 + VAD + 分离（+ 回声消除）+ 识别（说话时长 × 全量识别的实时倍数）+ 两条轨各加载一次模型 + （不带标点时）加标点；
 *  - 端到端估计（只有做过 VAD 端到端的候选才有）：解码 + 分离（+ 回声消除）+ 两条轨 ×（VAD 与识别一体程序的整条录音实时倍数，含模型加载）+（不带标点时）加标点。
 * 峰值内存同样取批识别与端到端里大的。
 */
export function asrRow(cand, ctx) {
  const { id, name, prescreen8, prescreen4, full, vad } = cand;
  const { speechFrac, rtf, useAec, diarMic, punct, chars } = ctx;
  // 预筛（头、中、尾三场，每位参会者前 5 分钟的标注段）：每个候选都有，被跳过的候选只有这一项
  const pre = prescreen8
    ? { cer: prescreen8.score.cer.rate, rtf8: prescreen8.summary.rtf, rtf4: prescreen4 ? prescreen4.summary.rtf : null, asrOnlyMin: asrOnlyMinutes(speechFrac, prescreen8.summary.rtf) }
    : null;
  if (!full) return { id, name, status: 'not-run', pre };
  if (full.skipped) return { id, name, status: 'skipped', reason: full.reason, pre };
  if (full.summary.loadSecMean === null) throw new Error(`${id}：没有解析到模型加载用时，算不出加载的开销`);
  const needsPunct = full.punctRate < PUNCT_RATE_MIN;
  const punctMin = needsPunct ? punctMinutes(punct, chars) : 0;
  const batch = estimatePipeline({ meetingMin: MEETING_MIN, speechFrac, rtf: { ...rtf, asr: full.summary.rtf }, useAec, diarMic });
  const batchMin = batch.total + (2 * full.summary.loadSecMean) / 60 + punctMin;
  let e2e = null;
  if (vad) {
    const rtfWall = vad.summary.wallSec / vad.summary.audioSec;
    const rest = estimatePipeline({ meetingMin: MEETING_MIN, speechFrac, rtf: { ...rtf, vad: 0, asr: 0 }, useAec, diarMic }).total;
    e2e = { cer: vad.score.cer.rate, rtfWall, peakMemGb: peakMemGb(vad.summary), totalMin: rest + 2 * MEETING_MIN * rtfWall + punctMin };
  }
  return {
    id,
    name,
    status: 'ok',
    pre,
    cer: full.score.cer.rate,
    rtf8: full.summary.rtf,
    loadSec: full.summary.loadSecMean,
    punctRate: full.punctRate,
    needsPunct,
    punctMin,
    stages: batch.stages,
    batchMin,
    e2e,
    estMinutes: Math.max(batchMin, e2e ? e2e.totalMin : 0),
    peakMemGb: Math.max(peakMemGb(full.summary), e2e ? e2e.peakMemGb : 0),
  };
}

/** 全部候选的行与默认识别引擎的结论（只有跑完全量的候选参加；速度、内存取各自的保守值，规则见 decide.pickAsr） */
export function asrSection(cands, ctx) {
  const rows = cands.map((c) => asrRow(c, ctx));
  const pick = pickAsr(rows.filter((r) => r.status === 'ok').map((r) => ({ name: r.id, cer: r.cer, estMinutes: r.estMinutes, peakMemGb: r.peakMemGb })));
  return { rows, pick };
}

/** 每场结果的一行：会议编号、真实人数、估计人数（按每个说话人出现过计）、DER */
function sessionRows(results) {
  return [...results]
    .sort((a, b) => (a.mixId < b.mixId ? -1 : a.mixId > b.mixId ? 1 : 0))
    .map((r) => ({ mixId: r.mixId, actual: r.actualSpeakers, estimated: r.estimatedSpeakers, estimated3s: r.estimatedSpeakers3s, der: r.der, total: r.total }));
}

/** 按真实人数分组（评测里“系统声音”里的远端说话人数各场不同：1、2、3 人），每组的场数、DER 合计与人数合格的场数 */
function groupByActual(results) {
  const groups = new Map();
  for (const r of results) {
    if (!groups.has(r.actualSpeakers)) groups.set(r.actualSpeakers, []);
    groups.get(r.actualSpeakers).push(r);
  }
  return [...groups.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]).map(([actual, list]) => ({ actual, sessions: list.length, ...pick(aggregate(list)) }));
}
const pick = ({ der, countHits }) => ({ der, countHits });

/**
 * 说话人分离部分。
 * sweep：全部扫描任务的结果；confirm：区间中点不在已测阈值上时在中点再测的结果；far：远场；rotations：“我”换成每位参会者；timing：计时的结果文件内容；
 * sessionCount：评测集的会议数（每个设置必须在每一场上都有结果）。
 * 返回 { pick, curves, point, gate, groups, perSession, far, rotations, timing }；中点要补测而没补测时 point 与 gate 为 null（status 'needs-confirm'）。
 */
export function diarSection({ sweep, confirm = [], far = [], rotations = [], timing = null, sessionCount }) {
  const c = curves(sweep, { sessions: sessionCount });
  const models = Object.entries(c).map(([name, v]) => ({ name, points: v.points.map(({ threshold, der, countHits }) => ({ threshold, der, countHits })) }));
  const chosen = pickEmbedding(models);
  const atChosen = (results) => results.filter((r) => r.embedding === chosen.model && r.clusters === null && Math.abs(r.threshold - chosen.threshold) < 1e-9);
  let results;
  let point;
  if (!chosen.needsConfirm) {
    results = atChosen(sweep);
    point = aggregate(results); // chosen.point 只有挑选用的三个字段（阈值、DER、人数合格场次）
  } else if (confirm.length > 0) {
    results = atChosen(confirm);
    if (results.length !== sessionCount) throw new Error(`中点 ${chosen.threshold} 的补测只有 ${results.length}/${sessionCount} 场的结果`);
    point = aggregate(results);
  } else {
    results = [];
    point = null;
  }
  const farResults = far.length > 0 ? far : null;
  if (farResults && farResults.length !== sessionCount) throw new Error(`远场只有 ${farResults.length}/${sessionCount} 场的结果`);
  const farPoint = farResults ? aggregate(farResults) : null;
  const rot = rotations.length > 0 ? aggregate(rotations) : null;
  return {
    pick: chosen,
    curves: c,
    status: point ? (checkDiarGate(point).pass ? 'pass' : 'fail') : 'needs-confirm',
    point,
    gate: point ? checkDiarGate(point) : null,
    groups: results.length > 0 ? groupByActual(results) : [],
    perSession: sessionRows(results),
    far: farPoint ? { point: farPoint, gate: checkFarFieldGate(farPoint.der), perSession: sessionRows(farResults) } : null,
    rotations: rot ? { sessions: rot.sessions, der: rot.der, countHits: rot.countHits, countMeanAbsError: rot.countMeanAbsError } : null,
    timing: timing ? timing.summary : null,
  };
}

/** 某个处理方式的实时倍数：各条件各场的“处理进程用时 ÷ 处理的音频时长”合计（进程用时含读写文件，保守） */
export function aecRtf(sessionResults, variant) {
  let wallSec = 0;
  let audioSec = 0;
  for (const s of sessionResults) {
    for (const cond of Object.values(s.conditions)) {
      const cell = cond[variant];
      if (!cell) throw new Error(`会议 ${s.session} 缺处理方式 ${variant} 的结果`);
      if (!cell.aec) throw new Error(`处理方式 ${variant} 没有回声消除程序的计时（它是“不处理”吗？）`);
      wallSec += cell.aec.wallMs / 1000;
      audioSec += s.seconds;
    }
  }
  return wallSec / audioSec;
}

/** 回声消除部分：汇总 → 去掉“不处理”后按事先写死的规则选 → 选中的处理方式的实时倍数 */
export function echoSection(sessionResults) {
  const agg = aggregateEcho(sessionResults);
  const cands = Object.entries(agg.variants)
    .filter(([name]) => name !== 'none')
    .map(([name, v]) => ({ name, conditions: v.conditions }));
  const chosen = pickAec(cands);
  return { baselineCer: agg.baselineCer, floor: agg.floor, none: agg.variants.none?.conditions ?? null, variants: agg.variants, pick: chosen, rtfAec: chosen.status === 'ok' ? aecRtf(sessionResults, chosen.chosen) : null };
}

const finite = (x, what) => {
  if (!Number.isFinite(x)) throw new Error(`${what}不是有限的数：${x}`);
  return x;
};

/**
 * 把各阶段的结果汇成一份报告（纯函数；读文件的是 report.mjs 脚本）。缺哪一项都报错并说明先跑什么，不拿残缺的数字下结论。
 * sessions：评测集的会议（dataset.loadSessions）；speed：speed.json；candidates：[{ id, name, prescreen8, prescreen4, full, vad }]；
 * diar：{ sweep, confirm, far, rotations, timing }；echo：每场一份的回声评测结果。
 * 估算里的 useAec 取决于回声消除有没有选中处理方式，diarMic 取决于远场是否过了门槛；挑出的默认识别器必须做过 VAD 端到端（否则估算没有取到保守值）。
 */
export function buildReport({ sessions, speed, candidates, diar, echo }) {
  const ids = sessions.map((s) => s.id).sort();
  const speechFrac = speechFractions(sessions);
  const chars = meetingChars({ charsPerSec: charsPerSpeechSec(sessions), speechFrac });
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (!c.prescreen8) throw new Error(`${c.id} 还没有预筛结果，先跑 asr prescreen`);
    if (!c.full) throw new Error(`${c.id} 还没有全量结果，先跑 asr full`);
  }
  const echoIds = echo.map((s) => s.session).sort();
  if (echoIds.join() !== ids.join()) throw new Error(`回声评测的会议与评测集不一致：有 ${echoIds.join('、')}，应有 ${ids.join('、')}`);
  const echoAsr = [...new Set(echo.map((s) => s.asr))].sort();
  if (echoAsr.length !== 1) throw new Error(`各场回声评测用的识别候选不一致：${echoAsr.join('、')}`);
  const echoRes = echoSection(echo);
  const useAec = echoRes.pick.status === 'ok';

  if (diar.sweep.length === 0) throw new Error('分离：没有扫描结果，先跑 diar sweep');
  const diarRes = diarSection({ ...diar, sessionCount: sessions.length });
  if (diarRes.status === 'needs-confirm') throw new Error(`分离：区间中点 ${diarRes.pick.threshold} 还没有补测的结果，先跑 diar confirm`);
  if (!diarRes.far) throw new Error('分离：缺远场结果，先跑 diar far');
  if (!diarRes.rotations) throw new Error('分离：缺“我”轮换的结果，先跑 diar rotations');
  if (!diarRes.timing) throw new Error('分离：缺计时结果，先跑 diar timing');
  const diarMic = diarRes.far.gate.pass;

  const rtf = {
    decode: finite(speed.decode.rtf, '解码实时倍数'),
    vad: finite(speed.vad.rtf, 'VAD 实时倍数'),
    aec: useAec ? finite(echoRes.rtfAec, '回声消除实时倍数') : 0,
    diar: finite(diarRes.timing.rtfWall, '分离实时倍数'),
  };
  const punct = { loadSec: finite(speed.punct.loadSec, '标点加载用时'), secPer1000: finite(speed.punct.secPer1000, '标点每千字用时') };
  const asr = asrSection(candidates, { speechFrac, rtf, useAec, diarMic, punct, chars });
  const chosen = asr.pick.chosen;
  if (chosen && !asr.rows.find((r) => r.id === chosen.name).e2e) throw new Error(`选出的 ${chosen.name} 没有 VAD 端到端的结果，先跑 asr vad --cands ${chosen.name}，再重新汇总`);
  return { meetingMin: MEETING_MIN, speechFrac, chars, rtf, punct, useAec, diarMic, asr, diar: diarRes, echo: { ...echoRes, asr: echoAsr[0] } };
}
```

```powershell
node --test scripts/s4/lib/report.test.mjs
```

预期：输出末尾的统计里有 `# pass 31` 和 `# fail 0`（其余的 `# …` 行不用管）。

- [ ] **步骤 6：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/render.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `render.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { candidate, dr, ecell, esession, fullOf, inputs, MIXES, none, pre8, vadOf } from './fixtures.mjs';
import { pct, renderReport, table } from './render.mjs';
import { buildReport } from './report.mjs';

const render = (over) => renderReport(buildReport(inputs(over)), { generatedAt: '2000-01-01 00:00' });
const lines = (md) => md.split('\n');
const has = (md, line) => assert.ok(lines(md).includes(line), `缺这一行：${line}`);
const lacks = (md, text) => assert.ok(!md.includes(text), `不该出现：${text}`);

test('比例写成百分数；表格转义竖线并补上分隔行', () => {
  assert.equal(pct(0.1), '10.0%');
  assert.equal(pct(0.12345, 2), '12.35%');
  assert.equal(pct(0.15, 0), '15%');
  assert.equal(table(['a', 'b'], [['1', 'x|y'], [2, 3]]), '| a | b |\n| --- | --- |\n| 1 | x\\|y |\n| 2 | 3 |');
});

test('结论一览：每项门槛、实测与结论（全部达标的情形）', () => {
  const md = render();
  assert.equal(lines(md)[0], '# S4 结果汇总（生成于 2000-01-01 00:00）');
  has(md, '| 识别准确度（近场、按标注切段） | CER ≤ 15% | a：10.00% | 达标 |');
  has(md, '| 速度（估算 60 分钟的会议） | ≤ 30 分钟 | a：16.2 分钟 | 达标 |');
  has(md, '| 峰值内存 | ≤ 4 GB | a：0.49 GB | 达标 |');
  has(md, '| 说话人分离 DER（干净混合） | ≤ 25% | m1，阈值 0.60：10.80% | 达标 |');
  has(md, '| 人数（估计与实际相差不超过 1 的场次） | ≥ 6/8 | 8/8 | 达标 |');
  has(md, '| 麦克风轨也分人（远场 DER） | ≤ 30% 才开放 | 28.00% | 开放 |');
  has(md, '| 回声消除 | 插入率 ≤ 5% 且近端 CER 增加 ≤ 2 个百分点（全部条件） | corti：0/1 个条件未达标 | 做：corti |');
});

test('结论一览：不达标的项用粗体标出；回声不做、麦克风轨不开放', () => {
  const o = inputs({ echo: MIXES.map((s) => ({ ...esession(s, 600, { c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ insHyp: 50 }) } }), asr: 'a' })) });
  o.diar.far = MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.31 }));
  o.diar.confirm = MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: 0.6, der: 0.27 }));
  const md = renderReport(buildReport(o), { generatedAt: 'T' });
  has(md, '| 麦克风轨也分人（远场 DER） | ≤ 30% 才开放 | 31.00% | **v1 不开放** |');
  has(md, '| 回声消除 | 插入率 ≤ 5% 且近端 CER 增加 ≤ 2 个百分点（全部条件） | corti：1/1 个条件未达标 | **不做**：提示“外放时请戴耳机” |');
  has(md, '| 说话人分离 DER（干净混合） | ≤ 25% | m1，阈值 0.60：27.00% | **不达标** |');
  has(md, '| 人数（估计与实际相差不超过 1 的场次） | ≥ 6/8 | 8/8 | 达标 |');
  has(md, '| 回声消除（没有选中处理方式，不做） | 0.00 |');
  has(md, '| 说话人分离（系统轨） | 4.20 |');
  has(md, '| 回声消除实时倍数 | 没有选中处理方式，不算 |');
  has(md, '| corti | 否 | c1 | 0.0 |');
});

test('结论一览：人数不合格、识别 CER 不达标与没有候选达标各自标出', () => {
  const over = inputs();
  const overCluster = (r) => ({ ...r, estimatedSpeakers: r.actualSpeakers === 3 ? 6 : r.actualSpeakers, estimatedSpeakers3s: r.actualSpeakers === 3 ? 5 : r.actualSpeakers });
  over.diar.sweep = over.diar.sweep.map(overCluster);
  over.diar.confirm = over.diar.confirm.map(overCluster);
  const md0 = renderReport(buildReport(over), { generatedAt: 'T' });
  has(md0, '| 人数（估计与实际相差不超过 1 的场次） | ≥ 6/8 | 4/8 | **不达标** |');
  has(md0, '| 3 | 4 | 10.80% | 0/4 |'); // 三个远端说话人的四场一场也没合格
  has(md0, '| s5 | 3 | 6 / 5 | 10.8% |'); // 实际 3 人，估计 6 人（说话满 3 秒的 5 人）
  has(md0, '| s1 | 1 | 1 / 1 | 10.8% |');

  const bad = inputs({ candidates: [candidate('b', 0.2, vadOf(0.2, 6, 100))] }); // 速度与内存达标的里面最好的 CER 是 20%
  const md1 = renderReport(buildReport(bad), { generatedAt: 'T' });
  has(md1, '| 识别准确度（近场、按标注切段） | CER ≤ 15% | b：20.00% | **不达标** |');
  has(md1, '| 速度（估算 60 分钟的会议） | ≤ 30 分钟 | b：16.2 分钟 | 达标 |');

  const slow = inputs({ candidates: [{ ...candidate('a', 0.1, null), full: fullOf(0.1, 5, { load: 3 }) }] });
  const md2 = renderReport(buildReport(slow), { generatedAt: 'T' });
  has(md2, '| 识别准确度（近场、按标注切段） | CER ≤ 15% | — | — |');
  has(md2, '| 速度（估算 60 分钟的会议） | ≤ 30 分钟 | — | **不达标** |');
  has(md2, '| 峰值内存 | ≤ 4 GB | — | **不达标** |');
  has(md2, '没有选中的识别候选，不列各阶段的用时。');
  has(md2, '| a | 9.00% | 0.1200 / — | 10.00% | 5.0000 | 3.0 | 否（另加 0.01 分钟） | 99.7 / — | 0.49 | 速度或内存超出 |');
  lacks(md2, '**默认**');
});

test('识别表：预筛与全量的数字、是否自带标点、批识别与端到端的估计、结论；跳过的候选只有预筛', () => {
  const md = render({
    candidates: [
      candidate('a', 0.1, vadOf(0.11, 6, 100)),
      { ...candidate('b', 0.2, null), full: fullOf(0.2, 0.1, { load: 3 }, 0.2) }, // 自带标点
      { id: 'c', name: 'C', prescreen8: pre8, prescreen4: { summary: { rtf: 0.3 } }, full: { skipped: true, reason: '太慢' }, vad: null },
    ],
  });
  has(md, '| a | 9.00% | 0.1200 / — | 10.00% | 0.1000 | 3.0 | 否（另加 0.01 分钟） | 11.5 / 16.2 | 0.49 | **默认** |');
  has(md, '| b | 9.00% | 0.1200 / — | 20.00% | 0.1000 | 3.0 | 是 | 11.4 / — | 0.49 | 速度与内存达标 |');
  has(md, '| c | 9.00% | 0.1200 / 0.3000 | — | — | — | — | — | — | 跳过：太慢 |');
  has(md, '| a | 11.00% | 0.0600 | 0.49 |'); // 端到端
  lacks(md, '| b | 11.00%');
  has(md, '候选：a = A；b = B；c = C。');
});

test('识别表：速度或内存超出的候选照样列出', () => {
  const md = render({ candidates: [candidate('a', 0.1, vadOf(0.11, 6, 100)), { ...candidate('b', 0.12, null), full: fullOf(0.12, 5, { load: 3 }) }] });
  has(md, '| b | 9.00% | 0.1200 / — | 12.00% | 5.0000 | 3.0 | 否（另加 0.01 分钟） | 99.7 / — | 0.49 | 速度或内存超出 |');
});

test('流水线用时估算：各阶段、批识别与端到端两种合计、取大者；没有选回声消除时标出', () => {
  const md = render();
  has(md, '默认识别器 a，一场 60 分钟的会议（分钟）：');
  has(md, '| 解码（两条轨） | 0.24 |');
  has(md, '| 回声消除（麦克风轨） | 0.30 |');
  has(md, '| VAD（两条轨） | 0.60 |');
  has(md, '| 说话人分离（两条轨） | 8.40 |');
  has(md, '| 识别（说话时间 × 全量实时倍数） | 1.80 |');
  has(md, '| 模型加载（两条轨各一次） | 0.10 |');
  has(md, '| 加标点（识别器不带标点） | 0.01 |');
  has(md, '| **批识别估计合计** | 11.45 |');
  has(md, '| **端到端估计**（解码 + 回声 + 分离 + 两条轨的 VAD 与识别一体程序 + 标点） | 16.15 |');
  has(md, '| **取大者（用于门槛）** | 16.15 |');
  has(md, '| “我”轨说话占比 / 系统轨说话占比（评测集实测） | 10.0% / 20.0% |');
  has(md, '| 一场 60 分钟会议的转写稿字数（按评测集的字数密度） | 47 字 |');
  has(md, '| 回声消除实时倍数 | 0.0050 |');
  has(md, '| 说话人分离实时倍数 | 0.0700 |');
  has(md, '| 加标点：模型加载 / 每千个单位 | 0.60 秒 / 0.12 秒 |');
  const own = render({ candidates: [{ ...candidate('a', 0.1, vadOf(0.11, 6, 100)), full: fullOf(0.1, 0.1, { load: 3 }, 0.2) }] });
  has(own, '| 加标点（识别器自带，不另算） | 0.00 |');
  // 选中的不是第一个候选：阶段表要取它自己的那一行
  const second = render({ candidates: [candidate('b', 0.2, null), candidate('a', 0.1, vadOf(0.11, 6, 100))] });
  has(second, '默认识别器 a，一场 60 分钟的会议（分钟）：');
  has(second, '| **取大者（用于门槛）** | 16.15 |');
});

test('说话人分离：曲线表、胜出者、分组、每场、远场、轮换与计时', () => {
  const md = render();
  has(md, '| 声纹模型 | 0.3 | 0.5 | 0.7 | 0.9 | 已知人数 |');
  has(md, '| m1 | 20.0% · 8/8 | 10.0% · 8/8 | 10.5% · 8/8 | 30.0% · 8/8 | — |');
  has(md, '胜出：m1，最低 DER 10.00%，距最低点 1 个百分点内的阈值区间 0.5～0.7，取中点 0.60（不在扫描的阈值上，已补测）。');
  has(md, '该设置合计：DER 10.80%（漏检 5.40%、误检 2.70%、混淆 2.70%），人数合格 8/8 场，平均人数误差 0.00。');
  has(md, '| 1 | 3 | 10.80% | 3/3 |');
  has(md, '| 3 | 4 | 10.80% | 4/4 |');
  has(md, '| s4 | 2 | 2 / 2 | 10.8% |');
  has(md, '远场（阵列第 1 通道，含全部参会者）：DER 28.00%，人数合格 8/8 场；过 30% 的门槛。');
  has(md, '| s4 | 2 | 2 / 2 | 28.0% |');
  has(md, '“我”换成每位其余参会者（补充，不是门槛）：3 种组合，DER 20.00%，人数合格 3/3，平均人数误差 0.00。');
  has(md, '计时（三场、8 线程、含模型加载）：实时倍数 0.0700，峰值内存 300 MB，别的程序的 CPU 占用均值 30%。');
});

test('说话人分离：各声纹模型扫的阈值不一样时，列按阈值从小到大排，缺的格子写横线', () => {
  const o = inputs();
  o.diar.sweep = [
    ...[[0.5, 0.1], [0.9, 0.3]].flatMap(([t, der]) => MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: t, der }))),
    ...[[0.3, 0.25], [0.7, 0.13]].flatMap(([t, der]) => MIXES.map((s) => dr('m2', s, { threshold: t, der }))),
  ];
  o.diar.confirm = [];
  const md = renderReport(buildReport(o), { generatedAt: 'T' });
  has(md, '| 声纹模型 | 0.3 | 0.5 | 0.7 | 0.9 | 已知人数 |');
  has(md, '| m1 | — | 10.0% · 8/8 | — | 30.0% · 8/8 | — |');
  has(md, '| m2 | 25.0% · 8/8 | — | 13.0% · 8/8 | — | — |');
});

test('说话人分离：区间中点本来就测过时不写“已补测”；已知人数那一列有数', () => {
  const o = inputs();
  const table2 = { m1: { 0.3: 0.2, 0.5: 0.1, 0.6: 0.102, 0.7: 0.105, 0.9: 0.3 } };
  o.diar.sweep = [
    ...Object.entries(table2.m1).flatMap(([t, der]) => MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { threshold: Number(t), der }))),
    ...MIXES.map((s) => dr('m1', s, { clusters: 1, der: 0.05 })),
  ];
  o.diar.confirm = [];
  const md = renderReport(buildReport(o), { generatedAt: 'T' });
  has(md, '胜出：m1，最低 DER 10.00%，距最低点 1 个百分点内的阈值区间 0.5～0.7，取中点 0.60。');
  has(md, '| m1 | 20.0% · 8/8 | 10.0% · 8/8 | 10.2% · 8/8 | 10.5% · 8/8 | 30.0% · 8/8 | 5.0% · 8/8 |');
});

test('回声消除：三张表与对照门槛；底线与基线', () => {
  const o = inputs({
    echo: MIXES.map((s) => ({
      ...esession(s, 600, {
        c1: { none: none(), corti: ecell({ insHyp: 4, nearErrors: 6, erleDb: 31.25 }), aec3: ecell({ insHyp: 50 }) },
        c2: { none: none({ nearErrors: 9 }), corti: ecell({ insHyp: 1 }), aec3: ecell({ nearErrors: 9 }) },
      }),
      floor: { nearErrors: 7, nearRefLen: 100, insHyp: 3, insRef: 100 }, // 底线与基线（5%）不同，免得写混
      asr: 'a',
    })),
  });
  const md = renderReport(buildReport(o), { generatedAt: 'T' });
  has(md, '识别器：a。基线（干净近端、不处理）CER 5.00%；没有回声时同样流程的底线：远端单讲段里识别出的字数占参考的 3.00%，近端 CER 7.00%。');
  has(md, '| 处理方式 | c1 | c2 |');
  has(md, '| none | 100.0% | 100.0% |');
  has(md, '| corti | 4.0% | 1.0% |');
  has(md, '| aec3 | 50.0% | 2.0% |');
  has(md, '| none | 0.0 | 4.0 |');
  has(md, '| corti | 1.0 | 0.0 |');
  has(md, '| corti | 31.3 | 20.0 |');
  has(md, '| corti | 是 | — | 0.5 |');
  has(md, '| aec3 | 否 | c1、c2 | 2.0 |');
});

test('回声评测用的识别器与默认识别器不同时，写一句注意', () => {
  const o = inputs({ echo: inputs().echo.map((s) => ({ ...s, asr: 'z' })) });
  const md = renderReport(buildReport(o), { generatedAt: 'T' });
  const note = '注意：回声评测用的识别器是 z，默认识别器是 a；回声部分的数字是用 z 测的。';
  has(md, note);
  assert.equal(lines(md)[lines(md).indexOf(note) - 1], ''); // 与上面的表之间空一行
  lacks(render(), '注意：回声评测用的识别器');
});

test('报告里出现 NaN、undefined、null 就报错，不交出残缺的报告', () => {
  const r = buildReport(inputs());
  const withTiming = (v) => ({ ...r, diar: { ...r.diar, timing: { ...r.diar.timing, ...v } } });
  assert.throws(() => renderReport(withTiming({ othersLoadMean: undefined }), { generatedAt: 'T' }), /无效的值（NaN）/);
  assert.throws(() => renderReport(withTiming({ peakWorkingSetMb: undefined }), { generatedAt: 'T' }), /无效的值（undefined）/);
  assert.throws(() => renderReport(withTiming({ peakWorkingSetMb: null }), { generatedAt: 'T' }), /无效的值（null）/);
  assert.throws(() => renderReport(withTiming({ rtfWall: Infinity }), { generatedAt: 'T' }), /无效的值（Infinity）/);
});
```
