- [ ] **步骤 8：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/render.mjs`**

```js
// 把汇总（report.mjs 的 buildReport）写成 Markdown，填设计文档 3.9 用。只负责排版：数字全部来自汇总结果，
// 达标与否的结论来自 decide.mjs 里事先写死的决策规则（这里不重新比大小，免得和规则在边界上不一致）。
import { GATES } from './decide.mjs';

/** 比例写成百分数 */
export const pct = (x, digits = 1) => `${(x * 100).toFixed(digits)}%`;
const n1 = (x) => x.toFixed(1);
const n2 = (x) => x.toFixed(2);
const n4 = (x) => x.toFixed(4);
const dash = '—';

/** Markdown 表格；单元格里的竖线要转义 */
export function table(header, rows) {
  const line = (cols) => `| ${cols.map((c) => String(c).replaceAll('|', '\\|')).join(' | ')} |`;
  return [line(header), line(header.map(() => '---')), ...rows.map(line)].join('\n');
}

const fits = (ok) => (ok ? '达标' : '**不达标**');

function verdicts(r) {
  const { pick } = r.asr;
  const c = pick.chosen;
  const { gate, point, far } = r.diar;
  const aec = r.echo.pick;
  const aecMeasured = aec.report.map((x) => `${x.name}：${x.failed.length}/${r.echo.variants[x.name].conditions.length} 个条件未达标`).join('；');
  return table(
    ['项', '门槛（测之前定下）', '实测', '结论'],
    [
      ['识别准确度（近场、按标注切段）', `CER ≤ ${pct(GATES.asr.cerMax, 0)}`, c ? `${c.name}：${pct(c.cer, 2)}` : dash, c ? fits(pick.status === 'ok') : dash],
      ['速度（估算 60 分钟的会议）', `≤ ${GATES.asr.estMinutesMax} 分钟`, c ? `${c.name}：${n1(c.estMinutes)} 分钟` : dash, fits(Boolean(c))],
      ['峰值内存', `≤ ${GATES.asr.peakMemGbMax} GB`, c ? `${c.name}：${n2(c.peakMemGb)} GB` : dash, fits(Boolean(c))],
      ['说话人分离 DER（干净混合）', `≤ ${pct(GATES.diar.derMax, 0)}`, `${r.diar.pick.model}，阈值 ${n2(r.diar.pick.threshold)}：${pct(point.der, 2)}`, fits(gate.derOk)],
      ['人数（估计与实际相差不超过 1 的场次）', `≥ ${GATES.diar.countSessionsMin}/${point.sessions}`, `${point.countHits}/${point.sessions}`, fits(gate.countOk)],
      ['麦克风轨也分人（远场 DER）', `≤ ${pct(GATES.diar.farFieldDerMax, 0)} 才开放`, pct(far.point.der, 2), far.gate.pass ? '开放' : '**v1 不开放**'],
      ['回声消除', `插入率 ≤ ${pct(GATES.aec.insertionMax, 0)} 且近端 CER 增加 ≤ ${(GATES.aec.nearCerIncreaseMax * 100).toFixed(0)} 个百分点（全部条件）`, aecMeasured, aec.status === 'ok' ? `做：${aec.chosen}` : '**不做**：提示“外放时请戴耳机”'],
    ],
  );
}

function asrSection(a) {
  const eligible = new Set(a.pick.eligible.map((c) => c.name));
  const chosen = a.pick.chosen?.name;
  // 汇总（buildReport）保证每个候选都有预筛与全量的结果（全量可以是“跳过”），所以这里只有“跳过”与“正常”两种行
  const rows = a.rows.map((r) => {
    const pre = [pct(r.pre.cer, 2), `${n4(r.pre.rtf8)} / ${r.pre.rtf4 === null ? dash : n4(r.pre.rtf4)}`];
    if (r.status === 'skipped') return [r.id, ...pre, dash, dash, dash, dash, dash, dash, `跳过：${r.reason}`];
    const verdict = r.id === chosen ? '**默认**' : eligible.has(r.id) ? '速度与内存达标' : '速度或内存超出';
    return [r.id, ...pre, pct(r.cer, 2), n4(r.rtf8), n1(r.loadSec), r.needsPunct ? `否（另加 ${n2(r.punctMin)} 分钟）` : '是', `${n1(r.batchMin)} / ${r.e2e ? n1(r.e2e.totalMin) : dash}`, n2(r.peakMemGb), verdict];
  });
  const e2e = a.rows.filter((r) => r.e2e).map((r) => [r.id, pct(r.e2e.cer, 2), n4(r.e2e.rtfWall), n2(r.e2e.peakMemGb)]);
  return [
    table(['候选', '预筛 CER', '预筛实时倍数（8 / 4 线程）', '全量 CER', '全量实时倍数（8 线程）', '加载（秒）', '自带标点', '估计用时（分钟）：批识别 / 端到端', '峰值内存（GB）', '结论'], rows),
    '',
    `候选：${a.rows.map((r) => `${r.id} = ${r.name}`).join('；')}。`,
    '',
    '端到端（VAD 自动切段 + 识别，整条录音）：',
    '',
    table(['候选', '端到端 CER', '实时倍数（整条录音，含加载）', '峰值内存（GB）'], e2e),
  ].join('\n');
}

function stagesSection(r) {
  const chosen = r.asr.pick.chosen;
  if (!chosen) return '没有选中的识别候选，不列各阶段的用时。';
  const row = r.asr.rows.find((x) => x.id === chosen.name);
  const st = row.stages;
  const rows = [
    ['解码（两条轨）', n2(st.decode)],
    [r.useAec ? '回声消除（麦克风轨）' : '回声消除（没有选中处理方式，不做）', n2(st.aec)],
    ['VAD（两条轨）', n2(st.vad)],
    [r.diarMic ? '说话人分离（两条轨）' : '说话人分离（系统轨）', n2(st.diar)],
    ['识别（说话时间 × 全量实时倍数）', n2(st.asr)],
    ['模型加载（两条轨各一次）', n2((2 * row.loadSec) / 60)],
    [row.needsPunct ? '加标点（识别器不带标点）' : '加标点（识别器自带，不另算）', n2(row.punctMin)],
    ['**批识别估计合计**', n2(row.batchMin)],
    ['**端到端估计**（解码 + 回声 + 分离 + 两条轨的 VAD 与识别一体程序 + 标点）', n2(row.e2e.totalMin)],
    ['**取大者（用于门槛）**', n2(row.estMinutes)],
  ];
  return [`默认识别器 ${chosen.name}，一场 ${r.meetingMin} 分钟的会议（分钟）：`, '', table(['阶段', '用时'], rows)].join('\n');
}

function diarSection(d) {
  const thresholds = [...new Set(Object.values(d.curves).flatMap((c) => c.points.map((p) => p.threshold)))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const curveRows = Object.entries(d.curves).map(([name, v]) => [
    name,
    ...thresholds.map((t) => {
      const p = v.points.find((x) => x.threshold === t);
      return p ? `${pct(p.der)} · ${p.countHits}/${p.sessions}` : dash;
    }),
    v.knownCount ? `${pct(v.knownCount.der)} · ${v.knownCount.countHits}/${v.knownCount.sessions}` : dash,
  ]);
  const { pick, point, far, rotations, timing } = d;
  const sessionRows = (list) => list.map((s) => [s.mixId, s.actual, `${s.estimated} / ${s.estimated3s}`, pct(s.der)]);
  const sessionHeader = ['会议', '实际远端人数', '估计人数（出现过 / 说话满 3 秒）', 'DER'];
  return [
    '声纹模型 × 聚类阈值：DER · 人数合格场次（最后一列是已知人数时的 DER，只作参考）：',
    '',
    table(['声纹模型', ...thresholds.map((t) => n1(t)), '已知人数'], curveRows),
    '',
    `胜出：${pick.model}，最低 DER ${pct(pick.minDer, 2)}，距最低点 1 个百分点内的阈值区间 ${n1(pick.band.lo)}～${n1(pick.band.hi)}，取中点 ${n2(pick.threshold)}${pick.needsConfirm ? '（不在扫描的阈值上，已补测）' : ''}。`,
    '',
    `该设置合计：DER ${pct(point.der, 2)}（漏检 ${pct(point.miss, 2)}、误检 ${pct(point.fa, 2)}、混淆 ${pct(point.conf, 2)}），人数合格 ${point.countHits}/${point.sessions} 场，平均人数误差 ${n2(point.countMeanAbsError)}。`,
    '',
    '按远端说话人数分组：',
    '',
    table(['远端说话人数', '场数', 'DER', '人数合格场次'], d.groups.map((g) => [g.actual, g.sessions, pct(g.der, 2), `${g.countHits}/${g.sessions}`])),
    '',
    '每场：',
    '',
    table(sessionHeader, sessionRows(d.perSession)),
    '',
    `远场（阵列第 1 通道，含全部参会者）：DER ${pct(far.point.der, 2)}，人数合格 ${far.point.countHits}/${far.point.sessions} 场；${far.gate.pass ? '过' : '没过'} ${pct(GATES.diar.farFieldDerMax, 0)} 的门槛。`,
    '',
    table(sessionHeader, sessionRows(far.perSession)),
    '',
    `“我”换成每位其余参会者（补充，不是门槛）：${rotations.sessions} 种组合，DER ${pct(rotations.der, 2)}，人数合格 ${rotations.countHits}/${rotations.sessions}，平均人数误差 ${n2(rotations.countMeanAbsError)}。`,
    '',
    `计时（三场、8 线程、含模型加载）：实时倍数 ${n4(timing.rtfWall)}，峰值内存 ${timing.peakWorkingSetMb} MB，别的程序的 CPU 占用均值 ${pct(timing.othersLoadMean, 0)}。`,
  ].join('\n');
}

function echoSection(e) {
  const names = Object.keys(e.variants);
  const ids = e.variants[names[0]].conditions.map((c) => c.id);
  const by = (fmt) => names.map((n) => [n, ...e.variants[n].conditions.map(fmt)]);
  const head = ['处理方式', ...ids];
  return [
    `识别器：${e.asr}。基线（干净近端、不处理）CER ${pct(e.baselineCer, 2)}；没有回声时同样流程的底线：远端单讲段里识别出的字数占参考的 ${pct(e.floor.insertion, 2)}，近端 CER ${pct(e.floor.nearCer, 2)}。`,
    '',
    '回声插入率（远端单讲段里识别出的字数 ÷ 远端实际字数）：',
    '',
    table(head, by((c) => pct(c.insertion))),
    '',
    '近端 CER 比基线增加（百分点）：',
    '',
    table(head, by((c) => n1(c.nearCerIncrease * 100))),
    '',
    '远端单讲段的回声消减量 ERLE（dB，各场平均）：',
    '',
    table(head, by((c) => n1(c.erleDb))),
    '',
    '各处理方式对照门槛：',
    '',
    table(['处理方式', '全部条件达标', '未达标的条件', '近端 CER 平均增加（百分点）'], e.pick.report.map((x) => [x.name, x.pass ? '是' : '否', x.failed.length ? x.failed.join('、') : dash, n1(x.meanIncrease * 100)])),
  ].join('\n');
}

function basisSection(r) {
  const rows = [
    ['“我”轨说话占比 / 系统轨说话占比（评测集实测）', `${pct(r.speechFrac.me)} / ${pct(r.speechFrac.sys)}`],
    ['一场 60 分钟会议的转写稿字数（按评测集的字数密度）', `${Math.round(r.chars)} 字`],
    ['解码实时倍数（ffmpeg 到 16 kHz 单声道）', n4(r.rtf.decode)],
    ['VAD 实时倍数', n4(r.rtf.vad)],
    ['回声消除实时倍数', r.useAec ? n4(r.rtf.aec) : '没有选中处理方式，不算'],
    ['说话人分离实时倍数', n4(r.rtf.diar)],
    ['加标点：模型加载 / 每千个单位', `${n2(r.punct.loadSec)} 秒 / ${n2(r.punct.secPer1000)} 秒`],
  ];
  return table(['量', '值'], rows);
}

/**
 * 整份报告。generatedAt：生成时间的文字（调用方从系统时钟取，这里不取）。
 * 某个结果文件缺字段时，排出来的会是 NaN / undefined：这种报告不能交出去，直接报错。
 */
export function renderReport(r, { generatedAt }) {
  const chosen = r.asr.pick.chosen;
  const notes = [];
  if (chosen && r.echo.asr !== chosen.name) notes.push(`注意：回声评测用的识别器是 ${r.echo.asr}，默认识别器是 ${chosen.name}；回声部分的数字是用 ${r.echo.asr} 测的。`);
  const md = [
    `# S4 结果汇总（生成于 ${generatedAt}）`,
    '',
    '## 结论一览',
    '',
    verdicts(r),
    ...(notes.length ? ['', ...notes] : []),
    '',
    '## 识别',
    '',
    asrSection(r.asr),
    '',
    '## 流水线用时估算',
    '',
    stagesSection(r),
    '',
    '估算用到的实测量：',
    '',
    basisSection(r),
    '',
    '## 说话人分离',
    '',
    diarSection(r.diar),
    '',
    '## 回声消除',
    '',
    echoSection(r.echo),
    '',
  ].join('\n');
  const bad = md.match(/NaN|undefined|Infinity|\bnull\b/);
  if (bad) throw new Error(`报告里出现了无效的值（${bad[0]}）：某个阶段的结果文件缺字段`);
  return md;
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 9：运行测试，确认通过**

```powershell
node --test scripts/s4/lib/render.test.mjs
```

预期：输出末尾的统计里有 `# pass 13` 和 `# fail 0`（其余的 `# …` 行不用管）。

- [ ] **步骤 10：创建 `scripts/s4/asr.mjs`**

识别评测的命令行脚本，三个阶段：`prescreen`（速度预选）、`full`（全量准确率）、`vad`（决赛圈的端到端）。它调用任务 13 的 `asr-eval`，并用刚写好的 `report` 选决赛圈。

```js
// 识别评测。结果写在 <S4 目录>\results\asr\<候选>\ 下，已有的结果不重跑（加 --force 重跑）。
//   node scripts/s4/asr.mjs prescreen --home <目录> [--cands a,b] [--threads 8,4]
//       三场会议（头、中、尾）、每人前 5 分钟的标注段，每个候选分别用 8 与 4 线程各跑一遍：量实时倍数、内存与 CER。
//       跑的时候别的程序不要占 CPU（这是计时）；别的程序的 CPU 占用会记在结果里。
// 批识别的做法：官方程序把一次调用里的所有文件当作同一个批次、按最长的那个补齐来解码，批里长短差得多时又慢又占内存
// （实测 paraformer：278 段一次调用，实时倍数 0.037、2.9 GB；按时长排序后每批 32 段，0.0076、0.5 GB）。
// 所以所有批识别都先按段长从短到长排序，再每批 --batch 段（默认 32）。
//   node scripts/s4/asr.mjs full --home <目录> [--cands a,b]
//       全部 25 条近场录音的全部标注段，8 线程。预筛里“只算识别的估计用时”超过门槛 2 倍的候选不跑全量（记录在案）。
//   node scripts/s4/asr.mjs vad --home <目录> [--cands a,b]
//       决赛圈（全量里速度与内存达标、CER 最低的前 3 个；也可以用 --cands 指定）对 25 条整条近场录音做“VAD + 识别”端到端。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { decodeFiles, decodeWithVad, punctuationRate, scorePairs, summarizeBatches } from './lib/asr-eval.mjs';
import { cerTotal } from './lib/cer.mjs';
import { log, parseArgs, pickThree } from './lib/cli.mjs';
import { GATES } from './lib/decide.mjs';
import { loadSessions, refText } from './lib/dataset.mjs';
import { homeFrom, readJson, s4Paths, writeJsonAtomic } from './lib/home.mjs';
import { measureAsync } from './lib/measure.mjs';
import { segDir, segmentItems } from './lib/prep.mjs';
import { asrOnlyMinutes, finalists, speechFractions } from './lib/report.mjs';
import { ASR_CANDIDATES } from './lib/sherpa.mjs';

const { positional, flags } = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
const stage = positional[0];
const paths = s4Paths(homeFrom(flags.home));
const sessions = loadSessions(paths.dataset);
const speechFrac = speechFractions(sessions);
const run = (req) => measureAsync({ priority: 'Normal', ...req });
const force = flags.force === true;
const batchSize = Number(flags.batch ?? 32);
if (!Number.isInteger(batchSize) || batchSize < 1) throw new Error(`--batch 要是正整数：${flags.batch}`);
const known = ASR_CANDIDATES.map((c) => c.id);
const cands = flags.cands ? String(flags.cands).split(',') : known;
for (const c of cands) if (!known.includes(c)) throw new Error(`不认识的识别候选：${c}（可选：${known.join('、')}）`);

const resultFile = (cand, name) => path.join(paths.results, 'asr', cand, name);
const stamp = () => new Date().toISOString();

/** 某些会议的近场标注段（可只取终点不超过 maxEndSec 的），每段带上它的 wav 与参考文本 */
function itemsFor(subset, maxEndSec = Infinity) {
  return subset.flatMap((s) =>
    s.tracks.flatMap((t) =>
      segmentItems(segDir(paths.work, s.id, t.speaker), t.segments)
        .filter((x) => x.end <= maxEndSec)
        .map((x) => ({ session: s.id, speaker: t.speaker, index: x.index, file: x.file, ref: x.text, dur: x.end - x.start })),
    ),
  );
}

function scoreItems(items, texts) {
  const pairs = items.map((it, i) => [it.ref, texts[i]]);
  const bySession = {};
  for (const id of new Set(items.map((i) => i.session))) bySession[id] = cerTotal(pairs.filter((_, i) => items[i].session === id)).rate;
  return { score: scorePairs(pairs), bySession, punctRate: punctuationRate(texts) };
}

async function decodeStage({ cand, name, items: unsorted, threads, extra }) {
  const items = [...unsorted].sort((a, b) => a.dur - b.dur); // 稳定排序：段长一样的保持原来的先后
  const file = resultFile(cand, name);
  if (fs.existsSync(file) && !force) {
    log(`已有，跳过：${file}`);
    return readJson(file);
  }
  log(`${cand}  ${name}：${items.length} 段，${threads} 线程`);
  const { texts, batches } = await decodeFiles({
    run,
    exe: paths.exe('sherpa-onnx-offline'),
    candId: cand,
    modelsRoot: paths.models,
    threads,
    files: items.map((i) => i.file),
    outDir: path.join(paths.work, 'asr-out', cand, name.replace(/\.json$/, '')),
    chunk: { maxCount: batchSize },
  });
  const summary = summarizeBatches(batches);
  const result = { cand, name, threads, batchSize, sortedByDuration: true, files: items.length, summary, batches, ...scoreItems(items, texts), ...extra, finishedAt: stamp() };
  writeJsonAtomic(file, result);
  writeJsonAtomic(file.replace(/\.json$/, '.texts.json'), items.map((it, i) => ({ session: it.session, speaker: it.speaker, index: it.index, ref: it.ref, hyp: texts[i] })));
  log(`  CER ${(result.score.cer.rate * 100).toFixed(2)}%  实时倍数 ${summary.rtf.toFixed(4)}  加载 ${summary.loadSecMean?.toFixed(1) ?? '-'} 秒  峰值内存 ${summary.peakWorkingSetMb} MB  别的程序占用均值 ${(summary.othersLoadMean * 100).toFixed(0)}%`);
  return result;
}

async function prescreen() {
  const picked = pickThree(sessions);
  const items = itemsFor(picked, 300);
  const threadList = String(flags.threads ?? '8,4').split(',').map(Number);
  for (const cand of cands) {
    for (const threads of threadList) {
      await decodeStage({ cand, name: `prescreen-t${threads}.json`, items, threads, extra: { stage: 'prescreen', sessions: picked.map((s) => s.id), excerptSec: 300 } });
    }
  }
}

async function full() {
  const items = itemsFor(sessions);
  for (const cand of cands) {
    const pre = resultFile(cand, 'prescreen-t8.json');
    if (!fs.existsSync(pre)) throw new Error(`${cand} 还没有预筛结果（${pre}），先跑 prescreen`);
    const est = asrOnlyMinutes(speechFrac, readJson(pre).summary.rtf);
    const budget = GATES.asr.estMinutesMax;
    const file = resultFile(cand, 'full-t8.json');
    if (est > 2 * budget) {
      writeJsonAtomic(file, { cand, name: 'full-t8.json', skipped: true, reason: `预筛得到只算识别的估计用时 ${est.toFixed(1)} 分钟，超过门槛 ${budget} 分钟的 2 倍，不跑全量`, finishedAt: stamp() });
      log(`${cand}：预筛估计 ${est.toFixed(1)} 分钟，超过 ${2 * budget} 分钟，不跑全量（已记录）`);
      continue;
    }
    await decodeStage({ cand, name: 'full-t8.json', items, threads: 8, extra: { stage: 'full', asrOnlyEstimateMinFromPrescreen: est } });
  }
}

async function vad() {
  let chosen = cands;
  if (!flags.cands) {
    const loaded = known.map((id) => ({ id, full: fs.existsSync(resultFile(id, 'full-t8.json')) ? readJson(resultFile(id, 'full-t8.json')) : null }));
    const missing = loaded.filter((c) => !c.full).map((c) => c.id);
    if (missing.length) throw new Error(`这些候选还没有全量结果：${missing.join('、')}。先跑 full，或者用 --cands 指定要做端到端的候选`);
    chosen = finalists({ cands: loaded, speechFrac }).map((c) => c.id);
    log(`决赛圈：${chosen.join('、')}`);
    if (chosen.length === 0) throw new Error('没有候选在速度与内存上达标，没有决赛圈');
  }
  for (const cand of chosen) {
    const file = resultFile(cand, 'vad-t8.json');
    if (fs.existsSync(file) && !force) {
      log(`已有，跳过：${file}`);
      continue;
    }
    const tracks = [];
    const batches = [];
    const texts = [];
    for (const s of sessions) {
      for (const t of s.tracks) {
        const tag = `${s.id}_${t.speaker}`;
        log(`${cand}  VAD 端到端 ${tag}`);
        const r = await decodeWithVad({ run, exe: paths.exe('sherpa-onnx-vad-with-offline-asr'), candId: cand, modelsRoot: paths.models, threads: 8, vadModel: path.join(paths.downloads, 'silero_vad.onnx'), wav: t.wav, outDir: path.join(paths.work, 'asr-out', cand, 'vad-t8'), tag });
        const hyp = r.segments.map((x) => x.text).join('');
        tracks.push({ session: s.id, speaker: t.speaker, ref: refText(t.segments), hyp, segments: r.segments.length, audioSec: t.durationSec });
        texts.push({ session: s.id, speaker: t.speaker, segments: r.segments });
        batches.push(r.batch);
      }
    }
    const pairs = tracks.map((t) => [t.ref, t.hyp]);
    const summary = summarizeBatches(batches);
    const result = { cand, name: 'vad-t8.json', stage: 'vad', threads: 8, tracks: tracks.map(({ ref, hyp, ...rest }) => ({ ...rest, cer: cerTotal([[ref, hyp]]).rate })), summary, batches, score: scorePairs(pairs), punctRate: punctuationRate(tracks.map((t) => t.hyp)), finishedAt: stamp() };
    writeJsonAtomic(file, result);
    writeJsonAtomic(file.replace(/\.json$/, '.texts.json'), texts);
    log(`  端到端 CER ${(result.score.cer.rate * 100).toFixed(2)}%  实时倍数（按整条录音）${summary.rtf.toFixed(4)}  峰值内存 ${summary.peakWorkingSetMb} MB`);
  }
}

const stages = { prescreen, full, vad };
if (!stages[stage]) throw new Error('用法：node scripts/s4/asr.mjs <prescreen|full|vad> --home <S4 目录> [--cands a,b] [--threads 8,4] [--force]');
await stages[stage]();
log(`${stage} 完成`);
```

- [ ] **步骤 12：创建 `scripts/s4/report.mjs`**

```js
// 汇总 S4 的全部结果：按事先写死的门槛与决策规则（lib/decide.mjs）下结论，写出 <S4 目录>\results\report.json 与 report.md（填设计文档 3.9 用）。
//   node scripts/s4/report.mjs --home <S4 目录>
// 缺哪一阶段的结果就报错并说明先跑什么，不拿残缺的数字下结论。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { log, parseArgs } from './lib/cli.mjs';
import { loadSessions } from './lib/dataset.mjs';
import { homeFrom, readJson, s4Paths, writeJsonAtomic } from './lib/home.mjs';
import { renderReport } from './lib/render.mjs';
import { buildReport } from './lib/report.mjs';
import { ASR_CANDIDATES } from './lib/sherpa.mjs';

const { flags } = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
const paths = s4Paths(homeFrom(flags.home));

const readIf = (file) => (fs.existsSync(file) ? readJson(file) : null);
const readDir = (dir) => (fs.existsSync(dir) ? fs.readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.json')).sort().map((f) => readJson(path.join(dir, f))) : []);

const speedFile = path.join(paths.results, 'speed.json');
if (!fs.existsSync(speedFile)) throw new Error(`没有 ${speedFile}，先跑 node scripts/s4/speed.mjs`);

const asrFile = (id, name) => path.join(paths.results, 'asr', id, name);
const candidates = ASR_CANDIDATES.map((c) => ({
  id: c.id,
  name: c.name,
  prescreen8: readIf(asrFile(c.id, 'prescreen-t8.json')),
  prescreen4: readIf(asrFile(c.id, 'prescreen-t4.json')),
  full: readIf(asrFile(c.id, 'full-t8.json')),
  vad: readIf(asrFile(c.id, 'vad-t8.json')),
}));
const diarDir = (sub) => path.join(paths.results, 'diar', sub);
const diar = {
  sweep: readDir(diarDir('sweep')),
  confirm: readDir(diarDir('confirm')),
  far: readDir(diarDir('far')),
  rotations: readDir(diarDir('rotations')),
  timing: readIf(path.join(diarDir('timing'), 'timing.json')),
};

const report = buildReport({ sessions: loadSessions(paths.dataset), speed: readJson(speedFile), candidates, diar, echo: readDir(path.join(paths.results, 'echo')) });
const md = renderReport(report, { generatedAt: `${new Date().toISOString()}（UTC）` });

writeJsonAtomic(path.join(paths.results, 'report.json'), report);
fs.writeFileSync(path.join(paths.results, 'report.md'), md);
log(`已写出 ${path.join(paths.results, 'report.md')} 与 report.json`);
console.log(md.split('\n## ')[1].replace(/^/, '## '));
```

## 任务 19：下载与摘要核对

**目标：** 三个模块：`download`——断点续传的下载（只管网络与文件，摘要对不对是调用方的事）；`fetch-plan`——要下载的 17 个条目、它们该核对的摘要与解压到哪里（纯数据与纯函数）；`fetch-run`——把两者连起来：先取各分类的 `checksum.txt`（必须与写死的 GitHub API 摘要一致才信），再逐个条目核对 SHA-256，已有且相符的不重下，下完不符的改名 `.BAD` 且不拿来用，核对过的记进 `fetch-manifest.json`。只下载、只算摘要，不运行下载来的任何东西。`fetch.mjs` 是命令行脚本。

**文件：**

- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/download.test.mjs` — `download.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/download.mjs` — 断点续传的下载
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/fetch-plan.test.mjs` — `fetch-plan.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/fetch-plan.mjs` — 要下载的条目表、摘要规则与解压位置（纯数据与纯函数）
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/fetch-run.test.mjs` — `fetch-run.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/fetch-run.mjs` — 下载并核对一批条目、记进下载清单
- 创建：`scripts/s4/fetch.mjs` — 命令行：下载官方程序、模型与评测集并核对 SHA-256

**接口：**

- 依赖：`home.mjs`
- 提供：`sha256File(file)`；`download(url, dest, { tries = 8, stallMs = 90_000, retryDelayMs = 3000, progressMs = 30_000, onLog = () => {} } = {})`；`CHECKSUM_FILES`；`checksumUrl`；`ITEMS`；`GROUPS`；`parseChecksums(text)`；`expectedDigest(item, checks)`；`selectItems(groups)`；`fetchAll({ home, items, checksumFiles, checksumUrlFor, say = () => {}, downloadOpts = {} })`
- 测试：`download.test.mjs` 18 个；`fetch-plan.test.mjs` 6 个；`fetch-run.test.mjs` 11 个

- [ ] **步骤 1：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/download.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `download.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import http from 'node:http';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { download, sha256File } from './download.mjs';

// 不重复的确定内容：每 32 字节是一个序号的 SHA-256，起点错一个字节都能看出来
const CONTENT = Buffer.concat(Array.from({ length: 31_250 }, (_, i) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(i)).digest()));
const FAST = { retryDelayMs: 0, stallMs: 5000 };

async function withServer(handler, fn) {
  const requests = [];
  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
    requests.push({ range: req.headers.range, encoding: req.headers['accept-encoding'] });
    handler(req, res, requests.length);
  });
  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
  const dir = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'record-dl-'));
  try {
    await fn({ url: `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}/f.bin`, dest: path.join(dir, 'sub', 'f.bin'), requests });
  } finally {
    server.closeAllConnections();
    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
    fs.rmSync(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

// 正常的服务器：支持 Range（range=false 则无视 Range 总是整份返回）
function send(req, res, { range = true, content = CONTENT } = {}) {
  const m = range && req.headers.range ? /^bytes=(\d+)-$/.exec(req.headers.range) : null;
  if (m) {
    const start = Number(m[1]);
    if (start >= content.length) {
      res.writeHead(416, { 'Content-Range': `bytes */${content.length}` });
      return res.end();
    }
    res.writeHead(206, { 'Content-Length': content.length - start, 'Content-Range': `bytes ${start}-${content.length - 1}/${content.length}` });
    return res.end(content.subarray(start));
  }
  res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Length': content.length });
  return res.end(content);
}

// 声明了完整长度，只发 n 字节就断开连接
function cutAfter(res, n, content = CONTENT) {
  res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Length': content.length });
  res.write(content.subarray(0, n), () => setTimeout(() => res.socket.destroy(), 30));
}

const partOf = (dest) => `${dest}.part`;

test('sha256File：与 crypto 直接算的一致；文件不存在就报错', async () => {
  const f = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `record-sha-${process.pid}.bin`);
  fs.writeFileSync(f, CONTENT);
  try {
    assert.equal(await sha256File(f), crypto.createHash('sha256').update(CONTENT).digest('hex'));
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(f);
  }
  await assert.rejects(sha256File(f), /ENOENT/);
});

test('一次下完：内容一致，建好目录，不留 .part，请求里不要压缩传输、不带 Range', async () => {
  await withServer(send, async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
    await download(url, dest, FAST);
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(partOf(dest)), false);
    assert.deepEqual(requests, [{ range: undefined, encoding: 'identity' }]);
  });
});

test('中途断开后用 Range 接着下，拼出来的内容一字节不差', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => (n === 1 ? cutAfter(res, 300_000) : send(req, res)),
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      await download(url, dest, FAST);
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.equal(requests.length, 2);
      assert.match(requests[1].range, /^bytes=[1-9]\d*-$/);
      assert.equal(fs.existsSync(partOf(dest)), false);
    },
  );
});

test('续传时服务器无视 Range、整份重发：丢掉旧的部分，不能把整份接在后面', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => (n === 1 ? cutAfter(res, 300_000) : send(req, res, { range: false })),
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      await download(url, dest, FAST);
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.match(requests[1].range, /^bytes=[1-9]\d*-$/);
    },
  );
});

test('续传时服务器给的起点不是请求的起点：丢弃已下的部分从头来，不拼接', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => {
      if (n === 1) return cutAfter(res, 300_000);
      if (n === 2) {
        res.writeHead(206, { 'Content-Length': CONTENT.length, 'Content-Range': `bytes 0-${CONTENT.length - 1}/${CONTENT.length}` });
        return res.end(CONTENT);
      }
      return send(req, res);
    },
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      await download(url, dest, FAST);
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.equal(requests.length, 3);
      assert.equal(requests[2].range, undefined, '第三次应当从头下，不带 Range');
    },
  );
});

test('206 没带 Content-Range：同样丢弃重来', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => {
      if (n === 1) return cutAfter(res, 300_000);
      if (n === 2) {
        res.writeHead(206, { 'Content-Length': 10 });
        return res.end(CONTENT.subarray(0, 10));
      }
      return send(req, res);
    },
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      await download(url, dest, FAST);
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.equal(requests.length, 3);
      assert.equal(requests[2].range, undefined, '丢弃后第三次应当从头下');
    },
  );
});

test('.part 其实已经下完（改名前被打断）：416 并且总长相符就直接改名，不再下载内容', async () => {
  await withServer(send, async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(partOf(dest), CONTENT);
    await download(url, dest, FAST);
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
    assert.equal(requests.length, 1);
    assert.equal(requests[0].range, `bytes=${CONTENT.length}-`);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(partOf(dest)), false);
  });
});

test('.part 比服务器上的文件还长：416 但总长对不上，丢弃重下', async () => {
  await withServer(send, async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
    fs.writeFileSync(partOf(dest), Buffer.concat([CONTENT, Buffer.from('多出来的')]));
    await download(url, dest, FAST);
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
    assert.equal(requests.length, 2);
    assert.equal(requests[1].range, undefined);
  });
});

test('416 没有给总长：不敢认，丢弃重下', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => {
      if (n === 1) {
        res.writeHead(416);
        return res.end();
      }
      return send(req, res);
    },
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(partOf(dest), CONTENT);
      await download(url, dest, FAST);
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.equal(requests.length, 2);
      assert.equal(requests[1].range, undefined, '丢弃后应当从头下');
    },
  );
});

test('卡住（超过 stallMs 没有新数据）就放弃这次连接，接着下', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => {
      if (n > 1) return send(req, res);
      res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Length': CONTENT.length });
      res.write(CONTENT.subarray(0, 200_000)); // 发一部分后不再有动静
    },
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      await download(url, dest, { retryDelayMs: 0, stallMs: 400 });
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.match(requests[1].range, /^bytes=[1-9]\d*-$/);
    },
  );
});

test('连响应头都迟迟不来也算卡住', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => {
      if (n > 1) send(req, res);
    },
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      await download(url, dest, { retryDelayMs: 0, stallMs: 300 });
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.equal(requests.length, 2);
    },
  );
});

test('服务器一直回错误码：试满 tries 次后报错，说明原因，不留下目标文件', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res) => {
      res.writeHead(404);
      res.end('no');
    },
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      await assert.rejects(download(url, dest, { ...FAST, tries: 3 }), (e) => /下载失败（试了 3 次）：http:\/\/127\.0\.0\.1:\d+\/f\.bin：HTTP 404/.test(e.message));
      assert.equal(requests.length, 3);
      assert.equal(fs.existsSync(dest), false);
    },
  );
});

test('每次中断都记到日志里；两次之间等的时间随次数加长（100、200 ms），最后一次失败后不再多等', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res) => {
      res.writeHead(500);
      res.end();
    },
    async ({ url, dest }) => {
      const logs = [];
      const t0 = Date.now();
      await assert.rejects(download(url, dest, { tries: 3, stallMs: 5000, retryDelayMs: 100, onLog: (s) => logs.push(s) }), /HTTP 500/);
      assert.deepEqual(logs, ['  下载中断（第 1/3 次）：HTTP 500', '  下载中断（第 2/3 次）：HTTP 500', '  下载中断（第 3/3 次）：HTTP 500']);
      const spent = Date.now() - t0;
      assert.ok(spent >= 290 && spent < 550, `该等 100 + 200 = 300 ms，实际 ${spent} ms`);
    },
  );
});

test('慢但一直有数据，不算卡住：每收到数据就重新计时，不会因为总时间超过 stallMs 被打断', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res) => {
      res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Length': CONTENT.length });
      const pieces = 6;
      const size = Math.ceil(CONTENT.length / pieces);
      let i = 0;
      const timer = setInterval(() => {
        res.write(CONTENT.subarray(i * size, (i + 1) * size));
        if (++i === pieces) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          res.end();
        }
      }, 120);
    },
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      await download(url, dest, { retryDelayMs: 0, stallMs: 400 }); // 总共约 720 ms，超过 400 ms，但每片之间只隔 120 ms
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.equal(requests.length, 1);
    },
  );
});

test('进度日志：已下 / 总长；续传时已下的数从已有的部分算起，总长包含已有的部分', async () => {
  const BIG = Buffer.concat([CONTENT, CONTENT, CONTENT, CONTENT]);
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => (n === 1 ? cutAfter(res, 1_500_000, BIG) : send(req, res, { content: BIG })),
    async ({ url, dest }) => {
      const logs = [];
      await download(url, dest, { ...FAST, progressMs: 0, onLog: (s) => logs.push(s) });
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(BIG));
      const progress = logs.filter((s) => s.endsWith(' MB'));
      assert.ok(progress.length > 10);
      assert.ok(progress.every((s) => /^ {2}f\.bin {2}\d \/ 4 MB$/.test(s)), progress.join('\n'));
      assert.equal(progress.at(-1), '  f.bin  4 / 4 MB');
      const done = progress.map((s) => Number(/ (\d) \//.exec(s)[1]));
      assert.deepEqual(done, [...done].sort((a, b) => a - b), '续传后已下的数不能倒退');
    },
  );
});

test('默认每 30 秒才记一次进度，短下载里一条也没有', async () => {
  await withServer(send, async ({ url, dest }) => {
    const logs = [];
    await download(url, dest, { ...FAST, onLog: (s) => logs.push(s) });
    assert.deepEqual(logs, []);
  });
});

test('下完后不留计时器：进程能马上退出，不用等 stallMs', async () => {
  const script = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `record-dl-child-${process.pid}.mjs`);
  fs.writeFileSync(script, `import { download } from ${JSON.stringify(pathToFileURL(fileURLToPath(new URL('./download.mjs', import.meta.url))).href)};\nawait download(process.argv[2], process.argv[3], { stallMs: 60_000 });\n`);
  try {
    await withServer(send, async ({ url, dest }) => {
      const child = spawn(process.execPath, [script, url, dest], { stdio: 'inherit' });
      const code = await new Promise((resolve) => {
        const t = setTimeout(() => {
          child.kill();
          resolve('超过 10 秒还没退出');
        }, 10_000);
        child.on('exit', (c) => {
          clearTimeout(t);
          resolve(c);
        });
      });
      assert.equal(code, 0);
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
    });
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(script, { force: true });
  }
});

test('服务器没给长度（分块传输）也能下；没给长度时中途断开同样会重试续传', async () => {
  await withServer(
    (req, res, n) => {
      if (n === 1) {
        res.writeHead(200); // 没有 Content-Length
        res.write(CONTENT.subarray(0, 300_000), () => setTimeout(() => res.socket.destroy(), 30));
        return;
      }
      res.writeHead(206, { 'Content-Range': `bytes ${/\d+/.exec(req.headers.range)[0]}-${CONTENT.length - 1}/${CONTENT.length}` });
      res.end(CONTENT.subarray(Number(/\d+/.exec(req.headers.range)[0])));
    },
    async ({ url, dest, requests }) => {
      const logs = [];
      await download(url, dest, { ...FAST, progressMs: 0, onLog: (s) => logs.push(s) });
      assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(dest).equals(CONTENT));
      assert.equal(requests.length, 2);
      const progress = logs.filter((s) => s.endsWith(' MB'));
      assert.ok(progress.length > 0);
      assert.ok(progress.every((s) => /^ {2}f\.bin {2}\d \/ \? MB$/.test(s)), '不知道总长时总长显示 ?');
    },
  );
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 3：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/download.mjs`**

```js
// 断点续传的下载（只管网络与文件；摘要对不对是调用方的事）。
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

export function sha256File(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const h = crypto.createHash('sha256');
    fs.createReadStream(file)
      .on('data', (c) => h.update(c))
      .on('end', () => resolve(h.digest('hex')))
      .on('error', reject);
  });
}

const sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

// 同步写，写完才返回：中断重试时 .part 的大小就是真正落盘的字节数（用流的话，上一次没写完的数据可能在重试后才落盘，续传位置就错了）
function writeAll(fd, chunk) {
  let off = 0;
  while (off < chunk.length) off += fs.writeSync(fd, chunk, off);
}

/**
 * 下载 url 到 dest：先写 dest.part，下完再改名为 dest（中途断开 fetch 会报错，长度它自己核对）。
 * 中途断开，或者 stallMs 毫秒内没有新数据，就用 Range 接着下；最多试 tries 次，仍不行就抛错。
 * 续传位置对不上（服务器无视 Range、给了别的起点、或已下的部分比服务器上的文件还长）时，丢弃已下载的部分从头来，不拼接可疑的数据。
 */
export async function download(url, dest, { tries = 8, stallMs = 90_000, retryDelayMs = 3000, progressMs = 30_000, onLog = () => {} } = {}) {
  const part = `${dest}.part`;
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(dest), { recursive: true });
  let lastError;
  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= tries; attempt++) {
    const ctl = new AbortController();
    let timer;
    const arm = () => {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      timer = setTimeout(() => ctl.abort(), stallMs);
    };
    try {
      const have = fs.existsSync(part) ? fs.statSync(part).size : 0;
      const headers = { 'Accept-Encoding': 'identity' }; // 不要压缩传输：长度对不上就没法核对
      if (have > 0) headers.Range = `bytes=${have}-`;
      arm();
      const res = await fetch(url, { headers, signal: ctl.signal }); // 默认就会跟随重定向（GitHub 的下载链接会跳转）
      if (res.status === 416) {
        // 请求的起点已经在文件末尾之外：总长等于已有长度才说明早已下完，否则这份 .part 是错的
        await res.body?.cancel();
        const size = /^bytes \*\/(\d+)$/.exec(res.headers.get('content-range') ?? '');
        if (size && Number(size[1]) === have) {
          fs.renameSync(part, dest);
          return;
        }
        fs.rmSync(part, { force: true });
        throw new Error(`已下载的 ${have} 字节与服务器上的文件对不上，丢弃重来`);
      }
      if (res.status !== 200 && res.status !== 206) {
        await res.body?.cancel();
        throw new Error(`HTTP ${res.status}`);
      }
      let append = false;
      if (res.status === 206) {
        const range = res.headers.get('content-range') ?? '';
        const from = /^bytes (\d+)-\d+\/(?:\d+|\*)$/.exec(range);
        if (!from || Number(from[1]) !== have) {
          await res.body?.cancel();
          fs.rmSync(part, { force: true });
          throw new Error(`服务器给的续传起点（${range}）与请求的（从 ${have} 字节起）不符，丢弃已下载的部分重来`);
        }
        append = true;
      }
      // 只用于进度显示：中途断开时 fetch 自己会报错（长度够不够它会核对），到这里没报错就是下完了
      const length = res.headers.has('content-length') ? Number(res.headers.get('content-length')) : null;
      const total = length === null ? null : length + (append ? have : 0);
      let got = append ? have : 0;
      let lastLog = Date.now();
      const fd = fs.openSync(part, append ? 'a' : 'w');
      try {
        for await (const chunk of res.body) {
          arm();
          writeAll(fd, chunk);
          got += chunk.length;
          if (Date.now() - lastLog >= progressMs) {
            lastLog = Date.now();
            onLog(`  ${path.basename(dest)}  ${(got / 1e6).toFixed(0)} / ${total === null ? '?' : (total / 1e6).toFixed(0)} MB`);
          }
        }
      } finally {
        fs.closeSync(fd);
      }
      fs.renameSync(part, dest);
      return;
    } catch (e) {
      lastError = e;
      onLog(`  下载中断（第 ${attempt}/${tries} 次）：${e.message}`);
      if (attempt < tries) await sleep(retryDelayMs * attempt);
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  }
  throw new Error(`下载失败（试了 ${tries} 次）：${url}：${lastError.message}`);
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 5：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/fetch-plan.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `fetch-plan.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { CHECKSUM_FILES, checksumUrl, expectedDigest, GROUPS, ITEMS, parseChecksums, selectItems } from './fetch-plan.mjs';

const H1 = 'a'.repeat(64);
const H2 = 'B'.repeat(64);

test('checksum.txt：每行“文件名 空白 64 位摘要”，换行用 LF 或 CRLF 都行，摘要统一成小写，别的行忽略', () => {
  const text = `x.onnx\t${H1}\r\ny.tar.bz2 ${H2}\n\n# 注释\nz.onnx\t${H1.slice(1)}\nw.onnx ${H1} 多余\n  v.onnx   ${H1}  \nu.onnx ${H1}${H1.slice(0, 1)}\n${H1} q.onnx\n前缀 t.onnx ${H1}\n`;
  const m = parseChecksums(text);
  assert.deepEqual([...m], [['x.onnx', H1], ['y.tar.bz2', 'b'.repeat(64)], ['v.onnx', H1]]);
});

test('该核对的摘要：写死的（带来源）、checksum.txt 里的、都没有的', () => {
  const checks = new Map([['t1', new Map([['m.onnx', H1]])]]);
  assert.deepEqual(expectedDigest({ name: 'a', sha256: H2, digestSource: 'GitHub API digest' }, checks), { expected: 'b'.repeat(64), source: 'GitHub API digest', official: true });
  assert.deepEqual(expectedDigest({ name: 'a', sha256: H1, digestSource: '首次下载时算出并记录（官方没有给摘要）' }, checks), { expected: H1, source: '首次下载时算出并记录（官方没有给摘要）', official: false });
  assert.deepEqual(expectedDigest({ name: 'm.onnx', tag: 't1' }, checks), { expected: H1, source: 'checksum.txt', official: true });
  // 写死的摘要优先于 checksum.txt
  assert.deepEqual(expectedDigest({ name: 'm.onnx', tag: 't1', sha256: H2, digestSource: 'GitHub API digest' }, checks), { expected: 'b'.repeat(64), source: 'GitHub API digest', official: true });
  assert.deepEqual(expectedDigest({ name: 'new.onnx', tag: 't1' }, checks), { expected: undefined, source: '无官方摘要（首次计算，记录在案）', official: false });
  assert.deepEqual(expectedDigest({ name: 'm.onnx', tag: 'nope' }, checks).expected, undefined);
  assert.deepEqual(expectedDigest({ name: 'n' }, checks).expected, undefined);
  assert.throws(() => expectedDigest({ name: 'a', sha256: H1 }, checks), /a：写死了摘要却没写来源/);
});

test('按分组取条目；meta 只下 checksum.txt 所以没有条目；不认识的分组报错', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(GROUPS, ['meta', 'tools', 'small', 'asr', 'big', 'data']);
  assert.deepEqual(selectItems(['meta']), []);
  assert.deepEqual(selectItems(['data']).map((i) => i.name), ['AliMeeting-Eval_Ali.tar.gz']);
  assert.equal(selectItems(GROUPS).length, ITEMS.length);
  assert.throws(() => selectItems(['tools', 'smal']), /不认识的分组：smal/);
});

test('下载清单自洽：文件名与网址一致、摘要来源齐全、压缩包都有解压目的地、网址只来自官方发布页与评测集的发布处', () => {
  assert.equal(ITEMS.length, 17);
  assert.deepEqual(Object.fromEntries(GROUPS.slice(1).map((g) => [g, ITEMS.filter((i) => i.group === g).length])), { tools: 1, small: 8, asr: 3, big: 4, data: 1 });
  assert.equal(new Set(ITEMS.map((i) => i.name)).size, ITEMS.length);
  const tags = new Set(CHECKSUM_FILES.map((c) => c.tag));
  for (const i of ITEMS) {
    // 评测集在本地改了名（Eval_Ali.tar.gz → AliMeeting-Eval_Ali.tar.gz），免得和别的东西混；其余文件名与网址一致
    assert.equal(i.url.split('/').at(-1), i.group === 'data' ? 'Eval_Ali.tar.gz' : i.name, `${i.name}：网址的文件名对不上`);
    assert.ok(/^https:\/\/(github\.com\/k2-fsa\/sherpa-onnx\/releases\/download\/|speech-lab-share-data\.oss-cn-shanghai\.aliyuncs\.com\/AliMeeting\/)/.test(i.url), `${i.name}：网址不在预期的发布处：${i.url}`);
    if (i.sha256) {
      assert.match(i.sha256, /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/, `${i.name}：摘要格式不对`);
      assert.ok(i.digestSource, `${i.name}：缺摘要来源`);
    } else {
      assert.ok(tags.has(i.tag), `${i.name}：既没有写死的摘要，也没有可用的 checksum.txt 分类（${i.tag}）`);
    }
    const archive = /\.tar\.(bz2|gz)$/.test(i.name);
    assert.equal(Boolean(i.extractTo), archive, `${i.name}：压缩包要有解压目的地，单文件不能有`);
    if (archive) assert.ok(['tools', 'models', 'data'].includes(i.extractTo));
  }
  for (const c of CHECKSUM_FILES) assert.match(c.sha256, /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
});

test('checksum.txt 的地址：官方发布页下该分类的 checksum.txt', () => {
  assert.equal(checksumUrl('asr-models'), 'https://github.com/k2-fsa/sherpa-onnx/releases/download/asr-models/checksum.txt');
  assert.deepEqual(CHECKSUM_FILES.map((c) => checksumUrl(c.tag)), [
    'https://github.com/k2-fsa/sherpa-onnx/releases/download/asr-models/checksum.txt',
    'https://github.com/k2-fsa/sherpa-onnx/releases/download/speaker-recongition-models/checksum.txt',
    'https://github.com/k2-fsa/sherpa-onnx/releases/download/punctuation-models/checksum.txt',
  ]);
});

test('解压目的地：程序到 tools，模型到 models，评测集到 data', () => {
  const dest = (name) => ITEMS.find((i) => i.name === name).extractTo;
  assert.equal(dest('sherpa-onnx-v1.13.8-win-x64-shared-MD-Release-no-tts.tar.bz2'), 'tools');
  assert.equal(dest('sherpa-onnx-paraformer-zh-int8-2025-10-07.tar.bz2'), 'models');
  assert.equal(dest('sherpa-onnx-pyannote-segmentation-3-0.tar.bz2'), 'models');
  assert.equal(dest('sherpa-onnx-punct-ct-transformer-zh-en-vocab272727-2024-04-12-int8.tar.bz2'), 'models');
  assert.equal(dest('AliMeeting-Eval_Ali.tar.gz'), 'data');
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 7：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/fetch-plan.mjs`**

```js
// S4 要下载的东西（全部来自官方发布页）、它们该核对的摘要与解压到哪里。网络与文件的操作在 fetch.mjs / extract.mjs，这里只有数据与纯函数。

const GH = 'https://github.com/k2-fsa/sherpa-onnx/releases/download';

/**
 * 各发布分类下的 checksum.txt：先用 GitHub API 给出的资产摘要（sha256）核对 checksum.txt 本身，再信它里面各文件的摘要。
 * （下载这三个文件时从 API 取得的摘要，写死在这里，之后任何一次下载都与它比。）
 */
export const CHECKSUM_FILES = Object.freeze([
  { tag: 'asr-models', sha256: '4e34edcb64434bcf533afaee9dcc14b5b2f9c277ed3a745f263e79a4464b28d0' },
  { tag: 'speaker-recongition-models', sha256: 'c3a928cc16d165ce869553f71fcbe6fc1c1b513aee0665414548780a979b2325' },
  { tag: 'punctuation-models', sha256: 'b1bdfef119bd75fef8304c7f9abbd67bcaf5ab22ea3f5fbc1c258c4fd3ea4177' },
]);

/** 某个发布分类下 checksum.txt 的下载地址 */
export const checksumUrl = (tag) => `${GH}/${tag}/checksum.txt`;

const API = 'GitHub API digest';
const FIRST_USE = '首次下载时算出并记录（官方没有给摘要）';
const model = (group, tag, name, extra = {}) => ({ group, name, url: `${GH}/${tag}/${name}`, tag, ...extra });

/**
 * 条目：group 分组；name 文件名；url；摘要的来源三选一：sha256 + digestSource（写死的摘要）或 tag（用该分类的 checksum.txt）；
 * extractTo：压缩包解到 S4 目录下的哪个子目录（单个 .onnx 文件不用解）。
 */
export const ITEMS = Object.freeze([
  { group: 'tools', name: 'sherpa-onnx-v1.13.8-win-x64-shared-MD-Release-no-tts.tar.bz2', url: `${GH}/v1.13.8/sherpa-onnx-v1.13.8-win-x64-shared-MD-Release-no-tts.tar.bz2`, sha256: '876e6b89b8cf84a3a1b375a397507f2cfe9c227c2a945411a11a668475fcb5d3', digestSource: API, extractTo: 'tools' },
  model('small', 'asr-models', 'silero_vad.onnx'),
  { group: 'small', name: 'sherpa-onnx-pyannote-segmentation-3-0.tar.bz2', url: `${GH}/speaker-segmentation-models/sherpa-onnx-pyannote-segmentation-3-0.tar.bz2`, sha256: '24615ee884c897d9d2ba09bb4d30da6bb1b15e685065962db5b02e76e4996488', digestSource: FIRST_USE, extractTo: 'models' },
  model('small', 'speaker-recongition-models', '3dspeaker_speech_eres2net_base_sv_zh-cn_3dspeaker_16k.onnx'),
  model('small', 'speaker-recongition-models', '3dspeaker_speech_eres2net_large_sv_zh-cn_3dspeaker_16k.onnx'),
  model('small', 'speaker-recongition-models', '3dspeaker_speech_campplus_sv_zh-cn_16k-common.onnx'),
  model('small', 'speaker-recongition-models', '3dspeaker_speech_eres2netv2_sv_zh-cn_16k-common.onnx'),
  model('small', 'speaker-recongition-models', 'wespeaker_zh_cnceleb_resnet34.onnx'),
  model('small', 'punctuation-models', 'sherpa-onnx-punct-ct-transformer-zh-en-vocab272727-2024-04-12-int8.tar.bz2', { sha256: 'c0d5aa5f8eeb686032345e180bedf39319dc2e0556781c6264bcadba8328a6e1', digestSource: API, extractTo: 'models' }),
  model('asr', 'asr-models', 'sherpa-onnx-sense-voice-zh-en-ja-ko-yue-int8-2025-09-09.tar.bz2', { extractTo: 'models' }),
  model('asr', 'asr-models', 'sherpa-onnx-paraformer-zh-int8-2025-10-07.tar.bz2', { extractTo: 'models' }),
  // 这个文件比 checksum.txt 新，清单里没有它：摘要取自 GitHub API 给的资产摘要
  model('asr', 'asr-models', 'sherpa-onnx-x-asr-zipformer-transducer-zh-en-punct-int8-2026-06-03.tar.bz2', { sha256: '5d02c36d7b44e886b7c8f0d8e051f8713acab96c264bb6ef9e718be39a6a2224', digestSource: API, extractTo: 'models' }),
  model('big', 'asr-models', 'sherpa-onnx-fire-red-asr2-ctc-zh_en-int8-2026-02-25.tar.bz2', { extractTo: 'models' }),
  model('big', 'asr-models', 'sherpa-onnx-fire-red-asr2-zh_en-int8-2026-02-26.tar.bz2', { extractTo: 'models' }),
  model('big', 'asr-models', 'sherpa-onnx-qwen3-asr-0.6B-int8-2026-03-25.tar.bz2', { extractTo: 'models' }),
  model('big', 'asr-models', 'sherpa-onnx-funasr-nano-int8-2025-12-30.tar.bz2', { extractTo: 'models' }),
  { group: 'data', name: 'AliMeeting-Eval_Ali.tar.gz', url: 'https://speech-lab-share-data.oss-cn-shanghai.aliyuncs.com/AliMeeting/openlr/Eval_Ali.tar.gz', sha256: 'dc47343b2474b5ebcf458927e878155f6ddeb59c85e685b3645c32a1f9578d92', digestSource: FIRST_USE, extractTo: 'data' },
]);

/** 全部分组（meta 只下 checksum.txt，不在 ITEMS 里） */
export const GROUPS = Object.freeze(['meta', ...new Set(ITEMS.map((i) => i.group))]);

/** checksum.txt：每行“文件名 空白 64 位十六进制摘要” → Map(文件名 → 小写摘要)；不符合格式的行忽略 */
export function parseChecksums(text) {
  const map = new Map();
  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    const m = /^(\S+)\s+([0-9a-fA-F]{64})$/.exec(line.trim());
    if (m) map.set(m[1], m[2].toLowerCase());
  }
  return map;
}

/**
 * 一个条目该核对的摘要与来源。checks：Map(分类 → Map(文件名 → 摘要))，来自已用 API 摘要核对过的 checksum.txt。
 * 没有可核对的摘要时 expected 为 undefined：第一次下载时算出来记录，并在清单里标明“无官方摘要”。
 */
export function expectedDigest(item, checks) {
  if (item.sha256) {
    if (!item.digestSource) throw new Error(`${item.name}：写死了摘要却没写来源`);
    return { expected: item.sha256.toLowerCase(), source: item.digestSource, official: item.digestSource === API };
  }
  const fromTag = item.tag ? checks.get(item.tag)?.get(item.name) : undefined;
  if (fromTag) return { expected: fromTag, source: 'checksum.txt', official: true };
  return { expected: undefined, source: '无官方摘要（首次计算，记录在案）', official: false };
}

/** 按分组取条目；不认识的分组报错（拼错了不能悄悄什么都不下） */
export function selectItems(groups) {
  const unknown = groups.filter((g) => !GROUPS.includes(g));
  if (unknown.length) throw new Error(`不认识的分组：${unknown.join('、')}（可选：${GROUPS.join('、')}）`);
  return ITEMS.filter((i) => groups.includes(i.group));
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 9：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/fetch-run.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `fetch-run.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import http from 'node:http';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { fetchAll } from './fetch-run.mjs';
import { readJson } from './home.mjs';

const sha = (b) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
const A = Buffer.from('文件 A 的内容'.repeat(2000));
const B = Buffer.from('file B contents'.repeat(3000));
const C = Buffer.from('c'.repeat(777));
const SUMS = Buffer.from(`b.bin\t${sha(B)}\nother.bin\t${sha(A)}\n`);
const FAST = { retryDelayMs: 0, stallMs: 5000, tries: 2 };

async function withEnv(fn, { routes = {} } = {}) {
  const served = { '/a.bin': A, '/b.bin': B, '/c.bin': C, '/t1/checksum.txt': SUMS, ...routes };
  const requests = [];
  const server = http.createServer((req, res) => {
    requests.push(req.url);
    const body = served[req.url];
    if (!body) {
      res.writeHead(404);
      return res.end();
    }
    res.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Length': body.length });
    return res.end(body);
  });
  await new Promise((r) => server.listen(0, '127.0.0.1', r));
  const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`;
  const home = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'record-fetch-'));
  const logs = [];
  const items = [
    { group: 'g', name: 'a.bin', url: `${base}/a.bin`, sha256: sha(A), digestSource: 'GitHub API digest' },
    { group: 'g', name: 'b.bin', url: `${base}/b.bin`, tag: 't1' },
    { group: 'g', name: 'c.bin', url: `${base}/c.bin` },
  ];
  const run = (over = {}) => fetchAll({ home, items, checksumFiles: [{ tag: 't1', sha256: sha(SUMS) }], checksumUrlFor: (t) => `${base}/${t}/checksum.txt`, say: (s) => logs.push(s), downloadOpts: FAST, ...over });
  const file = (...p) => path.join(home, ...p);
  try {
    await fn({ run, home, file, base, items, requests, logs, served });
  } finally {
    server.closeAllConnections();
    await new Promise((r) => server.close(r));
    fs.rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

test('第一次：全部下载、核对、落盘，清单里记下来源与是否核对过', async () => {
  await withEnv(async ({ run, file, base, requests, logs }) => {
    const { manifest, failed } = await run();
    const done = (name) => logs.find((l) => l.startsWith(`完成：${name}  `));
    assert.match(done('a.bin'), /^完成：a\.bin {2}0\.0 MB {2}\d+ s {2}sha256=[0-9a-f]{64} {2}（GitHub API digest：一致）$/);
    assert.match(done('b.bin'), /^完成：b\.bin {2}0\.0 MB {2}\d+ s {2}sha256=[0-9a-f]{64} {2}（checksum\.txt：一致）$/);
    assert.match(done('c.bin'), /^完成：c\.bin {2}0\.0 MB {2}\d+ s {2}sha256=[0-9a-f]{64} {2}（无官方摘要（首次计算，记录在案））$/);
    assert.deepEqual(logs.filter((l) => l.startsWith('开始：')), ['开始：a.bin', '开始：b.bin', '开始：c.bin']);
    assert.deepEqual(failed, []);
    assert.deepEqual(manifest, {
      'a.bin': { url: `${base}/a.bin`, bytes: A.length, sha256: sha(A), source: 'GitHub API digest', official: true, verified: true },
      'b.bin': { url: `${base}/b.bin`, bytes: B.length, sha256: sha(B), source: 'checksum.txt', official: true, verified: true },
      'c.bin': { url: `${base}/c.bin`, bytes: C.length, sha256: sha(C), source: '无官方摘要（首次计算，记录在案）', official: false, verified: false },
    });
    assert.deepEqual(readJson(file('fetch-manifest.json')), manifest);
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(file('downloads', 'a.bin')).equals(A));
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(file('downloads', 'b.bin')).equals(B));
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(file('downloads', 'c.bin')).equals(C));
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(file('meta', 'checksum-t1.txt')).equals(SUMS));
    assert.deepEqual([...requests].sort(), ['/a.bin', '/b.bin', '/c.bin', '/t1/checksum.txt']);
    assert.equal(fs.readdirSync(file('downloads')).filter((n) => n.endsWith('.part') || n.endsWith('.BAD')).length, 0);
  });
});

test('再跑一次：文件和 checksum.txt 都在且相符，一个网络请求也不发', async () => {
  await withEnv(async ({ run, requests, logs }) => {
    const first = await run();
    requests.length = 0;
    logs.length = 0;
    const second = await run();
    assert.deepEqual(requests, []);
    assert.deepEqual(second.manifest, first.manifest);
    assert.deepEqual(second.failed, []);
    assert.ok(logs.includes(`已有：a.bin  ${sha(A).slice(0, 12)}…`), logs.join('\n'));
    assert.ok(logs.includes(`已有：c.bin  ${sha(C).slice(0, 12)}…`), logs.join('\n'));
    assert.ok(logs.some((l) => l.includes('checksum.txt（t1）摘要与 API 一致，共 2 条')), logs.join('\n'));
  });
});

test('已有的文件摘要不符：删掉重下一次，下对了就用', async () => {
  await withEnv(async ({ run, file, requests, logs }) => {
    await run();
    fs.writeFileSync(file('downloads', 'a.bin'), 'damaged');
    requests.length = 0;
    logs.length = 0;
    const { manifest, failed } = await run();
    assert.deepEqual(failed, []);
    assert.deepEqual(requests, ['/a.bin']);
    assert.ok(fs.readFileSync(file('downloads', 'a.bin')).equals(A));
    assert.equal(manifest['a.bin'].sha256, sha(A));
    assert.ok(logs.includes('已有但摘要不符，重下：a.bin'), logs.join('\n'));
  });
});

test('下来的内容摘要不符：改名 .BAD，记入失败，不进清单，后面的条目照常处理', async () => {
  await withEnv(
    async ({ run, file, logs }) => {
      const { manifest, failed } = await run();
      assert.deepEqual(failed, [{ name: 'b.bin', expected: sha(B), actual: sha(Buffer.from('evil')) }]);
      assert.equal(fs.existsSync(file('downloads', 'b.bin')), false);
      assert.equal(fs.readFileSync(file('downloads', 'b.bin.BAD'), 'utf8'), 'evil');
      assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(manifest), ['a.bin', 'c.bin']);
      assert.deepEqual(Object.keys(readJson(file('fetch-manifest.json'))), ['a.bin', 'c.bin']);
      assert.ok(logs.some((l) => l.startsWith('！摘要不符：b.bin') && l.includes(`期望 ${sha(B)}`) && l.includes(`实际 ${sha(Buffer.from('evil'))}`)), logs.join('\n'));
    },
    { routes: { '/b.bin': Buffer.from('evil') } },
  );
});

      fs.writeFileSync(file('downloads', 'a.bin'), 'damaged');
      const { failed } = await run();
      assert.deepEqual(failed, [{ name: 'a.bin', expected: sha(A), actual: sha(Buffer.from('also wrong')) }]);
      assert.equal(requests.filter((r) => r === '/a.bin').length, 1);
      assert.equal(fs.readFileSync(file('downloads', 'a.bin.BAD'), 'utf8'), 'also wrong');
      assert.equal(fs.existsSync(file('downloads', 'a.bin')), false);
    },
    { routes: { '/a.bin': Buffer.from('also wrong') } },
  );
});

      await assert.rejects(run(), (e) => e.message.includes('checksum.txt（t1）的摘要') && e.message.includes('不一致') && e.message.includes(sha(Buffer.from('tampered sums'))) && e.message.includes(sha(SUMS)));
```

- [ ] **步骤 11：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/fetch-run.mjs`**

```js
// 下载并核对一批条目（只下载、只算摘要，不运行下载来的任何东西）。条目表与摘要规则见 fetch-plan.mjs。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { download, sha256File } from './download.mjs';
import { expectedDigest, parseChecksums } from './fetch-plan.mjs';
import { readJson, writeJsonAtomic } from './home.mjs';

/**
 * 在 home 下建 downloads、meta，先取各分类的 checksum.txt（必须与写死的 GitHub API 摘要一致才信），
 * 再逐个条目：已有且摘要相符就不动；已有但不符就删掉重下；下完不符就改名 .BAD 并记入 failed，不拿来用。
 * 每个核对过的条目记入 home/fetch-manifest.json（别的条目的记录原样保留）。
 * 返回 { manifest, failed: [{ name, expected, actual }] }；摘要不符不抛错，由调用方决定怎么退出。
 */
export async function fetchAll({ home, items, checksumFiles, checksumUrlFor, say = () => {}, downloadOpts = {} }) {
  const downloads = path.join(home, 'downloads');
  const meta = path.join(home, 'meta');
  const opts = { ...downloadOpts, onLog: say };

  const checks = new Map();
  for (const c of checksumFiles) {
    const dest = path.join(meta, `checksum-${c.tag}.txt`);
    if (!(fs.existsSync(dest) && (await sha256File(dest)) === c.sha256)) {
      await download(checksumUrlFor(c.tag), dest, opts); // download 会建好目录，并在下完后替换掉旧文件
      const got = await sha256File(dest);
      if (got !== c.sha256) {
        fs.renameSync(dest, `${dest}.BAD`);
        throw new Error(`checksum.txt（${c.tag}）的摘要 ${got} 与写死的（GitHub API 给的）${c.sha256} 不一致：上游可能更新了这个文件，核对清楚后再更新 CHECKSUM_FILES`);
      }
    }
    checks.set(c.tag, parseChecksums(fs.readFileSync(dest, 'utf8')));
    say(`checksum.txt（${c.tag}）摘要与 API 一致，共 ${checks.get(c.tag).size} 条`);
  }

  const manifestPath = path.join(home, 'fetch-manifest.json');
  const manifest = fs.existsSync(manifestPath) ? readJson(manifestPath) : {};
  const failed = [];
  for (const item of items) {
    const dest = path.join(downloads, item.name);
    const { expected, source, official } = expectedDigest(item, checks);
    let sha;
    if (fs.existsSync(dest)) {
      sha = await sha256File(dest);
      if (expected === undefined || sha === expected) {
        say(`已有：${item.name}  ${sha.slice(0, 12)}…`);
      } else {
        say(`已有但摘要不符，重下：${item.name}`);
        sha = undefined;
      }
    }
    if (sha === undefined) {
      say(`开始：${item.name}`);
      const t0 = Date.now();
      await download(item.url, dest, opts);
      sha = await sha256File(dest);
      if (expected !== undefined && sha !== expected) {
        fs.renameSync(dest, `${dest}.BAD`);
        say(`！摘要不符：${item.name}  期望 ${expected}  实际 ${sha}（已改名为 .BAD，不会使用）`);
        failed.push({ name: item.name, expected, actual: sha });
        continue;
      }
      say(`完成：${item.name}  ${(fs.statSync(dest).size / 1e6).toFixed(1)} MB  ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)} s  sha256=${sha}  （${source}${expected === undefined ? '' : '：一致'}）`);
    }
    manifest[item.name] = { url: item.url, bytes: fs.statSync(dest).size, sha256: sha, source, official, verified: expected !== undefined };
    writeJsonAtomic(manifestPath, manifest);
  }
  return { manifest, failed };
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 13：创建 `scripts/s4/fetch.mjs`**

```js
// S4 下载：把官方 sherpa-onnx 程序、候选模型和 AliMeeting 评测集下载到 <S4 目录>/downloads，逐个核对 SHA-256；只下载，不运行任何东西。
// 用法：node scripts/s4/fetch.mjs --home <S4 目录> [--groups tools,small,asr,big,data]（不写 --groups 就是全部；meta 只取各分类的 checksum.txt）
// 已有且摘要相符的文件不重下；摘要不符的文件改名 .BAD、不会被使用，并以非 0 退出码结束。
// 下载清单写在 <S4 目录>/fetch-manifest.json，日志同时追加到 <S4 目录>/fetch.log。
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { log, parseArgs } from './lib/cli.mjs';
import { CHECKSUM_FILES, checksumUrl, GROUPS, selectItems } from './lib/fetch-plan.mjs';
import { fetchAll } from './lib/fetch-run.mjs';
import { homeFrom } from './lib/home.mjs';

const { flags } = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
const home = homeFrom(flags.home);
fs.mkdirSync(home, { recursive: true });
const say = (message) => {
  const now = new Date();
  log(message, now);
  fs.appendFileSync(path.join(home, 'fetch.log'), `${now.toLocaleString('sv-SE')}  ${message}\n`);
};

try {
  const groups = flags.groups === undefined ? GROUPS : String(flags.groups).split(',');
  const items = selectItems(groups);
  say(`开始：S4 目录 ${home}，分组 ${groups.join(',')}（${items.length} 个文件）`);
  const { failed } = await fetchAll({ home, items, checksumFiles: CHECKSUM_FILES, checksumUrlFor: checksumUrl, say });
  if (failed.length > 0) {
    say(`！${failed.length} 个文件摘要不符，已改名为 .BAD、不会被使用：${failed.map((f) => f.name).join('、')}`);
    process.exitCode = 1;
  } else {
    say('本批完成');
  }
} catch (e) {
  say(`！失败：${e.message}`);
  process.exitCode = 1;
}
```

## 任务 20：静态解压与安全检查

**目标：** 把下载好的压缩包静态解压（不运行里面的任何东西）：先确认它就是 `fetch` 核对过摘要的那一份，再列出全部条目做安全检查（绝对路径、盘符或冒号、`..`、链接、特殊文件一律拒绝），通过了才解压。用 Windows 自带的 `System32\tar.exe`（bsdtar），不经 PATH。`archive` 是 tar 输出的解析与安全检查（纯函数），`extract` 是调 tar 的部分（tar 命令可注入，所以能用假的测），`tar-fixture` 是测试里手写最小 tar 的小工具（能造出正常工具不肯造的 `..`、绝对路径、链接条目）。

**文件：**

- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/tar-fixture.mjs` — 手写最小的 tar（只给测试用）
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/archive.test.mjs` — `archive.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/archive.mjs` — tar 输出解析与解压前的安全检查
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/extract.test.mjs` — `extract.mjs` 的测试
- 创建：`scripts/s4/lib/extract.mjs` — 把核对过摘要的压缩包静态解压
- 创建：`scripts/s4/extract.mjs` — 命令行：静态解压已核对的压缩包

**接口：**

- 依赖：`download.mjs`、`home.mjs`
- 提供：`outputLines(text)`；`unsafeEntries(names, verboseLines)`；`tarPath(env = process.env)`；`runTar(tar, name, args)`；`extractAll({ home, items, manifest, tar = tarPath(), exec = runTar, say = () => {} })`
- 测试：`archive.test.mjs` 9 个；`extract.test.mjs` 20 个

- [ ] **步骤 1：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/tar-fixture.mjs`**

```js
// 仅供测试：手写最小的 tar（可以造出 .. 、绝对路径、盘符、符号链接、硬链接这类正常工具不肯造的条目），再 gzip 压缩。
import zlib from 'node:zlib';

function header({ name, size, type, link }) {
  const h = Buffer.alloc(512);
  h.write(name, 0, 100, 'utf8');
  h.write('0000644\0', 100);
  h.write('0000000\0', 108);
  h.write('0000000\0', 116);
  h.write(`${size.toString(8).padStart(11, '0')}\0`, 124);
  h.write('00000000000\0', 136);
  h.write('        ', 148); // 校验和先按 8 个空格算
  h.write(type, 156);
  h.write(link, 157, 100, 'utf8');
  h.write('ustar\0', 257);
  h.write('00', 263);
  let sum = 0;
  for (const b of h) sum += b;
  h.write(`${sum.toString(8).padStart(6, '0')}\0 `, 148);
  return h;
}

/**
 * entries：[{ name, data?, type?, link? }]；type 默认 '0'（文件），'5' 目录，'2' 符号链接，'1' 硬链接。
 * 返回 .tar.gz 的内容。
 */
export function tarGz(entries) {
  const parts = [];
  for (const e of entries) {
    const data = Buffer.from(e.data ?? '');
    parts.push(header({ name: e.name, size: data.length, type: e.type ?? '0', link: e.link ?? '' }));
    if (data.length) parts.push(data, Buffer.alloc((512 - (data.length % 512)) % 512));
  }
  parts.push(Buffer.alloc(1024));
  return zlib.gzipSync(Buffer.concat(parts));
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 2：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/archive.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `archive.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import test from 'node:test';
import { outputLines, unsafeEntries } from './archive.mjs';

test('tar 输出按行切：CRLF 和 LF 都行，去掉末尾的空行，中间的空行保留，空输出没有行', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(outputLines('a/\r\na/b\r\n'), ['a/', 'a/b']);
  assert.deepEqual(outputLines('a\nb\n'), ['a', 'b']);
  assert.deepEqual(outputLines('a\nb'), ['a', 'b']);
  assert.deepEqual(outputLines('a\n\nb\n'), ['a', '', 'b']);
  assert.deepEqual(outputLines('a\n\n'), ['a', '']);
  assert.deepEqual(outputLines(''), []);
  assert.deepEqual(outputLines('\r\n'), ['']);
});

const file = (name) => `-rw-r--r--  0 0      0      1234 Oct  7  2025 ${name}`;
const dir = (name) => `drwxr-xr-x  0 0      0          0 Oct  7  2025 ${name}`;
const check = (names) => unsafeEntries(names, names.map(file));

test('正常的压缩包：目录、文件、名字里带点的都没问题', () => {
  const names = ['model/', 'model/a.onnx', 'model/test_wavs/0.wav', 'a..b', '..a/x', 'x/b..', 'x/..b/y', 'x/.../y', 'x/.hidden'];
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(names, ['model/', ...names.slice(1)].map((n, i) => (i === 0 ? dir(n) : file(n)))), []);
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries([], []), []);
});

test('绝对路径（含 UNC）要拦住', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(check(['/etc/passwd']), ['/etc/passwd：绝对路径']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['\\\\server\\share\\x']), ['\\\\server\\share\\x：绝对路径']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['\\x']), ['\\x：绝对路径']);
});

test('盘符与带冒号的文件名要拦住（冒号后可能是备用数据流）', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(check(['C:\\x']), ['C:\\x：文件名里有冒号（盘符或备用数据流）']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['c:x']), ['c:x：文件名里有冒号（盘符或备用数据流）']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['a/b.txt:stream']), ['a/b.txt:stream：文件名里有冒号（盘符或备用数据流）']);
});

test('路径里的 .. 段要拦住，正反斜杠都算；名字里只是带两个点的不算', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(check(['../x']), ['../x：路径里有 ..']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['a/../b']), ['a/../b：路径里有 ..']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['a\\..\\b']), ['a\\..\\b：路径里有 ..']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['a/..']), ['a/..：路径里有 ..']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['a//../b']), ['a//../b：路径里有 ..']);
  assert.deepEqual(check(['..']), ['..：路径里有 ..']);
});

test('符号链接和硬链接要拦住，普通文件名里出现 l、h 开头不算', () => {
  const links = ['lrwxrwxrwx  0 0      0          0 Oct  7  2025 evil -> ../../x', 'hrw-r--r--  0 0      0          0 Oct  7  2025 hard link to a'];
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(['evil', 'hard'], links), [`${links[0]}：链接`, `${links[1]}：链接`]);
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(['link', 'hello'], [file('link'), file('hello')]), []);
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(['d'], [dir('lib')]), []);
});

test('管道、设备、套接字这类特殊文件也要拦住；不认识的类型字符和空行同样不放行（只放行普通文件和目录）', () => {
  for (const t of ['p', 'c', 'b', 's', '?', ' ']) {
    const line = `${t}rw-r--r--  0 0      0          0 Oct  7  2025 x`;
    assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(['x'], [line]), [`${line}：不是普通文件或目录`], `类型字符 ${JSON.stringify(t)}`);
  }
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(['x'], ['']), ['：不是普通文件或目录']);
});

test('两份清单行数对不上就不能证明检查到了每一条，要报出来', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(['a', 'b'], [file('a')]), ['条目名清单有 2 行，详细清单有 1 行，对不上，没法逐条检查']);
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(['a'], [file('a'), file('b')]), ['条目名清单有 1 行，详细清单有 2 行，对不上，没法逐条检查']);
});

test('多个问题按出现顺序全部列出：先名字后链接', () => {
  const names = ['ok', '../a', '/b', 'c'];
  const verbose = [file('ok'), file('../a'), file('/b'), 'lrwxrwxrwx 0 0 0 0 Oct 7 2025 c -> d'];
  assert.deepEqual(unsafeEntries(names, verbose), ['../a：路径里有 ..', '/b：绝对路径', 'lrwxrwxrwx 0 0 0 0 Oct 7 2025 c -> d：链接']);
});
```

- [ ] **步骤 4：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/archive.mjs`**

```js
/** tar 的输出按行切开（Windows 下换行是 CRLF），去掉末尾那个空行 */
export function outputLines(text) {
  const lines = text.split(/\r?\n/);
  if (lines.at(-1) === '') lines.pop();
  return lines;
}

// 解压前的安全检查（纯函数）：压缩包里有没有会写到目标目录之外的条目，或者除普通文件、目录之外的东西。
// names 是 `tar -tf` 的每行条目名；verboseLines 是 `tar -tvf` 的每行（第一个字符是类型：- 文件，d 目录，l 符号链接，h 硬链接，p 管道，c / b 设备，s 套接字）。
// 返回有问题的条目说明；空数组表示可以解压。
export function unsafeEntries(names, verboseLines) {
  const bad = [];
  if (names.length !== verboseLines.length) {
    bad.push(`条目名清单有 ${names.length} 行，详细清单有 ${verboseLines.length} 行，对不上，没法逐条检查`);
  }
  for (const name of names) {
    if (/^[\\/]/.test(name)) bad.push(`${name}：绝对路径`);
    // 盘符，也顺带挡住 Windows 的备用数据流（文件名里带冒号）
    else if (name.includes(':')) bad.push(`${name}：文件名里有冒号（盘符或备用数据流）`);
    else if (name.split(/[\\/]+/).includes('..')) bad.push(`${name}：路径里有 ..`);
  }
  for (const line of verboseLines) {
    if (/^[lh]/.test(line)) bad.push(`${line}：链接`);
    else if (!/^[-d]/.test(line)) bad.push(`${line}：不是普通文件或目录`);
  }
  return bad;
}
```

- [ ] **步骤 6：创建 `scripts/s4/lib/extract.test.mjs`**

先只写测试。这时 `extract.mjs` 还不存在，测试一导入它就会报错。

```js
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import test from 'node:test';
import { extractAll, runTar, tarPath } from './extract.mjs';
import { readJson } from './home.mjs';
import { tarGz } from './tar-fixture.mjs';

const sha = (b) => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
const GOOD = [{ name: 'model/', type: '5' }, { name: 'model/a.onnx', data: 'AAA' }, { name: 'model/sub/b.txt', data: 'B'.repeat(700) }];

async function withHome(fn) {
  const root = fs.mkdtempSync(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'record-extract-'));
  const home = path.join(root, 'home');
  fs.mkdirSync(path.join(home, 'downloads'), { recursive: true });
  const manifest = {};
  const items = [];
  const logs = [];
  const put = (name, entries, { extractTo = 'models', verified = true, raw } = {}) => {
    const bytes = raw ?? tarGz(entries);
    fs.writeFileSync(path.join(home, 'downloads', name), bytes);
    manifest[name] = { sha256: sha(bytes), verified };
    if (!items.some((i) => i.name === name)) items.push({ name, extractTo });
    return bytes;
  };
  const run = (over = {}) => extractAll({ home, items, manifest, say: (s) => logs.push(s), ...over });
  try {
    await fn({ root, home, put, run, items, manifest, logs, at: (...p) => path.join(home, ...p) });
  } finally {
    fs.rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
}

test('正常的压缩包：解到 <home>/<extractTo>，记下“已解压”的标记和条目数，日志写明去向', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at, logs, manifest }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    assert.deepEqual(await run(), [{ name: 'm.tar.gz', status: 'extracted', entries: 3, unchecked: 0 }]);
    assert.equal(fs.readFileSync(at('models', 'model', 'a.onnx'), 'utf8'), 'AAA');
    assert.equal(fs.readFileSync(at('models', 'model', 'sub', 'b.txt'), 'utf8'), 'B'.repeat(700));
    const marker = readJson(at('extracted', 'm.tar.gz.json'));
    assert.equal(marker.sha256, manifest['m.tar.gz'].sha256);
    assert.equal(marker.entries, 3);
    assert.equal(marker.unchecked, 0);
    assert.ok(Math.abs(Date.now() - Date.parse(marker.extractedAt)) < 60_000, marker.extractedAt);
    assert.equal(logs.length, 1);
    assert.match(logs[0], /^解压：m\.tar\.gz → models（3 个条目，\d+ s）$/);
  });
});

test('解压目的地跟着条目走（tools / models / data）', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at }) => {
    put('t.tar.gz', GOOD, { extractTo: 'tools' });
    await run();
    assert.ok(fs.existsSync(at('tools', 'model', 'a.onnx')));
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('models')), false);
  });
});

test('已经解压过（标记里的摘要与现在一致）：不再解压，也不动目录里现有的东西', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at, logs }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    await run();
    fs.rmSync(at('models', 'model'), { recursive: true });
    logs.length = 0;
    assert.deepEqual(await run(), [{ name: 'm.tar.gz', status: 'already' }]);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('models', 'model')), false, '没有重新解压');
    assert.deepEqual(logs, ['已解压：m.tar.gz']);
  });
});

test('压缩包换了（摘要变了，清单也更新了）：重新解压，覆盖旧内容，标记更新', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at, manifest }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    await run();
    const oldSha = manifest['m.tar.gz'].sha256;
    put('m.tar.gz', [{ name: 'model/a.onnx', data: 'NEW' }]);
    assert.deepEqual(await run(), [{ name: 'm.tar.gz', status: 'extracted', entries: 1, unchecked: 0 }]);
    assert.equal(fs.readFileSync(at('models', 'model', 'a.onnx'), 'utf8'), 'NEW');
    assert.notEqual(readJson(at('extracted', 'm.tar.gz.json')).sha256, oldSha);
  });
});

test('没有 extractTo 的条目（单个模型文件）直接跳过，清单里没有它也不要紧', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ run, items, at }) => {
    items.push({ name: 'silero_vad.onnx' });
    assert.deepEqual(await run(), []);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('models')), false);
  });
});

test('下载清单里没有这个文件：先跑 fetch，什么都不解', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ run, items, at }) => {
    items.push({ name: 'x.tar.gz', extractTo: 'models' });
    await assert.rejects(run(), /x\.tar\.gz：下载清单里没有它，先跑 fetch/);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('models')), false);
  });
});

test('清单里记着没核对过摘要：不解压', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at }) => {
    put('u.tar.gz', GOOD, { verified: false });
    await assert.rejects(run(), /u\.tar\.gz：下载时没有核对过摘要，不解压/);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('models')), false);
  });
});

test('压缩包在核对之后被改过（摘要与清单不一致）：不解压，说明两个摘要', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at, manifest }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    const changed = tarGz([{ name: 'model/a.onnx', data: 'tampered' }]);
    fs.writeFileSync(at('downloads', 'm.tar.gz'), changed);
    await assert.rejects(run(), (e) => e.message.includes('m.tar.gz：现在的摘要') && e.message.includes(sha(changed)) && e.message.includes(manifest['m.tar.gz'].sha256) && e.message.includes('下载之后被改过'));
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('models')), false);
  });
});

test('有不安全的条目就整个不解压（..、绝对路径、盘符、符号链接、硬链接），什么都不写', async () => {
  const cases = {
    dotdot: [[{ name: 'model/', type: '5' }, { name: '../../evil.txt', data: 'x' }], '../../evil.txt：路径里有 ..'],
    abs: [[{ name: '/abs-evil.txt', data: 'x' }], '/abs-evil.txt：绝对路径'],
    drive: [[{ name: 'C:/drive-evil.txt', data: 'x' }], 'C:/drive-evil.txt：文件名里有冒号'],
    symlink: [[{ name: 'model/', type: '5' }, { name: 'model/link', type: '2', link: '../../x' }], 'model/link -> ../../x：链接'],
    hardlink: [[{ name: 'model/a', data: 'q' }, { name: 'model/hard', type: '1', link: 'model/a' }], 'model/hard link to model/a：链接'],
    fifo: [[{ name: 'model/a', data: 'q' }, { name: 'model/pipe', type: '6' }], 'model/pipe：不是普通文件或目录'],
    device: [[{ name: 'model/dev', type: '3' }], 'model/dev：不是普通文件或目录'],
  };
  for (const [kind, [entries, expected]] of Object.entries(cases)) {
    await withHome(async ({ root, put, run, at, logs }) => {
      put(`${kind}.tar.gz`, entries);
      await assert.rejects(run(), (e) => e.message.startsWith(`${kind}.tar.gz：压缩包里有不安全的条目，不解压：`) && e.message.includes(expected), kind);
      assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('models')), false, `${kind}：目标目录都不该建`);
      assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('extracted')), false, `${kind}：不该留下已解压标记`);
      assert.equal(fs.existsSync(path.join(root, 'evil.txt')), false);
      assert.deepEqual(logs, []);
    });
  }
});

test('不安全的条目太多时只列前 5 条并给出总数', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run }) => {
    put('many.tar.gz', Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => ({ name: `../x${i}.txt`, data: 'x' })));
    await assert.rejects(run(), (e) => (e.message.match(/路径里有 \.\./g) ?? []).length === 5 && e.message.includes('…共 7 条') && e.message.includes('../x0.txt') && !e.message.includes('../x5.txt'));
  });
});

test('不安全的条目 5 条时不写“共多少条”，6 条起写', async () => {
  for (const [count, total] of [[5, null], [6, 6]]) {
    await withHome(async ({ put, run }) => {
      put('n.tar.gz', Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) => ({ name: `../x${i}.txt`, data: 'x' })));
      await assert.rejects(run(), (e) => (e.message.match(/路径里有 \.\./g) ?? []).length === 5 && (total === null ? !e.message.includes('共') : e.message.endsWith(`\n  …共 ${total} 条`)), `${count} 条`);
    });
  }
});

test('压缩包本身是坏的（摘要与清单一致但 tar 读不了）：报错并点名文件，不留标记', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at }) => {
    put('bad.tar.gz', [], { raw: Buffer.from('this is not an archive at all'.repeat(40)) });
    await assert.rejects(run(), (e) => /^bad\.tar\.gz：tar -tf 失败（退出码 \d+）：/.test(e.message) && e.message.includes('Unrecognized archive format') && e.message === e.message.trimEnd());
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('extracted')), false);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('models')), false);
  });
});

test('tar 运行不起来（路径不对）：报错，说明是哪个文件、哪个程序', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    await assert.rejects(run({ tar: 'Z:\\no\\such\\tar.exe' }), (e) => e.message.startsWith('m.tar.gz：没法运行 Z:\\no\\such\\tar.exe：'));
  });
});

test('解压本身失败（tar 报错）：把错误抛出去，不留“已解压”的标记（下次还会重来）', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at, logs }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    const exec = (tar, name, args) => {
      if (args[0] === '-xf') throw new Error(`${name}：tar -xf 失败（退出码 1）：disk full`);
      return runTar(tar, name, args);
    };
    await assert.rejects(run({ exec }), /^Error: m\.tar\.gz：tar -xf 失败（退出码 1）：disk full$/);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('extracted')), false);
    assert.deepEqual(logs, []);
  });
});

test('tar 退出码为 0 但文件根本没出来：报错，给出缺少的个数和例子，不留标记', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at, logs }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    const exec = (tar, name, args) => (args[0] === '-xf' ? '' : runTar(tar, name, args));
    await assert.rejects(run({ exec }), /^Error: m\.tar\.gz：tar 没有报错，但有 2 个文件不在磁盘上，例如 model\/a\.onnx$/);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('extracted')), false);
    assert.deepEqual(logs, []);
  });
});

test('只缺一个文件也要报（目录条目不算文件）', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    const exec = (tar, name, args) => {
      const out = runTar(tar, name, args);
      if (args[0] === '-xf') fs.rmSync(at('models', 'model', 'sub', 'b.txt'));
      return out;
    };
    await assert.rejects(run({ exec }), /有 1 个文件不在磁盘上，例如 model\/sub\/b\.txt$/);
    assert.equal(fs.existsSync(at('extracted')), false);
  });
});

test('文件名里的空格、~ 等可见字符都能核对（不会被当成非 ASCII 跳过）', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', [{ name: 'model/a ~b!.txt', data: 'x' }, { name: 'model/ok.txt', data: 'y' }]);
    assert.deepEqual(await run(), [{ name: 'm.tar.gz', status: 'extracted', entries: 2, unchecked: 0 }]);
  });
});

test('文件名含非 ASCII 字符的文件没法核对（tar 列出来是乱码）：只核对 ASCII 的，没核对的个数记在标记和日志里', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at, logs }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    const exec = (tar, name, args) => {
      if (args[0] === '-tf') return 'model/\r\nmodel/a.onnx\r\nmodel/\u4f1a\ufffd\\256.txt\r\nmodel/\u4f1a2.txt\r\n';
      if (args[0] === '-tvf') return ['drwxr-xr-x  0 0 0 0 x', '-rw-r--r-- 0 0 0 3 x', '-rw-r--r-- 0 0 0 1 x', '-rw-r--r-- 0 0 0 1 x'].join('\r\n');
      fs.mkdirSync(path.join(args[3], 'model'), { recursive: true });
      fs.writeFileSync(path.join(args[3], 'model', 'a.onnx'), 'AAA');
      return '';
    };
    assert.deepEqual(await run({ exec }), [{ name: 'm.tar.gz', status: 'extracted', entries: 4, unchecked: 2 }]);
    assert.equal(readJson(at('extracted', 'm.tar.gz.json')).unchecked, 2);
    assert.equal(logs.length, 1);
    assert.match(logs[0], /（4 个条目，\d+ s；2 个文件名含非 ASCII 字符，没法逐个核对）$/);
  });
});

test('传给 tar 的参数：先 -tf、-tvf 列清单，再 -xf <压缩包> -C <目的地>，用的是传入的 tar', async () => {
  await withHome(async ({ put, run, at }) => {
    put('m.tar.gz', GOOD);
    const calls = [];
    const exec = (tar, name, args) => {
      calls.push([tar, name, ...args]);
      return runTar(tar, name, args);
    };
    await run({ exec, tar: tarPath() });
    const archive = at('downloads', 'm.tar.gz');
    assert.deepEqual(calls, [
      [tarPath(), 'm.tar.gz', '-tf', archive],
      [tarPath(), 'm.tar.gz', '-tvf', archive],
      [tarPath(), 'm.tar.gz', '-xf', archive, '-C', at('models')],
    ]);
  });
});

test('tar 的位置：系统目录下的 tar.exe，不走 PATH；没有 SystemRoot 就报错', () => {
  assert.equal(tarPath({ SystemRoot: 'C:\\Windows' }), path.join('C:\\Windows', 'System32', 'tar.exe'));
  assert.throws(() => tarPath({}), /SystemRoot 没有设置/);
  assert.ok(fs.existsSync(tarPath()), '这台机器上系统自带的 tar 应该存在');
});
```
